function allowedNavs(user=state.user){const cmAllowed=user?.role==='admin'||['operator','reviewer'].includes(user?.role)&&['business','finance','support'].includes(state.commissionPermission);return Object.keys(labels).filter(k=>k==='commission'?cmAllowed:user?.role==='admin'||user?.role==='operator'&&!['users','settings','moderation','commerce','privacy','aiGateway','memberSubscriptions','cardService','commission'].includes(k)||user?.role==='reviewer'&&['dashboard','posts','audits','creators','creatorReviews'].includes(k));}
function invalidateAuth(preserveTarget=false){const target=preserveTarget?(state.resumeNav||state.nav):null;++state.authEpoch;++state.seq;++authStatusRevision;authStatusPending=null;Object.assign(state,{user:null,commissionPermission:null,items:[],editing:null,settings:null,records:null,analytics:null,teacherOptions:[],categoryOptions:[],resumeNav:target});if(!preserveTarget)state.nav='dashboard';if(typeof caState!=='undefined')Object.assign(caState,{creators:[],teachers:[],works:[],selected:null,settings:null});}
function broadcastAuth(){try{localStorage.setItem('ew-identity-change-v1',Date.now()+'-'+Math.random());}catch{}}
function reconcileAuth(){invalidateAuth(true);broadcastAuth();authBootstrap();}
function requireLogin(){invalidateAuth(true);auth(false,'登录已失效，请重新登录后继续查看原页面。');}
function authConnectionError(message){el.innerHTML='<main class="auth"><h1>后台暂时无法连接</h1><p class="error" role="alert">'+esc(message)+'</p><button class="btn" data-action="auth-retry">重新连接</button><p><a href="/app">返回APP</a></p></main>';}
async function api(path,body,method){
 const epoch=state.authEpoch,verb=method||(body?'POST':'GET'),authWrite=verb!=='GET'&&path.startsWith('auth/'),controller=verb==='GET'?new AbortController():null,timer=controller?setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),12000):null;
 try{const r=await fetch('/api/'+path,{method:verb,credentials:'same-origin',headers:body?{'Content-Type':'application/json'}:undefined,body:body?JSON.stringify(body):undefined,signal:controller?.signal});let d;try{d=await r.json();}catch(error){if(error.name==='AbortError')throw error;throw Error('服务返回了无法读取的内容，请重试。');}if(!authCurrent(epoch))throw authChanged();if(!r.ok){const error=Object.assign(Error(d.error||'操作失败'),{status:r.status});if(r.status===401&&!path.startsWith('auth/')){requireLogin();error.handled=true;}throw error;}return d;
 }catch(error){if(error.handled)throw error;if(!authCurrent(epoch))throw authChanged();if(error.name==='AbortError')throw Object.assign(Error('读取超时，请重试。'),{code:'READ_TIMEOUT'});if(error instanceof TypeError)throw Error('网络连接失败，请检查连接后重试。');throw error;}finally{if(timer)clearTimeout(timer);if(authWrite&&!authCurrent(epoch))reconcileAuth();}
}
async function authBootstrap(){
 if(authStatusPending)return authStatusPending;const epoch=state.authEpoch,revision=++authStatusRevision,active=()=>authCurrent(epoch)&&revision===authStatusRevision;el.innerHTML='<main class="auth"><h1>正在连接管理后台…</h1><p role="status">正在确认当前账号。</p></main>';
 const pending=(async()=>{try{const d=await api('auth/status');if(!active())return false;if(!d||typeof d.setupRequired!=='boolean'||!Object.hasOwn(d,'user')||d.user!==null&&(!d.user||typeof d.user.id!=='string'||!d.user.id.trim()||typeof d.user.role!=='string'))throw Error('账号状态响应异常，请重新连接。');state.user=d.user;state.commissionPermission=d.commissionPermission||null;if(state.user&&['admin','operator','reviewer'].includes(state.user.role))shell();else auth(d.setupRequired,state.user?'当前账号没有后台管理权限。':'');return true;}catch(error){if(!active()||error.code==='AUTH_CHANGED')return false;if(error.status===401)requireLogin();else authConnectionError(error.message);return false;}finally{if(active())authStatusPending=null;}})();authStatusPending=pending;return pending;
}
function toast(message){const node=document.getElementById('toast');node.textContent=message;clearTimeout(toast.timer);toast.timer=setTimeout(()=>node.textContent='',4200);}
function showError(err){if(err.handled||err.code==='AUTH_CHANGED')return;const node=document.getElementById('top-error');if(node)node.textContent=err.message;else toast(err.message);}
function badge(status){return `<span class="badge ${esc(status)}">${esc(statuses[status]||status)}</span>`;}
function date(value){return new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-CN',{hour12:false});}
function auth(setup=false,message=''){el.innerHTML=`<main class="auth"><h1>${setup?'创建首位管理员':'登录管理后台'}</h1><p>${setup?'设置你自己的管理账号，后续可分配运营、审核和老师权限。':'东方智慧内容、社群与服务统一管理。'}</p><form id="auth-form" data-mode="${setup?'setup':'login'}"><label>账号<input name="username" autocomplete="username" pattern="[A-Za-z0-9_]{3,32}" placeholder="3–32位字母、数字或下划线" required></label><label>密码<input name="password" type="password" autocomplete="${setup?'new-password':'current-password'}" minlength="${setup?10:1}" maxlength="128" required></label>${setup?'<label>管理昵称<input name="name" maxlength="24" required></label>':''}<button class="btn">${setup?'创建并进入':'登录'}</button><p id="auth-error" class="error" role="alert">${esc(message)}</p></form><p><a href="/app">返回APP</a></p></main>`;}
function shell(){const allowed=allowedNavs(),requested=state.resumeNav||'dashboard',target=allowed.includes(requested)?requested:'dashboard',denied=target!==requested;state.resumeNav=null;el.innerHTML=`<div class="shell"><aside class="sidebar"><div class="brand">东方智慧</div><div class="brand-sub">OPERATIONS CONSOLE</div><nav aria-label="后台模块">${allowed.map(k=>`<button data-nav="${k}">${labels[k]}</button>`).join('')}</nav><a href="/creator" target="_blank" rel="noopener">打开创作者中心 ↗</a><a href="/app" target="_blank" rel="noopener">打开APP查看效果 ↗</a></aside><div class="workspace"><header class="toolbar"><b>统一管理后台</b><div class="actions"><span>${esc(state.user.name)} · ${roles[state.user.role]}</span><button class="btn secondary small" data-action="logout">退出</button></div></header><p id="top-error" role="alert"></p><main id="main"></main></div></div>`;load(target);if(denied)document.getElementById('top-error').textContent='当前账号没有原页面权限，已返回运营概览。';}
function heading(name,action=''){return `<div class="heading"><h1>${name}</h1>${action}</div>`;}
function table(headers,rows){return rows.length?`<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr>${headers.map(v=>`<th>${v}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(row=>`<tr>${row.map(v=>`<td>${v}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="empty">暂无记录</div>';}
async function load(nav){if(!state.user||!document.getElementById('main'))return false;const seq=++state.seq;state.nav=nav;state.editing=null;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',String(b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false')));document.getElementById('top-error').textContent='';const main=document.getElementById('main');main.innerHTML='<p class="hint">正在读取…</p>';try{
 if(['creators','creatorReviews','moderation'].includes(nav)){await loadCreatorAdmin(nav,seq);}
 else if(nav==='privacy'){await loadPrivacyAdmin(seq);}
 else if(nav==='commerce'){await loadCommerceAdmin(seq);}
 else if(nav==='analytics'){await loadAnalyticsAdmin(seq);}
 else if(schemas[nav]){const d=await api('admin/content/'+nav);let teacherOptions,categoryOptions;if(nav==='courses'){teacherOptions=(await api('admin/content/teachers')).items;categoryOptions=(await api('admin/content/courseCategories')).items;}if(seq!==state.seq)return;if(teacherOptions)state.teacherOptions=teacherOptions;if(categoryOptions)state.categoryOptions=categoryOptions;state.items=d.items;renderContent();}
 else if(nav==='dashboard'){const d=await api('admin/dashboard');if(seq!==state.seq)return;main.innerHTML=heading('运营概览')+`<div class="stats">${[['users','账号'],['content','内容条目'],['pending','待审核'],['posts','社群发布'],['services','服务需求'],['records','签到与觉察记录'],['devices','设备授权']].map(([k,name])=>`<div class="stat"><strong>${d.counts[k]||0}</strong><span>${name}</span></div>`).join('')}</div><section class="panel"><h2>日常工作</h2><p class="hint">内容编辑、社群审核、角色权限、计时记录、AI及设备接口配置集中维护。内容发布后，APP重新打开即可读取。</p><div class="actions"><button class="btn" data-nav="posts">处理待审核内容</button>${state.user.role!=='reviewer'?'<button class="btn secondary" data-nav="courses">管理课程与素材</button><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="services">处理服务需求</button><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="records">查询签到与计时</button>':''}</div></section>`;}
 else if(nav==='posts'){const d=await api('admin/posts');if(seq!==state.seq)return;d.items=(d.items||[]).filter(p=>!p.creatorId);main.innerHTML=heading(labels.posts,`<button class="btn secondary" data-nav="posts">刷新</button>`)+`<p class="hint">此处处理普通社群发布。创作者作品请在“创作者内容审核”中处理，查看AI初审与人工复核轨迹。只有审核通过的内容公开展示。</p><div class="post-review">${d.items.length?d.items.map(p=>`<article><div class="actions">${badge(p.status)}<span class="meta">${esc(p.authorName)} · ${roles[p.authorRole]} · ${date(p.createdAt)}</span></div><h3>${esc(p.title)}</h3><p>${esc(p.body)}</p>${p.mediaUrl?`<video controls preload="metadata" src="${esc(p.mediaUrl)}"></video>`:''}${p.reason?`<p class="meta">上次审核说明：${esc(p.reason)}</p>`:''}<label class="meta">审核说明<input id="reason-${p.id}" maxlength="500" placeholder="驳回或下架时必填"></label><div class="actions"><button class="btn small" data-action="moderate" data-id="${p.id}" data-status="published" ${p.status==='draft'?'disabled':''}>审核通过</button><button class="btn secondary small" data-action="moderate" data-id="${p.id}" data-status="rejected" ${p.status==='draft'?'disabled':''}>驳回修改</button><button class="btn danger small" data-action="moderate" data-id="${p.id}" data-status="withdrawn" ${p.status==='draft'?'disabled':''}>下架</button></div></article>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">暂无内容待审核</div>'}</div>`;}
 else if(nav==='users'){const d=await api('admin/users');if(seq!==state.seq)return;main.innerHTML=heading('账号与权限')+'<p class="hint">老师账号拥有自主发布能力；审核员仅处理审核，运营维护内容，管理员管理权限与接口凭据。</p>'+table(['账号','昵称','角色','操作'],d.items.map(u=>[esc(u.username),esc(u.name),`<select id="role-${u.id}">${Object.entries(roles).map(([key,name])=>`<option value="${key}" ${u.role===key?'selected':''}>${name}</option>`).join('')}</select>`,`<button class="btn small" data-action="role" data-id="${u.id}">保存角色</button>`]));}
 else if(nav==='settings'){const d=await api('admin/settings');if(seq!==state.seq)return;state.settings=d;renderSettings();}
 else if(nav==='records'){const d=await api('admin/records');if(seq!==state.seq)return;state.records=d;main.innerHTML=heading('签到与计时',`<div class="actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-action="export-records">完整JSON导出</button><button class="btn secondary" data-action="export-records-csv">完整CSV导出</button></div>`)+`<p class="hint">全局计时由APP心跳自动记账。记录共 ${state.records.total??state.records.items.length} 条，列表显示最近 ${state.records.items.length} 条；完整导出包含全部记录元数据和计时汇总，不含自评正文及私密会话。</p><h2 style="margin:20px 0 12px">计时汇总</h2>`+table(['账号标识','使用会话','累计时长','最近活动'],state.records.timers.map(x=>[esc(x.name||x.username||x.owner)+'<br><small>'+esc(x.owner)+'</small>',x.sessions,Math.floor(x.totalMs/1000)+' 秒',date(x.lastSeen)]))+'<h2 style="margin:25px 0 12px">签到与觉察记录</h2>'+table(['账号标识','类型','日期'],state.records.items.map(x=>[esc(x.name||x.username||x.owner)+'<br><small>'+esc(x.owner)+'</small>',esc(x.kind),esc(x.day)]));}
 else if(nav==='services'){const d=await api('admin/service-requests');if(seq!==state.seq)return;main.innerHTML=heading('服务需求')+table(['学员／老师','需求与期望日期','状态','处理'],d.items.map(x=>[`${esc(x.user_name)} → ${esc(x.teacher_name)}`,`${esc(x.body)}<br><small>${esc(x.preferred_date)}</small>`,badge(x.status),`<div class="actions"><button class="btn small" data-action="service" data-id="${x.id}" data-status="accepted">受理</button><button class="btn secondary small" data-action="service" data-id="${x.id}" data-status="closed">结束</button></div>`]));}
 else if(nav==='revisions'){const d=await api('admin/revisions');if(seq!==state.seq)return;main.innerHTML=heading('内容版本')+'<p class="hint">每次修改已有内容均保存修改前的版本，恢复后APP读取该版本的内容及发布状态。</p>'+table(['模块','内容编号','保存时间','操作'],d.items.map(x=>[esc(labels[x.kind]||x.kind),esc(x.item_id),date(x.at),`<button class="btn secondary small" data-action="rollback" data-id="${x.id}">恢复此版本</button>`]));}
 else if(nav==='audits'){const d=await api('admin/audits');if(seq!==state.seq)return;main.innerHTML=heading('操作日志')+table(['时间','操作者','操作','对象／说明'],d.items.map(x=>[date(x.at),esc(x.name||x.actor),esc(x.action),esc(x.target)+(x.detail?'<br>'+esc(x.detail):'')]));}
 }catch(e){if(seq===state.seq&&main.isConnected&&!e.handled&&e.code!=='AUTH_CHANGED'){main.innerHTML=heading(labels[nav]||nav)+'<p class="error">'+esc(e.message)+'</p>'+(e.status===403?'':'<button class="btn secondary" data-action="read-retry">重试当前查询</button>')+(nav==='analytics'&&e.status===400?'<button class="btn secondary" data-an-reset>重置查询并重试</button>':'');}}finally{if(seq===state.seq&&main.isConnected)enhanceAdminLists();}}
function renderContent(){const kind=state.nav;document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading(labels[kind],'<button class="btn" data-action="new-content">'+(kind==='courseCategories'?'新增筛选项':'新增内容')+'</button>')+`<p class="hint">编辑后选择保存草稿或发布。排序值越小越靠前；归档后不再出现在APP的新内容列表。课程筛选项发布且启用后进入筛选条；删除分类会解除课程关联，课程本身保留。老师仅在真实资料、已认证且已推荐时进入社群推荐。四语文案在同一条内容中维护，翻译留空时使用内置译文；新内容没有译文时显示原文。</p><div class="editor-layout"><div>${table(kind==='courseCategories'?['名称','启用','状态','排序','操作']:['名称','状态','操作'],state.items.map(x=>[esc(x.zh||x.title||x.name),...(kind==='courseCategories'?[x.enabled===false?'停用':'启用']:[]),badge(x._status),...(kind==='courseCategories'?[esc(x._position??0)]:[]),`<div class="actions">${kind==='courses'&&x.creatorId?'<button class="btn secondary small" data-nav="creatorReviews">创作者审核</button>':`<button class="btn secondary small" data-edit="${esc(x.id)}">编辑</button>`}${kind==='courseCategories'?'<button class="btn danger small" data-action="delete-category" data-id="'+esc(x.id)+'">删除</button>':''}</div>`]))}</div><section class="panel" id="editor">${state.editing?editor(state.editing):'<h2>选择一项内容</h2><p class="hint">从左侧选择编辑，或新增内容。</p>'}</section></div>`;enhanceAdminLists();}
// Translation fields share the original content's revision and publication lifecycle.
const translatedFields={quotes:['zh','bannerLabel','description','buttonLabel'],maps:['name','heroTitle','heroText'],cards:['zh','lineZh','readingZh','actionZh'],music:['zh','descZh'],courses:['zh','summaryZh','goalsZh'],courseCategories:['name'],teachers:['name','specialty','bio'],devices:['description'],signin:['name','description'],membership:['zh','periodZh','copyZh'],benefits:['zh','subZh','copyZh','ctaZh']};
for(const [kind,fields] of Object.entries(translatedFields)){for(const source of fields){const original=schemas[kind].find(x=>x[0]===source);if(!original)continue;const prefix=source==='zh'?'':source.endsWith('Zh')?source.slice(0,-2):source;for(const [suffix,label] of [['En','英文'],['Hant','繁体中文'],['Bo','藏文']]){const key=prefix?prefix+suffix:suffix.toLowerCase();if(!schemas[kind].some(x=>x[0]===key))schemas[kind].push([key,label+' · '+original[1],original[2]==='lines'?'lines':original[2]==='textarea'?'textarea':'text']);}}}
schemas.courses.push(['sectionsEn','英文章节（每段标题换行正文；空行分隔章节）','localized-chapters'],['sectionsHant','繁体章节（与原文章节顺序一致）','localized-chapters'],['sectionsBo','藏文章节（与原文章节顺序一致）','localized-chapters']);
function editor(item){return `<h2 style="margin-bottom:17px">${item._new?'新增内容':'编辑内容'}</h2><form id="content-form" class="grid-form"><label class="wide">内容编号<input name="id" value="${esc(item.id)}" ${item._new?'':'readonly'} required pattern="[A-Za-z0-9_-]+"></label>${schemas[state.nav].map(([key,label,type])=>field(key,label,type,item[key])).join('')}<label>排序值<input name="position" type="number" min="-10000" max="10000" value="${item._position||0}"></label><label>当前状态<input value="${esc(statuses[item._status]||'新内容')}" readonly></label><div class="actions wide"><button class="btn" value="published">发布</button><button class="btn secondary" value="draft">保存草稿</button><button class="btn danger" value="archived">归档</button></div><p id="content-error" class="error wide" role="alert"></p></form>`;}
function field(key,label,type,value){const wide=['textarea','audio','media','list','lines','chapters','localized-chapters','image','sources','categories'].includes(type);let input;
 if(type==='teacher'){const options=state.teacherOptions||[];input='<select name="'+key+'"><option value="">平台课程（未关联老师）</option>'+options.map(x=>'<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'"'+(value===x.id?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(x.name||x.id)+(x._status==='published'?'':' · 未发布')+'</option>').join('')+(value&&!options.some(x=>x.id===value)?'<option value="'+esc(value)+'" selected>'+esc(value)+' · 资料不可用</option>':'')+'</select><span class="field-note">关联老师用于课程作者信息；社群老师推荐由认证和推荐状态决定。</span>';}
 else if(type==='categories'){const options=state.categoryOptions||[],selected=Array.isArray(value)?value:[];input='<select name="'+key+'" multiple size="'+Math.max(2,Math.min(6,options.length))+'">'+options.map(x=>'<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'"'+(selected.includes(x.id)?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(x.name||x.id)+(x._status!=='published'?' · 未发布':x.enabled===false?' · 停用':'')+'</option>').join('')+'</select><span class="field-note">可多选（电脑按住Ctrl/Command选择）。分类名称和排序在“课程筛选项”管理；不选择的课程仍在全部中显示。</span>';}
  else if(type==='boolean'||type==='certification'){const checked=value===true||(type==='boolean'&&key==='enabled'&&value===undefined),locked=type==='certification'&&state.user.role!=='admin';input='<input style="width:auto;justify-self:start" name="'+key+'" type="checkbox"'+(checked?' checked':'')+(locked?' disabled':'')+'><span class="field-note">'+(key==='verified'?'确认平台已核验真实老师资料后再勾选。':key==='recommended'?'已认证并发布的真实老师才可进入推荐。':'停用后前台隐藏此项，课程仍保留。')+'</span>';}
  else if(type==='textarea')input=`<textarea name="${key}" rows="3" maxlength="8000">${esc(value||'')}</textarea>`;
 else if(type==='list')input=`<input name="${key}" value="${esc((value||['life','body','mind']).join(','))}"><span class="field-note">life=生命地图，body=身体地图，mind=心智地图；用英文逗号分隔。</span>`;
 else if(type==='lines')input=`<textarea name="${key}" rows="3">${esc((value||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea>`;
 else if(type==='sources')input=`<textarea name="${key}" rows="4" placeholder="参考资料名称|https://来源地址">${esc((value||[]).filter(x=>Array.isArray(x)).map(x=>x.join('|')).join('\n'))}</textarea><span class="field-note">仅填写真实资料；参考链接不代表课程授权。</span>`;
 else if(type==='localized-chapters')input='<textarea name="'+key+'" rows="6">'+esc((value||[]).map(v=>v[0]+'\n'+v[1]).join('\n\n'))+'</textarea><span class="field-note">留空时使用内置译文；新增内容请补齐翻译。</span>';
 else if(type==='chapters')input=`<textarea name="${key}" rows="7">${esc((value||[]).map(v=>v[0]+'\n'+v[2]).join('\n\n'))}</textarea><span class="field-note">各章节之间留一个空行。</span>`;
 else if(type.startsWith('select:'))input=`<select name="${key}">${type.slice(7).split(',').map(v=>{const [id,name]=v.split('=');return `<option value="${id}" ${value===id?'selected':''}>${name}</option>`;}).join('')}</select>`;
 else if(type==='audio'||type==='media'||type==='image')input=`<input name="${key}" value="${esc(value||'')}" placeholder="上传素材或填写HTTPS地址"><input type="file" data-upload="${key}" accept="${type==='image'?'image/png,image/jpeg':type==='audio'?'audio/mpeg,audio/wav,audio/ogg':'video/mp4,video/webm,video/quicktime,audio/mpeg,audio/wav,audio/ogg'}"><span class="field-note">文件上限50MB，上传后再发布内容。</span>${value?mediaPreview(type,value):''}`;
 else input=`<input name="${key}" type="${type==='number'?'number':'text'}" ${type==='number'?'min="0"':''} value="${esc(value??'')}" maxlength="500">`;
 return `<label class="${wide?'wide':''}">${label}${input}</label>`;}
function renderSettings(){const s=state.settings,a=s.ai;document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('AI与接口配置')+`<section class="panel"><h2>AI问答服务</h2><p class="hint">请求通过本机服务端发送，凭据不下发给APP。接口按 class="wide">Responses API 地址<input name="endpoint" type="url" value="${esc(a.endpoint)}" required></label><label>模型名称<input name="model" value="${esc(a.model)}" maxlength="120" placeholder="填写有权限使用的模型"></label><label>实际供应商ID<input name="providerId" maxlength="80" value="${esc(a.providerId||'')}" placeholder="与隐私接收方ID一致"></label><label>实际处理地区（逗号分隔）<input name="processingRegions" value="${esc((a.processingRegions||[]).join(','))}"></label><label>隐私政策版本<input name="privacyPolicyVersion" maxlength="80" value="${esc(a.privacyPolicyVersion||'')}"></label><label>API密钥<input name="apiKey" type="password" autocomplete="off" placeholder="${a.hasApiKey?'已保存，留空则保持':'尚未配置'}"></label><label class="wide class="wide">参考知识<textarea name="knowledge" rows="5" maxlength="20000">${esc(a.knowledge)}</textarea></label><label>主强调色<input name="theme_cta" value="${esc(s.theme['--cta']||'#087f83')}" pattern="#[A-Fa-f0-9]{6}"></label><label>日间背景<input name="theme_bg" value="${esc(s.theme['--bg']||'#f3f7f7')}" pattern="#[A-Fa-f0-9]{6}"></label><div class="wide"><button class="btn">保存AI及主题配置</button><p id="settings-error" class="error" role="alert"></p></div></form></section><section class="panel"><h2>设备接入凭据</h2><p class="hint">先在设备供应商模块发布设备条目，再用相同编号配置服务端密钥。上传测量数据需明确的用户设备授权。</p><form id="integration-form" class="grid-form"><label>设备供应商编号<input name="provider" pattern="[A-Za-z0-9_-]+" required></label><label>接口密钥<input name="secret" type="password" minlength="24" maxlength="500" autocomplete="off" placeholder="至少24位，留空保持已有值"></label><label class="wide inline-check"><input name="clearSecret" type="checkbox">清除该供应商已保存密钥</label><label class="wide">供应商字段映射<textarea name="fieldMap" rows="3" placeholder='{"heart_rate":"heartRate"}'>{}</textarea></label><div class="wide"><button class="btn">保存设备配置</button><p id="integration-error" class="error" role="alert"></p></div></form><h3 style="margin:22px 0 12px">已配置供应商</h3>${table(['编号','接口凭据','字段映射','操作'],Object.entries(s.integrations).map(([key,v])=>[esc(key),v.hasSecret?'已设置':'未设置',esc(JSON.stringify(v.fieldMap||{})),`<button class="btn secondary small" data-action="edit-integration" data-provider="${esc(key)}">编辑配置</button>`]))}</section>`;}
function csvCell(value){let v=String(value??'');if(/^[\s]*[=+@-]/.test(v))v="'"+v;return '"'+v.replace(/"/g,'""')+'"';}
function downloadAdmin(name,body,type){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([body],{type})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;document.body.append(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
function mediaPreview(type,url){if(!/^(https:\/\/|\/api\/media\/)/.test(url))return '';return `<div class="asset-preview">${type==='image'?`<img src="${esc(url)}" alt="封面预览" class="preview-thumb">`:type==='audio'?`<audio controls preload="metadata" src="${esc(url)}"></audio>`:`<video controls preload="metadata" src="${esc(url)}"></video>`}</div>`;}
function enhanceAdminLists(){document.querySelectorAll('#main .table-wrap,#main .post-review').forEach((wrap,index)=>{if(wrap.dataset.enhanced)return;wrap.dataset.enhanced='true';const rows=[...wrap.querySelectorAll('tbody tr, :scope > article')];if(!rows.length)return;const texts=rows.map(r=>(r.textContent+' '+[...r.querySelectorAll('[data-edit],[data-id]')].map(e=>e.dataset.edit||e.dataset.id).join(' ')).toLocaleLowerCase()),statuses=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>r.querySelector('.badge')?.textContent).filter(Boolean))];const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='list-tools';bar.innerHTML=`<label class="list-search">查找当前列表<input type="search" data-list-search maxlength="100" placeholder="名称、编号或关键词" aria-label="查找当前列表 ${index+1}"></label>${statuses.length?`<label>状态<select data-list-status aria-label="筛选状态"><option value="">全部状态</option>${statuses.map(v=>`<option>${esc(v)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:''}<span class="list-count" role="status"></span>`;wrap.before(bar);const result=document.createElement('p');result.className='list-empty';result.hidden=true;result.textContent='没有匹配项，请调整关键词或状态。';wrap.after(result);function filter(){const query=bar.querySelector('input').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase(),status=bar.querySelector('select')?.value;let count=0;rows.forEach((row,i)=>{const visible=texts[i].includes(query)&&(!status||row.querySelector('.badge')?.textContent===status);row.hidden=!visible;if(visible)count++;});bar.querySelector('.list-count').textContent=`显示 ${count} / ${rows.length} 项`;result.hidden=count>0;}bar.addEventListener('input',filter);bar.addEventListener('change',filter);filter();});}
document.addEventListener('click',async e=>{const nav=e.target.closest('[data-nav]');if(nav){load(nav.dataset.nav);return;}const edit=e.target.closest('[data-edit]');if(edit){state.editing=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.items.find(x=>x.id===edit.dataset.edit)));renderContent();return;}const action=e.target.closest('[data-action]');if(!action)return;const a=action.dataset.action,actionEpoch=state.authEpoch,actionSeq=state.seq,actionActive=()=>authCurrent(actionEpoch)&&actionSeq===state.seq&&action.isConnected;try{
 if(a==='auth-retry'){await authBootstrap();return;}if(a==='read-retry'){await load(state.nav);return;}
 if(a==='edit-integration'){const f=document.getElementById('integration-form'),v=state.settings.integrations[action.dataset.provider];f.elements.provider.value=action.dataset.provider;f.elements.secret.value='';f.elements.clearSecret.checked=false;f.elements.fieldMap.value=JSON.stringify(v.fieldMap||{},null,2);f.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});f.elements.provider.focus();toast('已载入配置，密钥留空将保持现有值');}
 else if(a==='logout'){if(authBusy)return;authBusy=true;invalidateAuth(false);const epoch=state.authEpoch;el.innerHTML='<main class="auth"><h1>正在退出…</h1></main>';try{await api('auth/logout',{});if(!authCurrent(epoch))return;broadcastAuth();auth(false);}catch(error){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&error.code!=='AUTH_CHANGED')authConnectionError('退出状态尚未确认，请重新连接核对当前账号。');}finally{authBusy=false;}}
 else if(a==='new-content'){state.editing={id:state.nav+'_'+Date.now().toString(36),_new:true,_status:'draft',_position:state.items.length};renderContent();}
 else if(a==='delete-category'){if(!confirm('删除此筛选项？课程会解除此分类关联，但不会删除课程。'))return;action.disabled=true;await api('admin/content/courseCategories/'+encodeURIComponent(action.dataset.id),undefined,'DELETE');if(!actionActive())return;toast('筛选项已删除，课程保留在全部中');await load('courseCategories');}
  else if(a==='moderate'){const reason=document.getElementById('reason-'+action.dataset.id).value;action.disabled=true;await api('admin/posts/'+action.dataset.id,{status:action.dataset.status,reason},'PATCH');if(!actionActive())return;toast('审核状态已更新');await load('posts');}
 else if(a==='role'){await api('admin/users/'+action.dataset.id,{role:document.getElementById('role-'+action.dataset.id).value},'PATCH');if(!actionActive())return;toast('角色已更新');load('users');}
 else if(a==='service'){await api('admin/service-requests/'+action.dataset.id,{status:action.dataset.status},'PATCH');if(!actionActive())return;toast('服务需求已更新');load('services');}
 else if(a==='rollback'){action.disabled=true;await api('admin/revisions/'+action.dataset.id,{});if(!actionActive())return;toast('已恢复所选历史版本');load('revisions');}
 else if(a==='export-records'||a==='export-records-csv'){action.disabled=true;const data=await api('admin/records/export');if(!actionActive())return;const csv=a.endsWith('-csv');const rows=[['类别','账号标识','账号','昵称','记录类型','日期','创建时间','会话数','累计秒数','最近活动'],...data.items.map(x=>['记录',x.owner,x.username,x.name,x.kind,x.day,date(x.created),'','','']),...data.timers.map(x=>['计时',x.owner,x.username,x.name,'','','',x.sessions,Math.floor(x.totalMs/1000),date(x.lastSeen)])];downloadAdmin(csv?'东方智慧记录完整导出.csv':'东方智慧记录完整导出.json',csv?'\ufeff'+rows.map(row=>row.map(csvCell).join(',')).join('\r\n'):JSON.stringify(data,null,2),csv?'text/csv;charset=utf-8':'application/json');toast('已导出全部 '+data.total+' 条记录及计时汇总');} 
 }catch(err){if(actionActive()&&!err.handled&&err.code!=='AUTH_CHANGED')showError(err);}finally{if(action.isConnected&&authCurrent(actionEpoch))action.disabled=false;}});
document.addEventListener('change',async e=>{const fileInput=e.target.closest('[data-upload]');if(!fileInput||!fileInput.files[0])return;const file=fileInput.files[0],epoch=state.authEpoch,form=fileInput.closest('form');try{if(file.size>50*1024*1024)throw Error('文件不能超过50MB。');fileInput.disabled=true;let mime=file.type==='audio/x-wav'?'audio/wav':file.type;const r=await fetch('/api/media',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':mime,'X-Filename':encodeURIComponent(file.name)},body:file});const d=await r.json();if(!authCurrent(epoch)||!fileInput.isConnected||!form?.isConnected)return;if(!r.ok){const error=Object.assign(Error(d.error||'上传失败'),{status:r.status});if(r.status===401){requireLogin();error.handled=true;}throw error;}const field=form.querySelector(`[name="${fileInput.dataset.upload}"]`);if(!field)return;field.value=d.url;field.closest('label').querySelector('.asset-preview')?.remove();field.closest('label').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',mediaPreview(d.mime?.startsWith('image/')?'image':d.mime?.startsWith('audio/')?'audio':'media',d.url));toast('素材已上传，请保存或发布内容。');}catch(err){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&fileInput.isConnected)showError(err);}finally{if(authCurrent(epoch)&&fileInput.isConnected)fileInput.disabled=false;}});
document.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{const form=e.target,formId=form.getAttribute('id');if(!['auth-form','content-form','settings-form','integration-form'].includes(formId))return;e.preventDefault();const submitter=e.submitter,epoch=state.authEpoch;const values=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));const output=form.querySelector('.error');if(output)output.textContent='';if(submitter)submitter.disabled=true;try{
 if(formId==='auth-form'){if(authBusy)return;authBusy=true;invalidateAuth(true);const loginEpoch=state.authEpoch;try{const d=await api('auth/'+form.dataset.mode,values);if(!authCurrent(loginEpoch))return;if(!d.user||typeof d.user.id!=='string'||!d.user.id.trim()||typeof d.user.role!=='string')throw Error('登录响应异常，请重新连接核对当前账号。');state.user=d.user;broadcastAuth();if(!['admin','operator','reviewer'].includes(state.user.role)){state.user=null;auth(false,'当前账号没有后台管理权限。');return;}await authBootstrap();}catch(error){if(authCurrent(loginEpoch)&&error.code!=='AUTH_CHANGED'){if(output?.isConnected)output.textContent=error.message;else showError(error);}}finally{authBusy=false;}}
 else if(formId==='content-form'){const item={...state.editing};for(const [key,,type] of schemas[state.nav]){if(type==='categories')item[key]=new FormData(form).getAll(key).map(String);else if(type==='boolean')item[key]=values[key]==='on';else if(type==='certification'){if(state.user.role==='admin')item[key]=values[key]==='on';}else if(type==='number')item[key]=Number(values[key]||0);else if(type==='list')item[key]=String(values[key]||'').split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);else if(type==='lines')item[key]=String(values[key]||'').split('\n').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);else if(type==='sources')item[key]=String(values[key]||'').split('\n').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean).map(line=>{const at=line.indexOf('|');if(at<1||!line.slice(at+1).trim().startsWith('https://'))throw Error('出处格式：名称|HTTPS地址，每行一项。');return [line.slice(0,at).trim(),line.slice(at+1).trim()];});else if(type==='localized-chapters')item[key]=String(values[key]||'').split(/\n\s*\n/).filter(x=>x.trim()).map(block=>{const [title,...lines]=block.split('\n');return [title,lines.join('\n')];});else if(type==='chapters')item[key]=String(values[key]||'').split(/\n\s*\n/).filter(x=>x.trim()).map((block,i)=>{const [title,...lines]=block.split('\n');return [title,item[key]?.[i]?.[1]||title,lines.join('\n'),item[key]?.[i]?.[3]||lines.join('\n')];});else item[key]=values[key]||'';}item.id=values.id;await api('admin/content/'+state.nav,{item,status:submitter?.value||'draft',position:Number(values.position)||0});toast('内容已保存，APP重新打开后读取最新配置。');await load(state.nav);}
 else if(formId==='settings-form'){await api('admin/settings',{ai:{enabled:values.enabled==='on',endpoint:values.endpoint,model:values.model,apiKey:values.apiKey,clearApiKey:values.clearApiKey==='on',prompt:values.prompt,knowledge:values.knowledge,providerId:values.providerId,processingRegions:values.processingRegions.split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean),privacyPolicyVersion:values.privacyPolicyVersion},theme:{'--cta':values.theme_cta,'--bg':values.theme_bg}});toast('AI及主题配置已保存');await load('settings');}
 else if(formId==='integration-form'){let fieldMap;try{fieldMap=JSON.parse(values.fieldMap||'{}');}catch{throw Error('字段映射须为JSON对象。');}await api('admin/settings',{integrations:{[values.provider]:{secret:values.secret,clearSecret:values.clearSecret==='on',fieldMap}}});toast('设备配置已保存');await load('settings');}
 }catch(err){if(!authCurrent(epoch)||err.handled||err.code==='AUTH_CHANGED')return;if(output?.isConnected)output.textContent=err.message;else showError(err);}finally{if(submitter?.isConnected)submitter.disabled=false;}});

function caBadge(value){return '<span class="badge '+esc(value==='manual_review'||value==='ai_review'?'pending':value)+'">'+esc(caStatuses[value]||value)+'</span>';}
function caTime(value){return value?esc(date(value)):'—';}
function caMedia(url,type='video'){
 if(!/^(https:\/\/|\/api\/media\/)/.test(url||''))return '';
 if(type==='image')return '<img class="ca-cover" src="'+esc(url)+'" alt="作品封面" loading="lazy">';
 return type==='audio'?'<audio controls preload="metadata" src="'+esc(url)+'"></audio>':'<video controls playsinline preload="metadata" src="'+esc(url)+'"></video>';
}

async function loadCreatorAdmin(nav,seq){
 caInstallStyle();caState.selected=null;++caState.detailRevision;
 if(nav==='creators'){
  const d=await api('admin/creators');let teachers=[];
  if(state.user.role==='admin')teachers=(await api('admin/content/teachers')).items||[];
  if(seq!==state.seq)return;caState.creators=d.items||[];caState.teachers=teachers;renderCreatorList();
 }else if(nav==='creatorReviews'){
  const d=await api('admin/creator-reviews');if(seq!==state.seq)return;caState.works=d.items||[];renderCreatorReviews();
 }else if(nav==='moderation'){
  if(state.user.role!=='admin')throw Error('审核规则和知识库仅管理员可修改。');
  const d=await api('admin/moderation-settings');if(seq!==state.seq)return;caState.settings=d;renderModerationSettings();
 }
}
function renderCreatorList(){
 const main=document.getElementById('main'),items=caState.creators;
 main.innerHTML=heading('创作者管理','<button class="btn secondary" data-nav="creators">刷新</button>')+
 '<p class="hint">查看老师入驻申请及账号状态。开通资格后，老师在独立创作者中心管理自己的作品和数据。</p>'+
 '<div class="stats">'+[['pending','待审核'],['approved','已开通'],['suspended','已暂停'],['rejected','未通过']].map(([key,label])=>'<div class="stat"><strong>'+items.filter(x=>x.status===key).length+'</strong><span>'+label+'</span></div>').join('')+'</div>'+
 '<div class="editor-layout ca-review-grid"><div>'+table(['创作者／账号','状态','操作'],items.map(p=>[esc(p.displayName||p.name||p.username)+'<br><span class="meta">'+esc(p.username||p.userId)+'</span>',caBadge(p.status),'<button class="btn secondary small" data-ca-profile="'+esc(p.userId)+'">查看资料</button>']))+'</div><section class="panel ca-detail" id="ca-detail"><h2>入驻资料</h2><p class="hint">选择一位创作者，查看申请资料和账号关联。</p><a href="/creator" target="_blank" rel="noopener">打开创作者中心 ↗</a></section></div>';
 enhanceAdminLists();
}
function renderCreatorProfile(userId){
 const p=caState.creators.find(x=>x.userId===userId);if(!p)return;caState.selected=p;
 const admin=state.user.role==='admin',teachers=caState.teachers.filter(t=>t.profileType!=='demo');
 document.getElementById('ca-detail').innerHTML='<div class="ca-statusline">'+caBadge(p.status)+'<span class="meta">账号：'+esc(p.username||p.userId)+'</span></div><h2 style="margin-top:12px">'+esc(p.displayName||p.name||'未填写名称')+'</h2>'+
 (p.avatarUrl&&/^(https:\/\/|\/api\/media\/)/.test(p.avatarUrl)?'<img class="ca-avatar" src="'+esc(p.avatarUrl)+'" alt="申请人头像">':'')+
 '<p class="ca-body">'+esc(p.bio||'暂未填写个人介绍。')+'</p><p class="meta">账号标识：'+esc(p.userId)+'</p><p class="meta">老师资料：'+esc(p.teacherId||'尚未关联')+'</p>'+
 (p.reason?'<p class="ca-note">最近处理说明：'+esc(p.reason)+'</p>':'')+
 (admin?'<form id="ca-profile-form" class="grid-form ca-readonly" data-user-id="'+esc(p.userId)+'"><label class="wide">关联老师资料<select name="teacherId"><option value="">开通时自动建立老师资料</option>'+teachers.map(t=>'<option value="'+esc(t.id)+'"'+(t.id===p.teacherId?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(t.name||t.id)+(t._status==='published'?'':' · 未发布')+'</option>').join('')+(p.teacherId&&!teachers.some(t=>t.id===p.teacherId)?'<option selected value="'+esc(p.teacherId)+'">'+esc(p.teacherId)+'</option>':'')+'</select><span class="field-note">请核实申请人与老师身份一致。已有老师资料只能关联所属账号。</span></label><label class="wide">处理说明<textarea name="reason" rows="3" maxlength="500" minlength="2" required placeholder="记录核验结果或需补充的资料，申请人可看到此说明。"></textarea></label><div class="actions wide"><button class="btn" name="decision" value="approved">'+(p.status==='approved'?'保存开通与关联':'审核通过并开通')+'</button><button class="btn secondary" name="decision" value="rejected">退回申请</button><button class="btn danger" name="decision" value="suspended">暂停创作者资格</button></div><p class="hint wide">暂停后，该账号无法继续创作，已有创作者内容停止公开展示。</p><p class="error wide" role="alert"></p></form>':'<p class="permission-note ca-readonly">你可以查看入驻资料。资格开通、暂停和老师资料关联由管理员处理。</p>');
 document.getElementById('ca-detail').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
}
function renderCreatorReviews(){
 ++caState.detailRevision;caState.selected=null;
 const items=caState.works,filters=[['manual_review','待人工复核'],['ai_review','AI审核中'],['published','已发布'],['rejected','未通过'],['all','全部作品']],visible=caState.filter==='all'?items:items.filter(w=>w.status===caState.filter);
 document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('创作者内容审核','<button class="btn secondary" data-nav="creatorReviews">刷新</button>')+
 '<div class="ca-flow" aria-label="人工审核流程"><span>创作者提交作品</span><b>→</b><span>平台人工复核</span><b>→</b><span>通过后发布到APP</span></div>'+
 '<p class="hint">当前按人工审核处理，AI接入暂缓。先查看原始内容、素材和审核记录，再作出决定。每次审核对应一个作品版本；作品有更新时需重新读取。</p>'+
 '<div class="ca-filters" aria-label="审核状态">'+filters.map(([key,label])=>'<button data-ca-filter="'+key+'" aria-pressed="'+(caState.filter===key)+'">'+label+' <span>'+ (key==='all'?items.length:items.filter(w=>w.status===key).length)+'</span></button>').join('')+'</div>'+
 '<div class="editor-layout ca-review-grid"><div>'+ (visible.length?table(['作品／作者','状态','操作'],visible.map(w=>[esc(w.title)+'<br><span class="meta">'+esc(w.displayName||w.ownerId)+' · '+esc(caTypes[w.type]||w.type)+'</span>',caBadge(w.status)+'<br><small class="meta">v'+esc(w.version)+'</small>','<button class="btn secondary small" data-ca-review="'+esc(w.id)+'">审阅作品</button>'])):'<div class="empty ca-empty">'+(caState.filter==='manual_review'?'当前没有待人工复核的作品。':'该状态下暂无作品。')+'</div>')+'</div><section class="panel ca-detail" id="ca-detail"><h2>作品审阅</h2><p class="hint">从列表选择作品，查看完整内容和审核轨迹。</p></section></div>';
 enhanceAdminLists();
}
function caHistory(items){
 return items.length?'<ol class="ca-history">'+items.map(r=>'<li><div class="ca-statusline"><b>'+esc({ai:'AI审核',human:'人工复核',appeal:'创作者申诉',submit:'提交审核',system:'系统处理'}[r.stage]||r.stage)+'</b><span class="badge">'+esc({approve:'通过',reject:'未通过',manual:'转人工',manual_review:'转人工',published:'通过',rejected:'未通过'}[r.decision]||r.decision)+'</span></div><p>'+esc(r.reason||'无补充说明')+'</p><small class="meta">'+caTime(r.created||r.createdAt)+(r.ruleVersion?' · 规则版本 '+esc(r.ruleVersion):'')+'</small></li>').join('')+'</ol>':'<p class="hint">暂无审核记录。</p>';
}
async function openCreatorReview(workId){
 const seq=state.seq,revision=++caState.detailRevision,detail=document.getElementById('ca-detail'),active=()=>revision===caState.detailRevision&&seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='creatorReviews'&&detail.isConnected&&document.getElementById('ca-detail')===detail;detail.innerHTML='<p class="ca-loading">正在读取作品与审核记录…</p>';
 try{
  const d=await api('admin/creator-reviews/'+encodeURIComponent(workId));if(!active())return;
  const w=d.item||caState.works.find(x=>x.id===workId);if(!w)throw Error('作品已不存在，请刷新列表。');caState.selected=w;
  const eligible=['manual_review','rejected','published'].includes(w.status),sections=Array.isArray(w.sections)?w.sections:[];
  detail.innerHTML='<div class="ca-statusline">'+caBadge(w.status)+'<span class="meta">'+esc(caTypes[w.type]||w.type)+' · 版本 '+esc(w.version)+'</span></div><h2 style="margin-top:12px">'+esc(w.title)+'</h2><p class="meta">'+esc(w.displayName||w.ownerId)+' · '+caTime(w.updatedAt)+'</p>'+
  caMedia(w.coverUrl,'image')+'<div class="ca-body">'+esc(w.body||'无正文')+'</div>'+caMedia(w.mediaUrl,w.format==='audio'?'audio':'video')+
  (sections.length?'<details><summary>课程章节（'+sections.length+'）</summary>'+sections.map((s,i)=>'<section class="ca-section"><b>'+esc(Array.isArray(s)?s[0]:(s.title||'章节 '+(i+1)))+'</b><p>'+esc(Array.isArray(s)?(s[2]||s[1]||''):(s.body||''))+'</p></section>').join('')+'</details>':'')+
  (w.reason?'<p class="ca-note">当前说明：'+esc(w.reason)+'</p>':'')+
  '<details open><summary>审核轨迹</summary>'+caHistory(d.reviews||d.items||[])+'</details>'+
  (eligible?'<form id="ca-review-form" class="grid-form ca-readonly" data-work-id="'+esc(w.id)+'" data-version="'+esc(w.version)+'"><label class="wide">人工复核说明<textarea name="reason" rows="3" maxlength="500" minlength="2" required placeholder="说明判断依据、具体片段和修改要求，创作者可看到此说明。"></textarea></label><div class="actions wide">'+(w.status==='published'?'':'<button class="btn" value="approve">审核通过并发布</button>')+'<button class="btn danger" value="reject">'+(w.status==='published'?'下架并说明原因':'退回修改')+'</button></div><p class="error ca-error wide" role="alert"></p></form>':'<p class="permission-note ca-readonly">'+(w.status==='draft'?'作品尚未提交审核，不能直接发布。':'AI审核正在处理中，完成后再进行人工复核。')+'</p>');
  detail.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
 }catch(e){if(active())detail.innerHTML='<p class="error ca-error" role="alert">'+esc(e.message)+'</p><button class="btn secondary" data-ca-review="'+esc(workId)+'">重新读取</button>';}
}
function renderModerationSettings(){
 const d=caState.settings||{};
 document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('审核规则与知识库','<button class="btn secondary" data-nav="moderation">刷新</button>')+
 '<section class="panel"><div class="ca-intro"><div><h2>内容审核规则</h2><p class="hint">本阶段保留人工复核与申诉，AI接入暂缓。管理员先依据平台规则查看原始内容与素材，再作出处理。</p></div><span class="badge">当前规则版本 '+esc(d.version||1)+'</span></div><div class="ca-flow"><span>创作者提交</span><b>→</b><span>人工复核与申诉</span><b>→</b><span>通过后发布</span></div>'+
 '<p class="ca-note">AI服务'+(d.aiReady?'已有配置，是否启用以实际开关为准。':'尚未配置，本阶段无需填写模型或凭据。')+' 当前AI接入暂缓；关闭AI初审时，作品直接转人工复核。音视频和图片需实际检查，不以标题或自填文字代替审核。</p>'+
 '<form id="ca-moderation-form" class="grid-form"><label class="wide ca-toggle"><input type="checkbox" name="enabled"'+(d.enabled?' checked':'')+'><span>开启 AI 初审<small>关闭时，提交的作品直接进入人工复核。</small></span></label><label class="wide ca-toggle"><input type="checkbox" name="autoApprove"'+(d.autoApprove?' checked':'')+'><span>允许符合条件的内容自动通过<small>规则与知识库完整、服务正常且内容可被完整分析时才适用。保留人工抽检。</small></span></label><label class="wide">审核规则<textarea name="rules" rows="8" maxlength="20000" placeholder="明确允许、禁止及必须人工复核的内容类别；说明判断依据和处理方式。">'+esc(d.rules||'')+'</textarea></label><label class="wide">公司审核知识库<textarea name="knowledge" rows="10" maxlength="30000" placeholder="维护经公司确认的专业资料、事实依据、术语解释与审核案例。">'+esc(d.knowledge||'')+'</textarea></label><p class="hint wide">每次保存生成新规则版本，历史审核保留当时的规则版本。审核知识和规则与日常AI问答内容分别维护。</p><div class="actions wide"><button class="btn">保存审核规则</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-nav="settings">查看接口配置</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-nav="creatorReviews">进入人工复核</button></div><p class="error wide" role="alert"></p></form></section>';
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const profile=e.target.closest('[data-ca-profile]');if(profile){renderCreatorProfile(profile.dataset.caProfile);return;}
 const filter=e.target.closest('[data-ca-filter]');if(filter){caState.filter=filter.dataset.caFilter;renderCreatorReviews();return;}
 const review=e.target.closest('[data-ca-review]');if(review)openCreatorReview(review.dataset.caReview);
});
document.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
 const form=e.target;if(!['ca-profile-form','ca-review-form','ca-moderation-form'].includes(form.id))return;e.preventDefault();
 const values=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),submitter=e.submitter,output=form.querySelector('.error'),buttons=[...form.querySelectorAll('button')],seq=state.seq;
 output.textContent='';buttons.forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);
 try{
  if(form.id==='ca-profile-form'){
   if(state.user.role!=='admin')throw Error('仅管理员可变更创作者资格。');
   if(!String(values.reason||'').trim())throw Error('请填写处理说明。');
   const status=submitter?.value;if(!['approved','rejected','suspended'].includes(status))throw Error('请选择处理方式。');
   await api('admin/creators/'+encodeURIComponent(form.dataset.userId),{status,teacherId:values.teacherId||'',reason:values.reason.trim()},'PATCH');
   if(seq===state.seq){toast('创作者状态已更新');await load('creators');}
  }else if(form.id==='ca-review-form'){
   if(!String(values.reason||'').trim())throw Error('请填写人工复核说明。');
   const decision=submitter?.value;if(!['approve','reject'].includes(decision))throw Error('请选择审核结果。');
   await api('admin/creator-reviews/'+encodeURIComponent(form.dataset.workId),{decision,reason:values.reason.trim(),version:Number(form.dataset.version)});
   if(seq===state.seq){toast(decision==='approve'?'审核通过，作品已发布':'作品已退回，创作者可查看原因');await load('creatorReviews');}
  }else{
   if(state.user.role!=='admin')throw Error('仅管理员可修改审核规则。');
   const enabled=values.enabled==='on',autoApprove=values.autoApprove==='on';
   if(enabled&&(!String(values.rules||'').trim()||!String(values.knowledge||'').trim()))throw Error('开启AI初审前，请填写审核规则和知识库。');
   await api('admin/moderation-settings',{enabled,autoApprove,rules:values.rules||'',knowledge:values.knowledge||''},'PUT');
   if(seq===state.seq){toast('审核规则与知识库已保存');await load('moderation');}
  }
 }catch(err){if(output.isConnected){output.textContent=err.message;if(form.id==='ca-review-form')output.textContent+=' 如作品已更新，请重新读取最新版本后再审核。';}}finally{buttons.forEach(b=>{if(b.isConnected)b.disabled=false;});}
});

// Company analytics: aggregated users only; no personal record or conversation export.
const anStyle=document.createElement('style');
anStyle.textContent='.an-controls{display:flex;align-items:end;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap}.an-controls label{flex:1;min-width:140px;font-size:12px}.an-controls .actions{padding-bottom:1px}.an-presets{display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:14px}.an-summary{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:14px;margin:18px 0}.an-summary span,.an-retention small{display:block;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px}.an-summary strong{font-size:22px}.an-chart{display:block;width:100%;height:auto;max-height:300px;margin-top:16px}.an-chart text{font:12px Arial,sans-serif;fill:var(--muted)}.an-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.3fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:20px}.an-grid>section{min-width:0}.an-retention{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:18px;margin:20px 0}.an-retention strong{display:block;font-size:26px}.an-detail{margin-top:16px}.an-window small{display:block;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:9px}.an-state{font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)}@media(max-width:900px){.an-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}@media(max-width:600px){.an-summary{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}.an-window strong{font-size:25px}.an-controls .actions{width:100%}.an-retention{gap:12px}.an-chart text{font-size:29px}}';
document.head.append(anStyle);
function anToday(){return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Shanghai',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date());}
function anShift(day,n){return new Date(Date.parse(day+'T00:00:00Z')+n*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
function anNumber(n){return n===null||n===undefined?'未采集':Number(n).toLocaleString('zh-CN',{maximumFractionDigits:2});}
function anCoverage(value){return ({uncollected:'未采集',partial:'部分覆盖／进行中',complete:'完整覆盖'})[value]||'';}
function anLocalTime(value){return value?new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-CN',{hour12:false,timeZone:'Asia/Shanghai'}):'尚未开始';}
function anTable(headers,rows){return table(headers,rows).replace('class="table-wrap"','class="table-wrap" data-enhanced="true"');}
function anChart(rows){
 const values=rows.map(x=>x.activeUsers),valid=values.filter(x=>typeof x==='number');
 if(!valid.length)return '<div class="empty">所选区间尚未采集活跃数据</div>';
 const left=94,right=780,top=20,bottom=192,max=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(Math.max(...valid)/2)*2),x=i=>left+(rows.length===1?(right-left)/2:i/(rows.length-1)*(right-left)),y=v=>bottom-v/max*(bottom-top);
 let lines='',points=[];
 function flush(){if(!points.length)return;lines+='<polyline points="'+points.join(' ')+'" fill="none" stroke="#087e83" stroke-width="2.5"/>';points=[];}
 values.forEach((v,i)=>{if(typeof v==='number')points.push(x(i).toFixed(1)+','+y(v).toFixed(1));else flush();});flush();
 if(valid.length===1){const i=values.findIndex(v=>typeof v==='number');lines+='<circle cx="'+x(i)+'" cy="'+y(values[i])+'" r="4" fill="#087e83"/>';}
 const labels=[0,Math.floor((rows.length-1)/2),rows.length-1].filter((v,i,a)=>a.indexOf(v)===i);
 return '<svg class="an-chart" viewBox="0 0 820 230" role="img" aria-label="所选区间每个周期内去重的活跃用户趋势；未采集时段留空"><title>活跃用户趋势，单位：人</title>'+[0,.5,1].map(p=>'<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+y(max*p)+'" x2="'+right+'" y2="'+y(max*p)+'" stroke="#d7e3e6" stroke-dasharray="4 4"/><text x="82" y="'+(y(max*p)+4)+'" text-anchor="end">'+new Intl.NumberFormat('zh-CN',{notation:'compact',maximumFractionDigits:1}).format(Math.round(max*p))+'</text>').join('')+lines+labels.map(i=>'<text x="'+x(i)+'" y="219" text-anchor="'+(i===0?'start':i===rows.length-1?'end':'middle')+'">'+esc(rows[i].date)+'</text>').join('')+'</svg>';
}
async function loadAnalyticsAdmin(seq){
 const end=anToday();state.analyticsFilters??={start:anShift(end,-29),end,grain:'day'};
 let data;try{data=await api('admin/analytics?'+new URLSearchParams(state.analyticsFilters));}catch(error){if(seq===state.seq)state.analytics=null;throw error;}
 if(seq!==state.seq||state.nav!=='analytics')return;
 state.analytics=data;renderAnalyticsAdmin();
}
function renderAnalyticsAdmin(){
 const d=state.analytics,f=state.analyticsFilters,today=anToday();
 const windows=[['day','今日活跃','DAU'],['week','本周活跃','WAU'],['month','本月活跃','MAU'],['year','本年活跃','YAU']];
 const featureRows=(d.features||[]).filter(x=>x.activeUsers>0||x.visits>0);
 document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('数据中心','<div class="actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="analytics">刷新</button><button class="btn" data-action="analytics-export">导出汇总 CSV</button></div>')+
 '<p class="hint">北京时间 · APP 内登录的普通用户与创作者 · 不含管理人员和未登录访问</p><div class="stats">'+windows.map(([key,label,short])=>{const w=d.windows[key];return '<div class="stat an-window"><span>'+label+' · '+short+'</span><strong>'+anNumber(w.activeUsers)+'</strong><small>'+esc(w.start)+' 至 '+esc(w.end)+'</small><small>'+anCoverage(w.coverage)+'</small></div>';}).join('')+'</div>'+
 '<p class="hint">以上四项按自然日、周一开始的自然周、自然月、自然年去重，截至当前。同一账号在一个周期内只计 1 人。</p>'+
 '<section class="panel"><h2>活跃趋势</h2><p class="hint">采集开始：'+esc(anLocalTime(d.collectedSince))+'。此前活跃数据标为“未采集”；今天仍在累计，未覆盖时段不补算。</p>'+
 '<form id="analytics-filter" class="an-controls"><label>开始日期<input type="date" name="start" value="'+esc(f.start)+'" max="'+today+'" required></label><label>结束日期<input type="date" name="end" value="'+esc(f.end)+'" max="'+today+'" required></label><label>汇总周期<select name="grain">'+[['day','按日'],['week','按周'],['month','按月'],['year','按年']].map(([k,v])=>'<option value="'+k+'" '+(f.grain===k?'selected':'')+'>'+v+'</option>').join('')+'</select></label><div class="actions"><button class="btn">查询</button></div></form>'+
 '<div class="an-presets">'+[7,30,90,365].map(n=>'<button class="btn secondary small" data-an-days="'+n+'">近 '+n+' 天</button>').join('')+'</div>'+
 '<p class="an-state">区间活跃覆盖：'+anCoverage(d.summary.coverage)+'</p><div class="an-summary">'+[['区间活跃用户',d.summary.activeUsers],['区间新增用户',d.summary.newUsers],['当前用户账号',d.summary.registeredUsers],['区间功能访问',d.summary.visits]].map(([label,value])=>'<div><span>'+label+'</span><strong>'+anNumber(value)+'</strong></div>').join('')+'</div>'+anChart(d.trend)+
 '<p class="hint">曲线为每个周期内去重的活跃人数，跨周期相加不等于区间活跃人数。功能访问按同账号、同功能 30 秒去重；续用心跳只计活跃，不增加访问。</p>'+
 '<details class="an-detail"><summary>查看周期数据明细</summary>'+anTable(['周期开始','周期结束','活跃用户','新增用户','功能访问','覆盖情况'],d.trend.map(x=>[esc(x.date),esc(x.end),anNumber(x.activeUsers),anNumber(x.newUsers),anNumber(x.visits),anCoverage(x.coverage)]))+'</details></section>'+
 '<div class="an-grid"><section class="panel"><h2>功能使用</h2><p class="hint">所选区间内实际使用的功能；同一用户可使用多个功能，各行人数不相加。</p>'+(d.summary.coverage==='uncollected'?'<div class="empty">所选区间未采集功能使用数据</div>':anTable(['功能','使用用户','访问次数'],featureRows.map(x=>[esc(x.label),anNumber(x.activeUsers),anNumber(x.visits)])))+'</section>'+
 '<section class="panel"><h2>新增用户留存</h2><div class="an-retention">'+(d.retention||[]).map(x=>'<div><span>'+(x.days===1?'次日留存':x.days+' 日留存')+'</span><strong>'+(x.rate===null?'—':anNumber(x.rate)+'%')+'</strong><small>'+(x.eligibleUsers?anNumber(x.returnedUsers)+' / '+anNumber(x.eligibleUsers)+' 个成熟样本':'暂无成熟样本')+'</small></div>').join('')+'</div><p class="hint">按所选区间内注册的账号计算第 1／7 个自然日是否活跃。仅纳入采集开始后注册，且观察日已完整结束的账号；采集前注册及尚未到期的账号不进入分母。</p></section></div>'+
 '<p class="hint">新增用户来自账号注册时间；活跃与功能使用来自 APP 前台实际采集。仅汇总账号关联的功能使用与时间，不采集出生资料、自评或聊天正文。更新于 '+esc(anLocalTime(d.generatedAt))+'。</p>';
}
function exportAnalyticsAdmin(){
 const d=state.analytics;if(!d)return;
 const rows=[['数据集','周期开始','周期结束','指标','数值','单位','说明']],add=(group,start,end,label,value,unit,note='')=>rows.push([group,start,end,label,value===null?'未采集':value,unit,note]);
 add('口径','','','统计时区',d.timezone,'','');add('口径','','','活跃采集开始',d.collectedSince,'','采集前不补算；仅APP登录普通用户与创作者');
 for(const [key,w] of Object.entries(d.windows))add('当前周期活跃',w.start,w.end,({day:'日活DAU',week:'周活WAU',month:'月活MAU',year:'年活YAU'})[key],w.activeUsers,'人',anCoverage(w.coverage));
 for(const [key,label] of [['activeUsers','区间去重活跃用户'],['newUsers','区间新增用户'],['registeredUsers','当前用户账号'],['visits','区间功能访问']])add('区间汇总',d.range.start,d.range.end,label,d.summary[key],key==='visits'?'次':'人',key==='registeredUsers'?'当前账号数，不受日期筛选影响':key==='newUsers'?'真实账号注册数据':anCoverage(d.summary.coverage));
 for(const x of d.trend)for(const [key,label,unit] of [['activeUsers','活跃用户','人'],['newUsers','新增用户','人'],['visits','功能访问','次']])add('趋势/'+d.range.grain,x.date,x.end,label,x[key],unit,key==='newUsers'?'真实账号注册数据':anCoverage(x.coverage));
 for(const x of d.features)for(const [key,label,unit] of [['activeUsers','使用用户','人'],['visits','访问次数','次']])add('功能使用',d.range.start,d.range.end,x.label+'/'+label,x[key],unit,'同账号同功能30秒去重');
 for(const x of d.retention)for(const [key,label,unit] of [['eligibleUsers','成熟样本','人'],['returnedUsers','回访用户','人'],['rate','留存率','%']])add(x.days+'日留存',d.range.start,d.range.end,label,x[key]===null?'暂无成熟样本':x[key],unit,'观察日完整结束；采集启用后注册');
 downloadAdmin('东方智慧数据中心-'+d.range.start+'至'+d.range.end+'-'+d.range.grain+'.csv','\ufeff'+rows.map(row=>row.map(csvCell).join(',')).join('\r\n'),'text/csv;charset=utf-8');toast('已导出当前查询的完整汇总数据');
}
document.addEventListener('submit',e=>{if(e.target.id!=='analytics-filter')return;e.preventDefault();state.analyticsFilters=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.target));load('analytics');});
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('[data-an-reset]')){state.analyticsFilters=null;load('analytics');return;}const preset=e.target.closest('[data-an-days]');if(preset){const end=anToday();state.analyticsFilters={start:anShift(end,1-Number(preset.dataset.anDays)),end,grain:state.analyticsFilters?.grain||'day'};load('analytics');return;}if(e.target.closest('[data-action="analytics-export"]'))exportAnalyticsAdmin();});

// Server-authorized catalog and sandbox order operations in the existing console.
const coAdmin={epoch:-1,data:null,editing:null,order:null,pending:null};
function coAdminReset(){if(coAdmin.epoch!==state.authEpoch)Object.assign(coAdmin,{epoch:state.authEpoch,data:null,editing:null,order:null,pending:null});}
function coAdminPrice(v,c='CNY'){try{const f=new Intl.NumberFormat('zh-CN',{style:'currency',currency:c});return f.format(Number(v||0)/10**f.resolvedOptions().maximumFractionDigits);}catch{return String(v||0)+' '+c;}}
function coAdminStatus(v){return ({expired:'预占已到期',pending:'待支付',unpaid:'待支付',paid:'已支付',closed:'已关闭',refunded:'已退款',partially_refunded:'部分退款',ready:'已发权益',unfulfilled:'待履约',shipped:'已发货',delivered:'已完成',cancelled:'已取消',draft:'草稿',published:'已上架',archived:'已下架',refund_pending:'待退款'})[v]||v||'—';}
async function loadCommerceAdmin(seq){
 coAdminReset();const epoch=state.authEpoch,d=await api('admin/commerce');if(seq!==state.seq||!authCurrent(epoch)||state.nav!=='commerce')return;coAdmin.data=d;renderCommerceAdmin();
}
function coAdminField(name,label,type='text',value='',extra=''){return `<label>${label}<input name="${name}" type="${type}" value="${esc(value??'')}" ${extra}></label>`;}
function coAdminSkuFields(v={},remove=false){
 const options=v.options||[];
 return `<fieldset class="grid-form wide" data-co-sku-row data-sku-id="${esc(v.skuId||'')}" data-stock="${esc(v.stock??0)}">
 <legend>${v.sku?esc(v.sku):'新规格'}</legend>
 ${coAdminField('variantSku','SKU编码','text',v.sku,'required maxlength="100"')}
 ${coAdminField('variantPrice','价格（最小货币单位）','number',v.priceMinor??0,'required min="0" max="1000000000" step="1"')}
 ${coAdminField('variantStock','总库存','number',v.stock??0,'required min="0" max="1000000000" step="1"')}
 <label class="inline-check"><input type="checkbox" name="variantActive" ${v.active!==false?'checked':''}>在售</label>
 <div class="wide grid-form">${Array.from({length:4},(_,i)=>coAdminField('optionName','规格名称','text',options[i]?.name,'maxlength="40"')+coAdminField('optionValue','规格取值','text',options[i]?.value,'maxlength="80"')).join('')}</div>
 ${v.skuId?`<p class="hint wide">已预占 ${Number(v.reserved||0)} 件。取消在售可停止该规格的新购买。</p>`:remove?'<button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-co-admin="remove-sku">移除未保存规格</button>':''}
 </fieldset>`;
}
function coAdminOrderLine(item){const options=(item.options||[]).map(o=>o.name+' · '+o.value).join(' / ');return [item.title||item.snapshot?.title||item.skuId,options,item.sku].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');}
function coAdminProductForm(){
 const p=coAdmin.editing||{},option=(v,label,selected)=>`<option value="${v}" ${selected===v?'selected':''}>${label}</option>`;
 return `<section class="panel" id="co-product-editor"><h2>${p.id?'编辑商品':'新增商品'}</h2><form id="co-product-form" class="grid-form">${coAdminField('title','商品名称','text',p.title,'required maxlength="120"')}<label>商品类型<select name="type">${[['digital','数字作品'],['physical','实物商品'],['membership','会员方案']].map(([v,n])=>option(v,n,p.type||'digital')).join('')}</select></label><label>交易环境<select name="environment">${option('sandbox','沙箱测试，不正式售卖',p.environment||'sandbox')}${option('production','正式商品资料，支付仍关闭',p.environment||'sandbox')}</select></label>${coAdminField('currency','币种 三位ISO代码','text',p.currency||'CNY','required pattern="[A-Z]{3}" maxlength="3"')}${coAdminField('contentId','数字作品对应课程ID','text',p.contentId,'maxlength="100"')}${coAdminField('imageUrl','商品图片 HTTPS或已上传地址','text',p.imageUrl,'maxlength="1500"')}${coAdminField('shippingCountries','配送国家代码 逗号分隔','text',(p.shippingCountries||[]).join(','),'maxlength="600"')}${coAdminField('shippingMinor','运费 最小货币单位整数','number',p.shippingMinor??0,'required min="0" max="1000000000" step="1"')}${coAdminField('taxMinor','税费 最小货币单位整数','number',p.taxMinor??0,'required min="0" max="1000000000" step="1"')}<div class="wide" id="co-variants"><h3>商品规格</h3><p class="hint">填写每个SKU的价格和库存。单规格可以留空规格名称与取值。</p><div data-co-sku-list>${(p.variants||[p]).map((v,i)=>coAdminSkuFields(v,i>0)).join('')}</div><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-co-admin="add-sku">新增规格</button></div><label class="wide">商品介绍<textarea name="description" maxlength="5000">${esc(p.description||'')}</textarea></label><label class="wide">退款与使用条款<textarea name="refundPolicy" maxlength="2000">${esc(p.refundPolicy||'')}</textarea></label><details class="wide"><summary>简繁英藏商品文案</summary><div class="grid-form">${[['Zh','简体中文'],['Hant','繁体中文'],['En','英文'],['Bo','藏文']].map(([suffix,label])=>`<label>${label}标题<input name="title${suffix}" value="${esc(p['title'+suffix]||'')}" maxlength="120"></label><label>${label}说明<textarea name="description${suffix}" maxlength="5000">${esc(p['description'+suffix]||'')}</textarea></label>`).join('')}</div></details><label>发布状态<select name="status">${[['draft','保存草稿'],['published','上架'],['archived','下架']].map(([v,n])=>option(v,n,p.status||'draft')).join('')}</select></label><p class="hint">CNY 100表示人民币1元，JPY 100表示100日元。正式价格、商品授权和条款需项目方确认；当前仅沙箱可模拟交易。数字商品不会因测试订单获得正式内容权限。</p><p class="wide error" data-co-admin-error role="alert"></p><div class="actions wide"><button class="btn" type="submit">保存商品</button><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-co-admin="new">清空表单</button></div></form></section>`;
}
function coAdminOrderDetail(){
 const o=coAdmin.order;if(!o)return '';
 const paid=['paid','partially_refunded'].includes(o.paymentStatus||o.status),physical=(o.items||[]).some(x=>x.type==='physical'||x.snapshot?.type==='physical');
 return `<section class="panel" id="co-order-editor"><h2>订单详情</h2><p class="hint">${esc(o.id)} · ${o.environment==='sandbox'?'沙箱订单，没有真实扣款':'正式订单'} · ${coAdminStatus(o.paymentStatus||o.status)}</p>${table(['商品','数量','已退款数量','单价'],(o.items||[]).map(x=>[esc(coAdminOrderLine(x)),Number(x.quantity),Number(x.refundedQuantity||0),esc(coAdminPrice(x.unitPriceMinor??x.priceMinor,o.currency))]))}<p class="hint">应付 ${esc(coAdminPrice(o.totalMinor,o.currency))}　履约 ${coAdminStatus(o.fulfillmentStatus)}　售后 ${esc(o.aftersaleStatus||'none')}</p>${o.shippingAddress?`<details><summary>收货资料</summary><p class="hint">${esc(Object.values(o.shippingAddress).filter(Boolean).join(' '))}</p></details>`:''}${(o.aftersaleRequests||[]).length?`<h3>售后申请</h3>${table(['原因','状态','时间'],o.aftersaleRequests.map(x=>[esc(x.reason),esc(x.status),date(x.createdAt||x.created)]))}`:''}${paid&&state.user.role==='admin'?`${physical?`<form id="co-fulfill-form" class="grid-form">${coAdminField('carrier','承运方','text',o.tracking?.carrier,'required maxlength="100"')}${coAdminField('trackingNumber','物流单号','text',o.tracking?.trackingNumber,'required maxlength="120"')}<button class="btn secondary" type="submit">登记发货</button><p data-co-admin-error class="error" role="alert"></p></form>`:''}<h3>按项退款</h3><p class="hint">此操作只处理该订单环境的退款账与权益。沙箱不会发起银行退款。每项数量不得超过未退款数量。</p><form id="co-refund-form" class="grid-form">${(o.items||[]).map(x=>coAdminField('refund_'+x.id,esc(coAdminOrderLine(x))+' 退款数量','number',0,`min="0" max="${Math.max(0,x.quantity-(x.refundedQuantity||0))}" step="1"`)).join('')}<label class="inline-check wide"><input type="checkbox" name="confirm" required>确认按所选订单项及数量办理退款</label><button class="btn" type="submit">登记所选项退款</button><p data-co-admin-error class="error" role="alert"></p></form>`:''}</section>`;
}
function renderCommerceAdmin(){
 coAdminReset();const d=coAdmin.data;if(!d||!state.user||state.nav!=='commerce')return;
 const main=document.getElementById('main'),write=state.user.role==='admin';
 main.innerHTML=heading('商城与统一订单','<button class="btn secondary" data-nav="commerce">刷新</button>')+`<p class="hint">商品、订单项、价格快照和权益统一管理。当前正式支付关闭，生产价格及渠道尚待确认；沙箱记录与正式权益隔离。</p>${write?`<section class="panel"><h2>交易开关</h2><form id="co-settings-form"><label class="inline-check"><input name="sandboxEnabled" type="checkbox" ${d.settings?.sandboxEnabled?'checked':''}>开启沙箱商城和模拟付款</label><p class="hint">正式支付开关保持关闭。开通真实渠道及价格规则确认后另行联调。</p><button class="btn secondary" type="submit">保存测试开关</button><p data-co-admin-error class="error" role="alert"></p></form></section>`:''}<section class="panel"><h2>商品目录</h2>${table(['商品','SKU','环境','价格','库存','状态','操作'],(d.products||[]).map(p=>[esc(p.title),esc((p.variants||[p]).map(v=>v.sku+(v.active===false?'（下架）':'')).join(' / ')),p.environment==='sandbox'?'沙箱':'正式',esc((p.variants||[p]).map(v=>coAdminPrice(v.priceMinor,v.currency)).join(' / ')),(p.variants||[p]).map(v=>String(v.availableStock??v.stock)).join(' / '),coAdminStatus(p.status),write?`<button class="btn secondary small" data-co-admin="edit" data-id="${esc(p.id)}">编辑</button>`:'只读']))}</section>${write?coAdminProductForm():''}<section class="panel"><h2>订单</h2>${table(['订单','环境','金额','付款','履约','售后','操作'],(d.orders||[]).map(o=>[esc(o.id),o.environment==='sandbox'?'沙箱':'正式',esc(coAdminPrice(o.totalMinor,o.currency)),coAdminStatus(o.paymentStatus||o.status),coAdminStatus(o.fulfillmentStatus),esc(o.aftersaleStatus||'none'),`<button class="btn secondary small" data-co-admin="order" data-id="${esc(o.id)}">查看</button>`]))}</section>${coAdminOrderDetail()}${write?payAdminPanel():''}${write?callbackAdminPanel():''}${write?recoveryAdminPanel():''}${write?lateAdminPanel():''}${write?digitalAdminPanel():''}${write?opsAdminPanel():''}${write?payRouteAdminPanel():''}`;
}
document.addEventListener('click',async event=>{
 const b=event.target.closest('[data-co-admin]');if(!b)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();coAdminReset();const epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=state.seq,active=()=>authCurrent(epoch)&&state.seq===seq&&state.nav==='commerce';
 try{
  if(b.dataset.coAdmin==='add-sku'){
   const list=document.querySelector('[data-co-sku-list]');
   if(list.children.length>=20)throw Error('最多维护20个SKU。');
   list.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',coAdminSkuFields({},true));
   list.lastElementChild.querySelector('input').focus();
   return;
  }
  if(b.dataset.coAdmin==='remove-sku'){
   b.closest('[data-co-sku-row]').remove();
   return;
  }
  if(b.dataset.coAdmin==='new'){coAdmin.editing=null;renderCommerceAdmin();return;}
  if(b.dataset.coAdmin==='edit'){coAdmin.editing=coAdmin.data?.products.find(p=>p.id===b.dataset.id);renderCommerceAdmin();document.getElementById('co-product-editor')?.scrollIntoView({block:'start'});return;}
  if(b.dataset.coAdmin==='order'){const order=await api('admin/commerce/orders/'+encodeURIComponent(b.dataset.id));if(active()){coAdmin.order=order;coAdmin.pending=null;renderCommerceAdmin();document.getElementById('co-order-editor')?.scrollIntoView({block:'start'});}}
 }catch(error){if(active())showError(error);}
},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',async event=>{
 const form=event.target;if(!['co-product-form','co-settings-form','co-fulfill-form','co-refund-form'].includes(form.id))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!form.checkValidity())return;
 const epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=state.seq,active=()=>authCurrent(epoch)&&seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='commerce',values=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),submit=event.submitter;
 if(submit)submit.disabled=true;
 try{
  if(form.id==='co-settings-form')await api('admin/commerce/settings',{sandboxEnabled:values.sandboxEnabled==='on',productionEnabled:false},'PATCH');
  else if(form.id==='co-product-form'){
   const p=coAdmin.editing||{};
   const variants=[...form.querySelectorAll('[data-co-sku-row]')].map(row=>{
    const fields=name=>[...row.querySelectorAll(`[name="${name}"]`)];
    const input=name=>fields(name)[0];
    const options=fields('optionName').map((field,i)=>({name:field.value.trim(),value:fields('optionValue')[i].value.trim()})).filter(option=>option.name||option.value);
    return {...(row.dataset.skuId?{skuId:row.dataset.skuId,expectedStock:Number(row.dataset.stock)}:{}),
     sku:input('variantSku').value,priceMinor:Number(input('variantPrice').value),stock:Number(input('variantStock').value),active:input('variantActive').checked,options};
   });
   for(const name of ['variantSku','variantPrice','variantStock','variantActive','optionName','optionValue'])delete values[name];
   const item={id:p.id,version:p.version,...values,variants,shippingMinor:Number(values.shippingMinor),taxMinor:Number(values.taxMinor),shippingCountries:String(values.shippingCountries||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean)};
   await api('admin/commerce/products',item);
  }else if(form.id==='co-fulfill-form')await api('admin/commerce/orders/'+encodeURIComponent(coAdmin.order.id)+'/fulfill',values);
  else if(form.id==='co-refund-form'){
   const items=(coAdmin.order.items||[]).map(x=>({itemId:x.id,quantity:Number(values['refund_'+x.id]||0)})).filter(x=>x.quantity>0);if(!items.length)throw Error('请填写至少一项退款数量。');const signature=JSON.stringify(items);if(coAdmin.pending?.signature!==signature)coAdmin.pending={signature,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()};await api('admin/commerce/orders/'+encodeURIComponent(coAdmin.order.id)+'/refund',{requestId:coAdmin.pending.requestId,items});
  }
  if(!active())return;const selected=coAdmin.order?.id;coAdmin.pending=null;coAdmin.editing=null;coAdmin.data=await api('admin/commerce');if(!active())return;if(selected)coAdmin.order=await api('admin/commerce/orders/'+encodeURIComponent(selected));if(active()){renderCommerceAdmin();toast('已保存，可在订单和审计记录中核对。');}
 }catch(error){if(!active()||error.handled)return;const output=form.querySelector('[data-co-admin-error]');if(output?.isConnected)output.textContent=error.message;else showError(error);}
 finally{if(submit?.isConnected)submit.disabled=false;}
},true);

const payAdminTypes={'duplicate-charge':'重复扣款','payment-missing':'支付未到账','restore-failed':'无法恢复购买','cancel-renewal':'取消订阅续费'};
const payAdminStatuses={submitted:'已提交',accepted:'处理中',resolved:'已回复并办结'};
let payAdminSession;
function payAdminState(){
 if(!payAdminSession||payAdminSession.epoch!==state.authEpoch||payAdminSession.seq!==state.seq)
  payAdminSession={epoch:state.authEpoch,seq:state.seq,filter:'',items:[],next:null};
 return payAdminSession;
}
function payAdminPanel(){
 return '<section class="panel" id="payment-support-admin"><h2>支付客服</h2><p class="hint">处理测试工单，核对订单状态与已记录的支付事件。</p><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-pay-admin="load">查看支付工单</button></section>';
}
function payAdminActive(s){
 return authCurrent(s.epoch)&&s.seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='commerce'&&state.user?.role==='admin';
}
function payAdminRender(s){
 const panel=document.getElementById('payment-support-admin');
 if(!payAdminActive(s)||!panel)return;
 const ticket=s.detail?.ticket,payment=s.detail?.payment;
 panel.innerHTML=`<h2>支付客服</h2><div class="actions"><label>工单状态 <select id="pay-admin-filter">
 <option value="">全部状态</option>${Object.entries(payAdminStatuses).map(([key,label])=>`<option value="${key}" ${s.filter===key?'selected':''}>${label}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
 <button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-pay-admin="load" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>刷新工单</button></div>
 ${s.busy?'<p role="status">正在处理…</p>':''}${s.error?`<p class="error" role="alert">${esc(s.error)}</p>`:''}
 ${s.items.length?table(['工单','问题','状态','订单','操作'],s.items.map(item=>[
  esc(item.id),payAdminTypes[item.kind],payAdminStatuses[item.status],esc(item.orderId||'未关联'),
  `<button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-pay-admin="view" data-id="${esc(item.id)}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>查看工单</button>`
 ])):`<p class="hint">${s.loaded?'暂无符合条件的工单。':''}</p>`}
 ${s.next?`<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-pay-admin="more" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>更早的工单</button>`:''}
 ${ticket?`<section class="panel"><h3>${payAdminTypes[ticket.kind]} · ${payAdminStatuses[ticket.status]}</h3>
 <p style="overflow-wrap:anywhere;white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(ticket.message)}</p>
 ${payment?`<p>订单 ${esc(payment.order.id)} · ${coAdminStatus(payment.order.paymentStatus)} · ${coAdminStatus(payment.order.fulfillmentStatus)} · ${esc(coAdminPrice(payment.order.totalMinor,payment.order.currency))}</p>
 ${payment.events.length?table(['记录编号','类型','金额','时间'],payment.events.map(e=>[
  esc(e.id)+(e.id===ticket.eventId?'（已关联）':''),e.kind==='refund'?'测试退款':'测试支付',esc(coAdminPrice(e.amountMinor,e.currency)),esc(new Date(e.created).toLocaleString('zh-CN'))
 ])):'<p>此订单暂无支付记录。</p>'}`:'<p>此工单未关联订单。</p>'}
 ${ticket.replies.map(r=>`<p style="overflow-wrap:anywhere;white-space:pre-wrap"><strong>${payAdminStatuses[r.status]}</strong> · ${esc(new Date(r.created).toLocaleString('zh-CN'))}<br>${esc(r.reply)}</p>`).join('')}
 ${ticket.status!=='resolved'&&!s.detail.accountClosed?`<form id="pay-admin-action" class="grid-form"><label class="wide">给用户的回复${ticket.status==='submitted'?'（选填）':''}<textarea name="reply" rows="3" maxlength="750" ${ticket.status==='accepted'?'required':''} ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>${esc(s.reply||'')}</textarea></label>
 <p class="hint wide">填写核查结果或下一步操作，请勿填写支付凭据和敏感资料。</p><button type="submit" class="btn" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>${ticket.status==='submitted'?'受理工单':'回复并办结'}</button></form>`:''}
 ${s.detail.accountClosed?'<p class="hint">账号已关闭，工单正文已清理。</p>':''}
 </section>`:''}`;
}
async function payAdminLoad(s,more=false){
 const query=new URLSearchParams();
 if(s.filter)query.set('status',s.filter);
 if(more)query.set('before',s.next);
 const data=await api('admin/payment-support/tickets?'+query);
 if(!payAdminActive(s))return;
 s.loaded=true;s.items=more?s.items.concat(data.items.filter(item=>!s.items.some(old=>old.id===item.id))):data.items;s.next=data.next;
}
async function payAdminRun(s,work){
 if(s.busy||!payAdminActive(s))return;
 const reply=document.querySelector('#pay-admin-action textarea');
 if(reply)s.reply=reply.value;
 s.busy=true;s.error='';payAdminRender(s);
 try{await work();}
 catch(error){if(payAdminActive(s)&&!error.handled)s.error=error.message;}
 finally{s.busy=false;payAdminRender(s);}
}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
 const control=event.target.closest('[data-pay-admin]');if(!control)return;
 event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
 const s=payAdminState(),action=control.dataset.payAdmin;
 payAdminRun(s,async()=>{
  if(action==='view'){
   const detail=await api('admin/payment-support/tickets/'+encodeURIComponent(control.dataset.id));
   if(!payAdminActive(s))return;
   if(s.detail?.ticket.id!==detail.ticket.id)s.reply='';
   s.detail=detail;
  }else{
   if(action==='load'&&s.detail){
    s.detail=await api('admin/payment-support/tickets/'+encodeURIComponent(s.detail.ticket.id));
   }
   await payAdminLoad(s,action==='more');
  }
 });
},true);
document.addEventListener('change',event=>{
 if(event.target.id!=='pay-admin-filter')return;
 const s=payAdminState();s.filter=event.target.value;s.next=null;
 payAdminRun(s,()=>payAdminLoad(s));
});
document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{
 if(event.target.id!=='pay-admin-action')return;
 event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
 const s=payAdminState(),ticket=s.detail?.ticket;
 if(!ticket)return;
 const reply=new FormData(event.target).get('reply').trim();
 const body={version:ticket.version,status:ticket.status==='submitted'?'accepted':'resolved',reply};
 const signature=JSON.stringify([ticket.id,body]);
 if(s.signature!==signature){s.signature=signature;s.requestId=crypto.randomUUID();}
 payAdminRun(s,async()=>{
  const data=await api('admin/payment-support/tickets/'+encodeURIComponent(ticket.id)+'/actions',{...body,requestId:s.requestId});
  if(!payAdminActive(s))return;
  s.detail.ticket=data.ticket;s.reply='';s.signature='';
  await payAdminLoad(s);
 });
},true);
