function callbackAdminState(){
 if(!callbackAdminSession||callbackAdminSession.epoch!==state.authEpoch||callbackAdminSession.seq!==state.seq)
  callbackAdminSession={epoch:state.authEpoch,seq:state.seq,filter:'',items:[],next:null};
 return callbackAdminSession;
}
function callbackAdminActive(s){return authCurrent(s.epoch)&&s.seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='commerce'&&state.user?.role==='admin';}
function callbackAdminPanel(){return '<section class="panel" id="payment-callback-admin">'+callbackAdminStyles+'<h2>支付回调收件箱</h2><p class="hint">查看沙箱通知的接收与处理进度。正式支付渠道尚未启用。</p><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-callback-action="load">查看回调收件箱</button></section>';}
function callbackTime(value){return value?new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-CN'):'—';}
function callbackProblem(code){return callbackErrors[code]||code||'—';}
function callbackAdminRender(s){
 const panel=document.getElementById('payment-callback-admin');if(!panel||!callbackAdminActive(s))return;
 const item=s.detail?.item;
 panel.innerHTML=`${callbackAdminStyles}<h2>支付回调收件箱</h2><p class="hint">正式支付渠道尚未启用。收到通知仅表示已保存；订单是否付款，以处理结果为准。当前支持数字与实物沙箱订单，异常及会员通知保留待核查。</p>
 <div class="list-tools"><label>处理状态<select id="callback-status-filter"><option value="">全部状态</option>${Object.entries(callbackStatuses).map(([key,label])=>`<option value="${key}" ${s.filter===key?'selected':''}>${label}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
 <button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-callback-action="load" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>刷新收件箱</button>
 <button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-callback-action="process" ${s.busy||!s.loaded?'disabled':''}>处理最多10条待处理通知</button></div>
 <p class="hint">处理按钮从全部待处理通知中领取，状态筛选只影响列表。等待重试的通知到期后才可领取。</p>
 ${s.busy?'<p role="status">正在处理…</p>':''}${s.error?`<p class="error" role="alert">${esc(s.error)}</p>`:''}${s.notice?`<p role="status">${esc(s.notice)}</p>`:''}
 ${s.items.length?table(['通知与订单','状态','接收次数','处理次数','最近更新','操作'],s.items.map(row=>[
  esc(row.eventId)+'<br>'+esc(row.orderId),esc(callbackStatuses[row.status]||row.status),Number(row.receivedCount),Number(row.attempts),esc(callbackTime(row.updated)),
  `<button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-callback-action="view" data-id="${esc(row.id)}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>查看通知</button>`
 ])):`<p class="hint">${s.loaded?'暂无符合条件的通知。':'点击查看收件箱以读取记录。'}</p>`}
 ${s.next?`<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-callback-action="more" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>更早的通知</button>`:''}
 ${item?`<section class="panel" id="callback-receipt-detail"><h3>通知详情 · ${esc(callbackStatuses[item.status]||item.status)}</h3>
 <p style="overflow-wrap:anywhere">通知编号：${esc(item.eventId)}<br>交易编号：${esc(item.transactionId)}<br>订单编号：${esc(item.orderId)}<br>金额：${esc(coAdminPrice(item.amountMinor,item.currency))}<br>接收时间：${esc(callbackTime(item.created))}<br>下次重试：${esc(callbackTime(item.nextAttempt))}<br>问题：${esc(callbackProblem(item.lastError))}</p>
 ${item.duplicate?'<p class="hint">此通知属于同一笔交易的重复接收，不会再次发放权益或扣减库存。</p>':''}
 ${['retry','manual_review'].includes(item.status)&&item.attempts<5&&!item.duplicate?`<p class="hint">核对问题后可重新排队，累计最多处理5次。重新排队不会直接改动订单。</p><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-callback-action="retry" data-id="${esc(item.id)}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>重新排队</button>`:''}
 ${(s.detail.attempts||[]).length?table(['时间','处理次数','操作','结果','问题'],s.detail.attempts.map(row=>[esc(callbackTime(row.created)),Number(row.attempt),esc(({claim:'领取',apply:'应用',retry:'重新排队',receive:'接收',duplicate:'重复接收',recover:'中断恢复',complete:'处理结束',failed:'处理失败'})[row.action]||row.action),esc(callbackStatuses[row.result]||row.result),esc(callbackProblem(row.errorCode))])):'<p class="hint">尚无处理记录。</p>'}
 </section>`:''}`;
}
async function callbackAdminLoad(s,more=false){
 const query=new URLSearchParams();if(s.filter)query.set('status',s.filter);if(more)query.set('before',s.next);
 const data=await api('admin/payment-callbacks?'+query);if(!callbackAdminActive(s))return;
 s.items=more?s.items.concat(data.items.filter(item=>!s.items.some(old=>old.id===item.id))):data.items;s.next=data.next;s.loaded=true;
}
async function callbackAdminRun(s,work){
 if(s.busy||!callbackAdminActive(s))return;s.busy=true;s.error='';s.notice='';callbackAdminRender(s);
 try{await work();}catch(error){if(callbackAdminActive(s)&&!error.handled)s.error=error.message;}
 finally{s.busy=false;callbackAdminRender(s);}
}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
 const control=event.target.closest('[data-callback-action]');if(!control)return;
 event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const s=callbackAdminState(),action=control.dataset.callbackAction;
 callbackAdminRun(s,async()=>{
  if(action==='view'){
   const detail=await api('admin/payment-callbacks/'+encodeURIComponent(control.dataset.id));if(callbackAdminActive(s))s.detail=detail;return;
  }
  if(action==='process'){
   const result=await api('admin/payment-callbacks/process',{limit:10});if(!callbackAdminActive(s))return;
   s.notice=`本次处理 ${result.processed} 条通知。请查看各通知的处理结果。`;
  }else if(action==='retry'){
   await api('admin/payment-callbacks/'+encodeURIComponent(control.dataset.id)+'/retry',{});if(!callbackAdminActive(s))return;
   s.notice='已重新排队，请核对问题后处理待处理通知。';
  }
  if(s.detail){const detail=await api('admin/payment-callbacks/'+encodeURIComponent(s.detail.item.id));if(!callbackAdminActive(s))return;s.detail=detail;}
  await callbackAdminLoad(s,action==='more');
 });
},true);
document.addEventListener('change',event=>{
 if(event.target.id!=='callback-status-filter')return;
 const s=callbackAdminState();s.filter=event.target.value;s.next=null;callbackAdminRun(s,()=>callbackAdminLoad(s));
});

async function lateAdminLoad(s,more=false){const query=new URLSearchParams();if(s.filter)query.set('status',s.filter);if(more)query.set('before',s.next);const data=await api('admin/payment-late?'+query);if(!lateAdminActive(s))return;s.items=more?s.items.concat(data.items.filter(item=>!s.items.some(old=>old.id===item.id))):data.items;s.next=data.next;s.loaded=true;}
async function lateAdminRun(s,work){if(s.busy||!lateAdminActive(s))return;s.busy=true;s.error='';s.notice='';lateAdminRender(s);try{await work();}catch(error){if(lateAdminActive(s)&&!error.handled)s.error=error.message;}finally{s.busy=false;lateAdminRender(s);}}

// Explicit sandbox assets and finite access rules in the existing commerce console.
let deAdminRuntime;
function deAdminState(){if(!deAdminRuntime||deAdminRuntime.epoch!==state.authEpoch||deAdminRuntime.seq!==state.seq)deAdminRuntime={epoch:state.authEpoch,seq:state.seq,data:null,grants:[],next:null,busy:false,error:''};return deAdminRuntime;}
function deAdminCurrent(s){return authCurrent(s.epoch)&&s.seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='commerce'&&state.user?.role==='admin';}

async function deAdminLoad(s,more=false){const [data,grants]=await Promise.all([api('admin/digital-entitlements'),api('admin/digital-entitlements/grants'+(more&&s.next?'?before='+encodeURIComponent(s.next):''))]);if(!deAdminCurrent(s))return;s.data=data;s.grants=more?s.grants.concat(grants.items.filter(item=>!s.grants.some(old=>old.id===item.id))):grants.items;s.next=grants.next;}
async function deAdminRun(s,work){if(s.busy||!deAdminCurrent(s))return;s.busy=true;s.error='';s.notice='';deAdminRender(s);try{await work();}catch(error){if(deAdminCurrent(s)&&!error.handled)s.error=error.message;}finally{s.busy=false;deAdminRender(s);}}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest('#digital-entitlements-admin [data-de-admin]');if(!button)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const s=deAdminState();deAdminRun(s,async()=>{const action=button.dataset.deAdmin;if(action==='detail'||action==='uses-more'){const id=action==='detail'?button.dataset.id:s.detail?.item.id;if(!id)return;const before=action==='uses-more'?s.detail.usesNext:null;const data=await api('admin/digital-entitlements/grants/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+(before?'?usesBefore='+encodeURIComponent(before):''));if(!deAdminCurrent(s))return;if(before)data.uses=s.detail.uses.concat(data.uses.filter(item=>!s.detail.uses.some(old=>old.id===item.id)));s.detail=data;return;}await deAdminLoad(s,action==='more');});},true);

const opsReasonNames={threshold_unconfirmed:'老化阈值未确认',threshold_null:'老化阈值未确认',missing_table:'来源表缺失',source_missing:'来源缺失',truncated:'读取已截限',invalid_json:'来源格式不符合要求',not_applicable:'当前不适用',backend_no_r2:'此后端没有对象存储消费者'};
function opsSourceLabel(value){const code=String(value||'—'),label=opsSourceNames[code]||(code.startsWith('aging:')?(opsRuleNames[code.slice(6)]||code.slice(6))+'规则':code);return esc(label)+(label!==code?'<br><span class="hint">'+esc(code)+'</span>':'');}
const opsAdminStyles='<style>#local-tasks-admin{min-width:0}#local-tasks-admin .ops-form{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(min(100%,240px),1fr));gap:14px}#local-tasks-admin fieldset{min-width:0;margin:0;border:1px solid var(--line,#cfc8b9);border-radius:10px;padding:12px}#local-tasks-admin .ops-wide{grid-column:1/-1}#local-tasks-admin .ops-reference,#local-tasks-admin pre{overflow-wrap:anywhere;word-break:break-word;white-space:pre-wrap}#local-tasks-admin pre{font:inherit;line-height:1.7}#local-tasks-admin .table-wrap{overflow-x:auto;max-width:100%}#local-tasks-admin table{min-width:900px;table-layout:fixed}#local-tasks-admin td,#local-tasks-admin th{overflow-wrap:anywhere;vertical-align:top}#local-tasks-admin th:first-child{width:250px}#local-tasks-admin .ops-actions{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}#local-tasks-admin .ops-actions>*{max-width:100%}#local-tasks-admin .ops-facts{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(min(100%,220px),1fr));gap:10px}#local-tasks-admin details{margin:12px 0}#local-tasks-admin summary{cursor:pointer;line-height:1.7}#local-tasks-admin .ops-check{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start}#local-tasks-admin .ops-check input{width:auto;flex:none;margin-top:5px}#local-tasks-admin textarea{min-height:100px}#local-tasks-admin .ops-disabled-group{border:0!important;padding:0!important}</style>';
const opsText=v=>esc(opsStatuses[v]||v||'—');
const opsDate=v=>v?new Date(v).toLocaleString('zh-CN'):'—';
const opsClone=v=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
const opsMerge=(old,rows)=>old.concat((rows||[]).filter(r=>!old.some(x=>x.id===r.id)));
function opsState(){if(!opsAdminSession||opsAdminSession.epoch!==state.authEpoch||opsAdminSession.seq!==state.seq)opsAdminSession={epoch:state.authEpoch,seq:state.seq,mount:crypto.randomUUID(),workerId:'browser-'+crypto.randomUUID(),runs:[],workers:[],alerts:[],filter:'',next:{},loaded:{},errors:{},open:new Set(),busy:false};return opsAdminSession;}
function opsActive(s){const node=document.getElementById('local-tasks-admin');return authCurrent(s.epoch)&&state.nav==='commerce'&&state.user?.role==='admin'&&state.seq===s.seq&&(!node||node.dataset.opsMount===s.mount);}
function opsField(name,label,type,value,extra=''){return `<label>${label}<input type="${type}" name="${name}" value="${esc(value??'')}" ${extra}></label>`;}
function opsDetailOpen(s,key){return s.open.has(key)?'open':'';}
function opsMeta(s,key,title,value){return `<details data-ops-detail="${esc(key)}" ${opsDetailOpen(s,key)}><summary>${title}</summary><pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(value??{},null,2))}</pre></details>`;}
function opsAdminPanel(){const s=opsState();return `<section class="panel" id="local-tasks-admin" data-ops-mount="${s.mount}">${opsMarkup(s)}</section>`;}
function opsCoverage(s,c){
 if(!c?.scanId&&!c?.observedAt)return '<p class="hint">尚无成功记录的监测扫描，不能据此认定没有异常。</p>';
 const rows=Array.isArray(c.sources)?c.sources:Object.entries(c.sources||{}).map(([source,v])=>({source,...v}));
 return `<section id="ops-coverage"><h3>监测覆盖</h3><p>观测时间：${esc(opsDate(c.observedAt))} · ${c.partial?'部分本地来源未覆盖':'请按下列实际来源核对覆盖'}</p>${rows.length?table(['来源','适用与覆盖','已读取 / 总数','截限','说明'],rows.map(r=>[opsSourceLabel(r.source||r.name),r.covered===true?'已覆盖':'未覆盖',`${r.loadedRows??'—'} / ${r.totalRows??'—'}`,r.truncated?'是':'否',esc(opsReasonNames[r.reason||r.reasonCode]||r.reason||r.reasonCode||(r.applicable===false?'当前不适用':'—'))])):''}
 ${opsMeta(s,'coverage-rules','规则覆盖与未覆盖原因',c.ruleCoverage||c.rules||{})}${opsMeta(s,'coverage-external','未覆盖项目与外部指标',{uncovered:c.uncovered||[],unsupportedMetrics:c.unsupportedMetrics||{}})}</section>`;
}
function opsConfigForm(s){
 const d=s.draft;if(!d)return '';
 const stale=s.config&&d.revision!==s.config.revision;
 return `<section><h3>运行规则</h3><p>编辑版本 ${Number(d.revision)}。期限留空表示该老化规则未确认；不会按零秒代入。</p>${stale?'<p class="error">服务器配置已有新版本，当前输入仍保留。请载入当前配置后重新核对，不能覆盖新版本。</p>':''}<form id="ops-config-form" class="ops-form"><fieldset class="ops-wide ops-form ops-disabled-group" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>
 <label class="ops-check ops-wide"><input type="checkbox" name="enabled" ${d.enabled?'checked':''}>启用这个本地隔离运行时的任务领取</label>${opsField('ruleVersion','规则版本','text',d.ruleVersion,'maxlength="80"')}
 ${Object.entries(opsKinds).map(([kind,label])=>{const t=d.tasks[kind],max=kind==='payment_recovery'?5:kind==='payment_monitor'?1:10,min=kind==='payment_monitor'?30:5;return `<fieldset data-ops-task="${kind}"><legend>${label}</legend><label class="ops-check"><input type="checkbox" name="${kind}_enabled" ${t.enabled?'checked':''}>启用此任务</label>${opsField(kind+'_interval','间隔（秒）','number',t.intervalSeconds,`required min="${min}" max="3600" step="1"`)}${opsField(kind+'_limit','单批上限','number',t.batchLimit,`required min="1" max="${max}" step="1" ${max===1?'readonly':''}`)}</fieldset>`;}).join('')}
 <fieldset class="ops-wide"><legend>独立老化阈值（秒）</legend><div class="ops-form">${Object.entries(opsThresholds).map(([key,label])=>opsField('age_'+key,label,'number',d.thresholdSeconds[key], 'min="0" max="31536000" step="1"')).join('')}</div></fieldset>
 <label class="ops-check ops-wide"><input type="checkbox" name="confirmed" required ${s.confirmSave?'checked':''}>确认按当前版本保存以上本地任务和监测规则</label><div class="ops-actions ops-wide"><button class="btn secondary" type="submit" ${s.busy||stale?'disabled':''}>保存规则</button><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-ops="config-current" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>载入服务器当前配置（替换未保存输入）</button></div></fieldset></form></section>`;
}
function opsRunDetail(s){
 const d=s.runDetail,r=d?.run;if(!r)return '';
 return `<section class="panel" id="ops-run-detail"><h3>运行记录 · ${opsText(r.status)}</h3><p class="ops-reference">记录：${esc(r.id)}<br>进程标识：${esc(r.workerId)}<br>任务：${esc(opsKinds[r.kind]||opsStatuses[r.kind]||r.kind)}<br>配置版本：${Number(r.configRevision)}<br>开始：${esc(opsDate(r.started))}<br>完成回执：${esc(opsDate(r.finished))}<br>本次处理数量：${Number(r.processed)}<br>核查代码：${esc(r.lastError||'—')}</p>
 ${(d.items||[]).map((item,i)=>`<p>${esc(opsKinds[item.kind]||item.kind)} · ${item.applicable===false?'此后端不适用':opsStatuses[item.status]||item.status} · ${Number(item.processed)}<br>${esc(item.errorCode||'')}</p>${opsMeta(s,'run-sources-'+i,'来源记录与覆盖明细',{sourceIds:item.sourceIds||[],coverage:item.coverage||null})}`).join('')}</section>`;
}
function opsAlertDetail(s){
 const d=s.alertDetail,a=d?.item;if(!a)return '';
 const categories=[['actions','actionsNext','受理记录',['时间','操作','操作者','备注'],r=>[esc(opsDate(r.created)),opsText(r.action),esc(r.actor),esc(r.note||'')]],['notifications','notificationsNext','本机通知事件',['事件','状态','实际尝试次数','下次时间','操作'],r=>[esc(r.eventId),opsText(r.status),Number(r.attempts),esc(opsDate(r.nextAttempt)),['retry','manual_review'].includes(r.status)&&r.attempts<5?`<button class="btn secondary small" type="button" data-ops="notification-retry" data-id="${esc(r.id)}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>原事件重新排队</button>`:'—']],['attempts','attemptsNext','通知尝试',['时间','次数','动作 / 结果','代码'],r=>[esc(opsDate(r.created)),Number(r.attempt),opsText(r.action)+' / '+opsText(r.result),esc(r.errorCode||'—')]],['receipts','receiptsNext','本机确认回执',['事件','实际HTTP','次数','确认时间'],r=>[esc(r.eventId),Number(r.httpStatus),Number(r.attempt),esc(opsDate(r.confirmedAt))]]];
 return `<section class="panel" id="ops-alert-detail"><h3>${esc(opsRuleNames[a.ruleCode]||a.ruleCode)} · ${opsText(a.status)}</h3><p class="ops-reference">告警：${esc(a.id)} · 第 ${Number(a.episode)} 次发生<br>来源：${esc(a.sourceType)} / ${esc(a.sourceId)}<br>首次：${esc(opsDate(a.firstSeen))}<br>最近：${esc(opsDate(a.lastSeen))}<br>观测次数：${Number(a.seenScanCount)}（不是付款失败次数）</p>${opsMeta(s,'alert-summary','来源事实与规则版本',a.summary)}
 ${a.status==='open'?`<form id="ops-ack-form"><fieldset ${s.busy?'disabled':''}><label>内部受理备注<textarea name="note" required maxlength="1000">${esc(s.ackNote||'')}</textarea></label><p class="hint">受理只记录处置责任，不修改订单、退款、权益或原异常状态。</p><button class="btn secondary" type="submit" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>记录已受理</button></fieldset></form>`:`<p>受理人：${esc(a.ackBy||'—')} · ${esc(opsDate(a.ackAt))}<br>解除时间：${esc(opsDate(a.resolvedAt))}</p>`}
 ${categories.map(([key,next,title,headers,row])=>`<h4>${title}</h4>${d[key]?.length?table(headers,d[key].map(row)):'<p class="hint">暂无相应记录。</p>'}${d[next]?`<button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-ops="detail-more" data-category="${key}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>更早的${title}</button>`:''}`).join('')}
 <p class="hint">“本机接收夹具已确认”只表示本机HTTP接收核验，未向真实外部收件人发送。重新排队不重置尝试次数。</p></section>`;
}
function opsMarkup(s){
 const r=s.runtime,disabled=s.busy?'disabled':'',ready=r?.identityReady===true&&r.envEnabled===true;
 return `${opsAdminStyles}<h2>持续任务与异常处置</h2><p class="hint">查看本地任务进程、监测覆盖、告警和处理回执。外部渠道、银行到账与真实收件方分别接入验收。</p><div class="ops-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-ops="refresh" ${disabled}>${s.loaded.runtime?'刷新运行与告警':'查看运行与告警'}</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-ops="tick" ${disabled||!ready||!s.config?.enabled?'disabled':''}>${s.pendingTick?'使用原编号重查并重试':'执行一次当前到期任务'}</button></div>
 ${s.busy?'<p role="status">正在处理，请稍候…</p>':''}${s.error?`<p class="error" role="alert">${esc(s.error)}</p>`:''}${s.notice?`<p role="status">${esc(s.notice)}</p>`:''}${Object.entries(s.errors).map(([key,msg])=>`<p class="error">${esc(key)}读取未完成：${esc(msg)}；已显示记录以各自读取时间为准。</p>`).join('')}
 ${r?`<div class="ops-facts"><p>运行身份：${ready?'已核验本地隔离标识':'未就绪，不能领取任务'}<br>持续领取：${s.config?.enabled?'已启用规则':'已关闭'}<br>本机通知夹具：${r.notificationReady?'已配置':'未配置'}</p><p class="ops-reference">运行标识：${esc(r.runtimeId||'未配置')}<br>APP构建SHA：${esc(r.appSha256||'未核验')}</p></div><p class="hint">本页单次执行不会启动常驻进程。常驻进程须使用本机已核验的启动配置及当前管理员登录；失去权限后停止新领取。关闭配置不表示已开始的业务被取消。</p>${opsConfigForm(s)}`:''}
 ${opsCoverage(s,s.coverage)}<section id="ops-runs"><h3>持久运行记录</h3>${s.runs.length?table(['任务 / 记录','状态','处理数量','开始时间','操作'],s.runs.map(x=>[esc(opsKinds[x.kind]||opsStatuses[x.kind]||x.kind)+'<br>'+esc(x.id),opsText(x.status),Number(x.processed),esc(opsDate(x.started)),`<button class="btn secondary small" type="button" data-ops="run" data-id="${esc(x.id)}" ${disabled}>查看</button>`])):`<p>${s.loaded.runs?'暂无运行记录。':'尚未读取运行记录。'}</p>`}${s.next.runs?`<button class="btn secondary" data-ops="runs-more" ${disabled}>更早的运行记录</button>`:''}<p class="hint">处理数量不是付款、退款或文件核验成功数量，请核对原业务回执。</p></section>${opsRunDetail(s)}
 <section id="ops-workers"><h3>进程心跳</h3>${s.workers.length?table(['进程标识','状态','最后心跳','代码'],s.workers.map(x=>[esc(x.id),opsText(x.status),esc(opsDate(x.lastHeartbeat)),esc(x.lastError||'—')])):`<p>${s.loaded.workers?'暂无已登记进程。':'尚未读取进程。'}</p>`}${s.next.workers?`<button class="btn secondary" data-ops="workers-more" ${disabled}>更早的进程</button>`:''}</section>
 <section id="ops-alerts"><h3>异常与受理</h3><label>状态筛选<select id="ops-alert-filter" ${disabled}><option value="">全部状态</option>${['open','acknowledged','resolved'].map(v=>`<option value="${v}" ${s.filter===v?'selected':''}>${opsStatuses[v]}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>${s.alerts.length?table(['规则 / 来源','状态','最近观测','次数','操作'],s.alerts.map(x=>[esc(opsRuleNames[x.ruleCode]||x.ruleCode)+'<br>'+esc(x.sourceId),opsText(x.status),esc(opsDate(x.lastSeen)),Number(x.seenScanCount),`<button class="btn secondary small" type="button" data-ops="alert" data-id="${esc(x.id)}" ${disabled}>查看与受理</button>`])):`<p>${s.loaded.alerts?'当前筛选下暂无已记录告警；仍须核对监测覆盖。':'尚未读取告警。'}</p>`}${s.next.alerts?`<button class="btn secondary" data-ops="alerts-more" ${disabled}>更早的告警</button>`:''}</section>${opsAlertDetail(s)}`;
}
function opsRender(s){if(!opsActive(s))return;const node=document.getElementById('local-tasks-admin');if(node)node.innerHTML=opsMarkup(s);}
async function opsRun(s,work){if(s.busy||!opsActive(s))return;s.busy=true;s.error='';s.notice='';opsRender(s);try{await work();}catch(e){if(opsActive(s)&&e.status===403){invalidateAuth(true);await authBootstrap();}else if(opsActive(s)&&!e.handled)s.error=e.message||'操作未确认，请回查原记录。';}finally{s.busy=false;opsRender(s);}}
function opsAlertQuery(s,more=false){const q=new URLSearchParams();if(s.filter)q.set('status',s.filter);if(more)q.set('before',s.next.alerts);return q.toString();}
async function opsLoad(s){
 const sequence=(s.readSeq||0)+1;s.readSeq=sequence;
 const keys=['runtime','runs','workers','alerts'],paths=['admin/local-tasks/runtime','admin/local-tasks/runs','admin/local-tasks/workers','admin/payment-alerts?'+opsAlertQuery(s)];
 const results=await Promise.allSettled(paths.map(p=>api(p)));if(!opsActive(s)||s.readSeq!==sequence)return;
 if(results.some(r=>r.status==='rejected'&&r.reason?.status===403)){invalidateAuth(true);await authBootstrap();return;}
 results.forEach((result,i)=>{const key=keys[i];if(result.status!=='fulfilled'){s.errors[key]=result.reason?.message||'暂时无法读取';return;}delete s.errors[key];s.loaded[key]=true;const d=result.value;if(key==='runtime'){s.runtime=d.runtime;s.config=d.config;if(!s.draft)s.draft=opsClone(d.config);}else{s[key]=d.items||[];s.next[key]=d.next;if(key==='alerts')s.coverage=d.coverage;}});
}
async function opsMore(s,key){const path=key==='alerts'?'admin/payment-alerts?'+opsAlertQuery(s,true):'admin/local-tasks/'+key+'?before='+encodeURIComponent(s.next[key]);const d=await api(path);if(!opsActive(s))return;s[key]=opsMerge(s[key],d.items);s.next[key]=d.next;if(key==='alerts')s.coverage=d.coverage;}
function opsDraftFromForm(form,s){const f=new FormData(form),d=s.draft;d.enabled=f.has('enabled');d.ruleVersion=String(f.get('ruleVersion')||'');s.confirmSave=f.has('confirmed');for(const kind of Object.keys(opsKinds))d.tasks[kind]={enabled:f.has(kind+'_enabled'),intervalSeconds:String(f.get(kind+'_interval')??''),batchLimit:String(f.get(kind+'_limit')??'')};for(const key of Object.keys(opsThresholds))d.thresholdSeconds[key]=String(f.get('age_'+key)??'');}
document.addEventListener('input',event=>{if(event.target.closest('#ops-config-form')){const s=opsState();if(!s.busy&&s.draft)opsDraftFromForm(event.target.closest('form'),s);}if(event.target.closest('#ops-ack-form'))opsState().ackNote=event.target.value;},true);
document.addEventListener('toggle',event=>{const details=event.target;if(!details.isConnected||!details.matches?.('#local-tasks-admin details[data-ops-detail]'))return;const s=opsState();if(!opsActive(s))return;if(details.open)s.open.add(details.dataset.opsDetail);else s.open.delete(details.dataset.opsDetail);},true);
document.addEventListener('change',event=>{if(event.target.id!=='ops-alert-filter')return;const s=opsState();if(s.busy)return;s.filter=event.target.value;s.alerts=[];s.next.alerts=null;s.loaded.alerts=false;opsRun(s,()=>opsLoad(s));},true);
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{const b=event.target.closest('#local-tasks-admin [data-ops]');if(!b)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const s=opsState(),action=b.dataset.ops;opsRun(s,async()=>{
 if(action==='refresh'){await opsLoad(s);return;}
 if(action==='config-current'){const d=await api('admin/local-tasks/config');if(!opsActive(s))return;s.config=d.config;s.runtime=d.runtime;s.draft=opsClone(d.config);s.confirmSave=false;s.notice='已载入服务器当前配置。';return;}
 if(action.endsWith('-more')&&['runs-more','workers-more','alerts-more'].includes(action)){await opsMore(s,action.slice(0,-5));return;}
 if(action==='run'){const d=await api('admin/local-tasks/runs/'+encodeURIComponent(b.dataset.id));if(opsActive(s))s.runDetail=d;return;}
 if(action==='alert'){const d=await api('admin/payment-alerts/'+encodeURIComponent(b.dataset.id));if(opsActive(s)){s.alertDetail=d;s.ackNote='';}return;}
 if(action==='detail-more'){const key=b.dataset.category,queryKeys={actions:'actionsBefore',notifications:'notificationsBefore',attempts:'attemptsBefore',receipts:'receiptsBefore'},nextKey=key+'Next';if(!queryKeys[key]||!s.alertDetail?.[nextKey])return;const id=s.alertDetail.item.id,d=await api('admin/payment-alerts/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'?'+queryKeys[key]+'='+encodeURIComponent(s.alertDetail[nextKey]));if(opsActive(s)&&s.alertDetail?.item.id===id){s.alertDetail[key]=opsMerge(s.alertDetail[key],d[key]);s.alertDetail[nextKey]=d[nextKey];s.alertDetail.item=d.item;}return;}
 if(action==='notification-retry'){await api('admin/payment-alerts/notifications/'+encodeURIComponent(b.dataset.id)+'/retry',{});if(!opsActive(s))return;const id=s.alertDetail?.item.id;if(id){const d=await api('admin/payment-alerts/'+encodeURIComponent(id));if(opsActive(s)&&s.alertDetail?.item.id===id)s.alertDetail=d;}s.notice='原事件已重新排队，尝试次数未重置；接收结果以实际回执为准。';return;}
 if(action==='tick'){if(!s.pendingTick)s.pendingTick={runtimeId:s.runtime.runtimeId,workerId:s.workerId,requestId:'browser-tick-'+crypto.randomUUID(),appSha256:s.runtime.appSha256};const d=await api('admin/local-tasks/tick',s.pendingTick);if(!opsActive(s))return;s.runDetail={run:d.run,items:d.items||[]};if(d.run.status!=='running')s.pendingTick=null;await opsLoad(s);s.notice='已读取本次运行记录，请分别核对原业务回执。';}
 });},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{
 const form=event.target;if(!['ops-config-form','ops-ack-form'].includes(form.id))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const s=opsState();if(s.busy||!form.checkValidity())return;
 if(form.id==='ops-config-form'){opsDraftFromForm(form,s);const d=s.draft,body={revision:d.revision,enabled:d.enabled,confirmed:s.confirmSave,ruleVersion:d.ruleVersion.trim(),tasks:Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(opsKinds).map(k=>[k,{enabled:d.tasks[k].enabled,intervalSeconds:Number(d.tasks[k].intervalSeconds),batchLimit:Number(d.tasks[k].batchLimit)}])),thresholdSeconds:Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(opsThresholds).map(k=>[k,String(d.thresholdSeconds[k]??'').trim()===''?null:Number(d.thresholdSeconds[k])]))};opsRun(s,async()=>{const r=await api('admin/local-tasks/config',body,'PATCH');if(!opsActive(s))return;s.config=r.config;s.runtime=r.runtime;s.draft=opsClone(r.config);s.confirmSave=false;s.notice='已按服务器版本保存配置。';});return;}
 const a=s.alertDetail?.item;if(!a)return;const note=String(new FormData(form).get('note')||'').trim();if(!note)return;const meaning=JSON.stringify([a.id,a.version,note]);if(s.pendingAck?.meaning!==meaning)s.pendingAck={meaning,id:a.id,body:{requestId:'ack-'+crypto.randomUUID(),version:a.version,note}};const pending=s.pendingAck;opsRun(s,async()=>{await api('admin/payment-alerts/'+encodeURIComponent(pending.id)+'/ack',pending.body);if(!opsActive(s))return;const d=await api('admin/payment-alerts/'+encodeURIComponent(pending.id));if(!opsActive(s)||s.alertDetail?.item.id!==pending.id)return;s.alertDetail=d;s.pendingAck=null;s.ackNote='';await opsLoad(s);s.notice='已记录受理；原业务异常由后续完整扫描与实际处置结果核验。';});
},true);

// Routing configuration is server authorized, versioned and separate from paid facts.
let payRouteAdminSession=null;
function payRouteAdminState(){if(!payRouteAdminSession||payRouteAdminSession.epoch!==state.authEpoch||payRouteAdminSession.seq!==state.seq)payRouteAdminSession={epoch:state.authEpoch,seq:state.seq,data:null,busy:false,error:'',preview:null,loading:false};return payRouteAdminSession;}
function payRouteAdminActive(s){return payRouteAdminSession===s&&authCurrent(s.epoch)&&s.seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='commerce'&&state.user?.role==='admin';}
function payRouteAdminPanel(){const s=payRouteAdminState();if(!s.data&&!s.loading)queueMicrotask(()=>payRouteAdminLoad(s));return `<section class="panel" id="payment-routing-admin">${payRouteAdminMarkup(s)}</section>`;}

.pr-admin-grid{display:grid;gap:12px;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(min(100%,250px),1fr))}#payment-routing-admin label{display:grid;gap:6px;min-width:0}#payment-routing-admin data-pr-admin-error>${esc(s.error)}</p>${!c?`<p>${s.loading?'正在读取真实配置…':'配置尚未读取。'}</p><button class="btn secondary" data-pr-admin="refresh" ${s.loading?'disabled':''}>重新读取</button>`:`<p>当前修订 style="border:0;padding:0"><div class="pr-admin-grid"><label>结算模式<select name="mode">${[['legacy_sandbox','原沙箱链（无需报价）'],['enforced_sandbox','已确认矩阵与报价'],['paused','暂停新结算与手动付款']].map(([v,n])=>`<option value="${v}" ${c.mode===v?'selected':''}>${n}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>规则版本名称<input name="ruleVersion" value="${esc(c.ruleVersion)}" maxlength="100"></label><label>待确认上下文摘要<input ${c.confirmed?'checked':''}>已核对本轮沙箱规则与上下文（不代表真实渠道批准）</label><label>精确渠道规则 JSON 数组<textarea name="rules" spellcheck="false" required>${esc(JSON.stringify(c.rules,null,2))}</textarea></label><p class="hint">每项为 id、environment（sandbox）、platform（web / app_store / google_play）、region（两位大写地区）、classification、currency（三位大写币种）、channel、reference。仅精确匹配，不使用通配或默认渠道。只有 sandbox data-pr-admin="refresh">读取最新配置</button></fieldset></form><details><summary>只读模拟预览</summary><p>使用已保存规则和自选模拟平台、地区。预览不会签发报价、创建订单、预占库存或发放权益。</p><form id="pr-admin-preview" class="pr-admin-grid"><label>模拟平台<select name="platform"><option>web</option><option>app_store</option><option>google_play</option></select></label><label>模拟地区<input name="region" maxlength="2" data-pr-preview-result>${esc(JSON.stringify(s.preview,null,2))}</pre>`:''}</details><details><summary>不可变配置版本与适配状态</summary><pre>${esc(JSON.stringify({versions:d.versions,next:d.next,adapterReadiness:d.adapterReadiness},null,2))}</pre>${d.next?'<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-pr-admin="more-versions">加载更早版本</button>':''}</details>`}`;}
function payRouteAdminPaint(s){if(!payRouteAdminActive(s))return;const node=document.getElementById('payment-routing-admin');if(node)node.innerHTML=payRouteAdminMarkup(s);}
async function payRouteAdminLoad(s=payRouteAdminState()){if(!payRouteAdminActive(s)||s.loading)return;s.loading=true;s.error='';payRouteAdminPaint(s);try{const d=await api('admin/payment-routing');if(payRouteAdminActive(s))s.data=d;}catch(error){if(payRouteAdminActive(s))s.error=error.message;}finally{if(payRouteAdminActive(s)){s.loading=false;payRouteAdminPaint(s);}}}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{const b=event.target.closest('[data-pr-admin]');if(!b)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const s=payRouteAdminState();if(!payRouteAdminActive(s)||s.busy)return;if(b.dataset.prAdmin==='refresh')return payRouteAdminLoad(s);if(b.dataset.prAdmin==='more-versions')return payRouteAdminMore(s);if(b.dataset.prAdmin==='use-context'){const field=document.querySelector('#pr-admin-config [name=contextDigest]');if(field&&s.data?.context?.ready)field.value=s.data.context.digest;}},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',async event=>{const form=event.target;if(!['pr-admin-config','pr-admin-preview'].includes(form.id))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const s=payRouteAdminState();if(!payRouteAdminActive(s)||s.busy||!form.checkValidity())return;const values=new FormData(form);s.busy=true;s.error='';payRouteAdminPaint(s);try{if(form.id==='pr-admin-config'){const rules=JSON.parse(String(values.get('rules')));if(!Array.isArray(rules))throw Error('规则必须是 JSON 数组。');await api('admin/payment-routing',{revision:s.data.config.revision,mode:values.get('mode'),confirmed:values.get('confirmed')==='on',ruleVersion:values.get('ruleVersion'),contextDigest:values.get('contextDigest'),rules,productionEnabled:false},'PATCH');if(!payRouteAdminActive(s))return;s.data=await api('admin/payment-routing');if(payRouteAdminActive(s)){s.preview=null;toast('已保存新的路由修订，原订单快照保持原值。');}}else{const items=JSON.parse(String(values.get('items')));if(!Array.isArray(items))throw Error('SKU 必须是 JSON 数组。');const result=await api('admin/payment-routing/preview',{environment:'sandbox',context:{platform:values.get('platform'),region:values.get('region')},items});if(payRouteAdminActive(s))s.preview=result;}}catch(error){if(payRouteAdminActive(s))s.error=error.message;}finally{if(payRouteAdminActive(s)){s.busy=false;payRouteAdminPaint(s);}}},true);

async function payRouteAdminMore(s){if(!payRouteAdminActive(s)||s.busy||!s.data?.next)return;s.busy=true;s.error='';payRouteAdminPaint(s);try{const cursor=typeof s.data.next==='string'?s.data.next:s.data.next.before;if(!cursor)throw Error('接口未提供有效版本游标。');const d=await api('admin/payment-routing?before='+encodeURIComponent(cursor));if(payRouteAdminActive(s)){const seen=new Set(s.data.versions.map(v=>v.id));s.data.versions.push(...d.versions.filter(v=>!seen.has(v.id)));s.data.next=d.next;}}catch(error){if(payRouteAdminActive(s))s.error=error.message;}finally{if(payRouteAdminActive(s)){s.busy=false;payRouteAdminPaint(s);}}}

// Administrative privacy declarations contain no provider credentials or user message bodies.
let privacyAdminData=null;
async function loadPrivacyAdmin(seq){
 const epoch=state.authEpoch,[d,r]=await Promise.all([api('admin/privacy/config'),api('admin/privacy/requests')]);
 if(!authCurrent(epoch)||seq!==state.seq||state.nav!=='privacy')return;privacyAdminData=d.config;
 const cfg=d.config,field=(key,label,max)=>`<label>${label}<input name="${key}" value="${esc(cfg[key]||'')}" maxlength="${max}" required></label>`;
 document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('隐私配置与处理申请')+`<section class="panel"><p class="hint">填写真实处理主体、供应商与已确认条款；未确认的保存期限留空。不在这里保存API密钥。数据处理规则改变时必须更新政策版本，用户需要重新确认。</p><form id="privacy-admin-form" class="grid-form">${field('policyVersion','政策版本',80)}${field('controllerName','处理主体',120)}${field('contact','联系路径',240)}<label class="wide">接收方清单 JSON<textarea name="recipients" rows="12" required>${esc(JSON.stringify(cfg.recipients||[],null,2))}</textarea></label><p class="hint wide">每项含 id、name、endpoint、models数组、regions数组、purpose、policyUrl、retentionDays（未知为null）、deletionSupported。实际AI配置中的供应商ID、完整地址、模型、地区及政策版本必须与此一致；不能用显示名称代替实际接收方。</p>${[['recordsDays','个人记录保存天数'],['conversationsDays','会话保存天数'],['backupDays','备份清理天数'],['financeDays','财务保留天数']].map(([k,label])=>`<label>${label}<input name="${k}" type="number" min="0" max="36500" step="1" value="${esc(cfg.retention[k]??'')}" placeholder="尚未确认"></label>`).join('')}${[['trainingImprovement','训练改进的独立用途说明'],['marketing','营销的独立用途说明']].map(([k,label])=>`<label class="wide">${label}<textarea name="${k}" maxlength="2000">${esc(cfg.purposeDetails[k]||'')}</textarea></label>`).join('')}<p class="error wide" data-privacy-admin-error role="alert"></p><button class="btn" type="submit">保存披露版本</button></form></section><section class="panel"><h2>本人提交的处理申请</h2><p class="hint">当前存储、供应商和备份分别核查；待处理申请不会自动标成已删除或已注销。这里仅展示处理元数据，不含会话正文。</p>${table(['申请时间','账号','类型','当前状态','供应商／备份'],r.items.map(x=>[date(x.createdAt),esc(x.accountId),esc(x.kind),esc(x.status),esc(JSON.stringify({providers:x.details.providers,backups:x.details.backups}))]))}</section>${privacyDomainsAdminPanel()}`;
}
document.addEventListener('submit',async event=>{
 if(event.target.id!=='privacy-admin-form')return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
 const form=event.target,epoch=state.authEpoch,values=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),output=form.querySelector('[data-privacy-admin-error]'),button=form.querySelector('button');button.disabled=true;
 try{
  const retention=Object.fromEntries(['recordsDays','conversationsDays','backupDays','financeDays'].map(k=>[k,values[k]===''?null:Number(values[k])]));
  await api('admin/privacy/config',{revision:privacyAdminData.revision,policyVersion:values.policyVersion,controllerName:values.controllerName,contact:values.contact,recipients:JSON.parse(values.recipients),retention,purposeDetails:{trainingImprovement:values.trainingImprovement,marketing:values.marketing}},'PATCH');
  if(!authCurrent(epoch))return;toast('隐私披露版本已保存');await load('privacy');
 }catch(error){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&output.isConnected)output.textContent=error.message;}finally{if(button.isConnected)button.disabled=false;}
},true);

// Continue only existing, owner-confirmed cleanup jobs. There is no cross-account preview.
let pdaRuntime;
function pdaState(){if(!pdaRuntime||pdaRuntime.epoch!==state.authEpoch||pdaRuntime.navSeq!==state.seq)pdaRuntime={epoch:state.authEpoch,navSeq:state.seq,seq:0,busy:false,error:'',notice:'',config:null,jobs:[],next:null,detail:null,selected:''};return pdaRuntime;}
function pdaCurrent(s,seq){return pdaRuntime===s&&authCurrent(s.epoch)&&s.navSeq===state.seq&&state.nav==='privacy'&&state.user?.role==='admin'&&(seq===undefined||seq===s.seq);}
function pdaNodeCurrent(node){const panel=node.closest('#privacy-domains-admin');return !!panel?.isConnected&&Number(panel.dataset.pdaEpoch)===state.authEpoch&&Number(panel.dataset.pdaSeq)===state.seq&&panel.dataset.pdaUser===state.user?.id&&state.nav==='privacy';}
function privacyDomainsAdminPanel(){return state.user?.role==='admin'?`<section class="panel" id="privacy-domains-admin" data-pda-epoch="${state.authEpoch}" data-pda-seq="${state.seq}" data-pda-user="${esc(state.user.id)}"><h2>资料逐域清点与清理</h2><p class="hint">维护独立到期规则，续办本人已确认的批次及真实闭户队列。</p><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="load">查看清理规则与已确认作业</button></section>`:'';}
function pdaDate(value){return value?new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-CN'):'—';}
function pdaStatus(value){return ({preview:'预览',ready:'可继续处理',objects_pending:'文件尚待核验',completed:'所选作业已处理',manual_review:'需要核查',pending:'待处理',processing:'处理中',retry:'等待重试',local_completed:'数据库批次已处理','batch-completed':'本批已处理',manual:'本人明确请求',expiry:'按确认期限',closure:'账号关闭',adopt:'从现在纳管'})[value]||value||'—';}
function pdaDomain(value){return ({creatorDrafts:'未公开私稿',creatorPrivateProfiles:'未完成入驻申请',serviceRequestContent:'咨询正文与日期',privateMedia:'无引用私人素材'})[value]||value;}
function pdaReason(value){return ({source_changed:'目标已变更',referenced:'仍有引用',published_or_reviewed:'公开或审核保护',transaction_linked:'成交关联保护',registered_digital_asset:'数字权益永久隔离素材',shared_object_key:'共享存储文件受保护',unmanaged_object_key:'非受管对象需核查',age_unknown:'历史年龄未知',retention_unconfirmed:'期限未确认',policy_changed:'政策已变更',object_identity_changed:'对象身份已变化',storage_delete_failed:'文件删除尚未成功',storage_verification_failed:'文件缺失尚未核实',retry_exhausted:'重试次数已到上限'})[value]||value||'—';}
function pdaReceipt(row){if(row.mode==='adopt')return `<article class="panel"><h4>从现在纳管 · ${esc(pdaDate(row.created))}</h4><p>回执 ${esc(row.id)}；保护起点 ${esc(pdaDate(row.protectionStart))}；删除正文 ${Number(row.deleted)}。</p><p>仅建立保护跟踪，未删除正文，已有起点保持。</p>${table(['范围','新纳管数量','已有跟踪保持'],Object.entries(row.adoptedCounts||{}).map(([key,n])=>[esc(pdaDomain(key)),String(Number(n)),String(Number(row.alreadyKnownSkipped?.[key]||0))]))}</article>`;const local=row.localDatabase||{},objects=row.objects||{};return `<article class="panel"><h4>${esc(pdaDate(row.created))} · ${esc(pdaStatus(row.mode))}</h4><p class="pda-reference">回执 ${esc(row.id)}</p><p>数据库：${esc(pdaStatus(local.status||row.status))}；待处理 ${Number(local.pending||0)}；变更跳过 ${Number(local.skippedChanged||0)}；引用保护 ${Number(local.skippedReferenced||0)}；已不存在 ${Number(local.missing||0)}</p>${table(['范围','本批实际数量'],Object.entries(local.counts||{}).map(([key,count])=>[esc(pdaDomain(key)),String(Number(count))]))}${objects.applicable?`<p>文件缺失已核实 ${Number(objects.verified||0)}；待处理 ${Number(objects.pending||0)}；待重试 ${Number(objects.retry||0)}；待核查 ${Number(objects.manualReview||0)}</p>`:'<p>此批次未产生远端文件核验任务，数据库逻辑处理以实际条数为准；不证明空闲页、日志或备份安全擦除。</p>'}<p class="hint">供应商和备份删除待确认；浏览器副本清理另行记录，不表示本方全部资料或外部副本已删除。</p></article>`;}
function pdaRender(s){const panel=document.getElementById('privacy-domains-admin');if(!panel||!pdaCurrent(s))return;const job=s.detail?.job;
 panel.innerHTML=`<style>#privacy-domains-admin{min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}#privacy-domains-admin .table-wrap{overflow-x:auto;max-width:100%}#privacy-domains-admin table{min-width:860px;table-layout:fixed}#privacy-domains-admin td{overflow-wrap:anywhere}#privacy-domains-admin textarea{width:100%;max-width:100%;min-width:0}#privacy-domains-admin .pda-reference{overflow-wrap:anywhere}#privacy-domains-admin p{line-height:1.9}</style><h2>资料逐域清点与清理</h2><p class="hint">管理员只能续办本人已经确认的原作业或真实闭户队列，不能代用户新建、扩选或确认删除。数据库、当前文件、供应商及备份结果分别核对。</p><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="load" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>刷新规则与作业</button><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="process" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>处理原已确认文件队列 · 最多10个</button>${s.error?`<p class="error" role="alert">${esc(s.error)}</p>`:''}${s.notice?`<p role="status">${esc(s.notice)}</p>`:''}${s.busy?'<p role="status">正在处理…</p>':''}
 ${s.config?`<section class="panel"><h3>新增四类资料的独立到期政策</h3><p>当前隐私披露版本：${esc(privacyAdminData?.policyVersion||'尚未确认')}</p><form id="pda-config-form"><label>规则 JSON<textarea name="config" rows="16" maxlength="16000" required>${esc(s.configDraft??JSON.stringify(s.config,null,2))}</textarea></label><p class="hint">enabled 只授权按期限执行，不阻止本人手动请求。各 retentionDays 为空值 null 表示未确认；0天表示从保护起点起立即到期，仍逐批复核。启用须 confirmed=true、policyVersion 与当前披露一致、revision 不过期。未部署自动调度，备份及供应商另验。</p><label><input type="checkbox" name="confirmed" required>我已核对当前政策及四项独立期限，明确保存本版本</label><button class="btn" type="submit" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>保存到期规则</button></form></section><section class="panel"><h3>本人已确认或闭户关联的原作业</h3>${s.jobs.length?table(['账号与作业','依据与状态','目标 / 待处理 / 文件待核','观察时间','操作'],s.jobs.map(item=>[esc(item.accountId)+'<br>'+esc(item.id),esc(pdaStatus(item.mode))+'<br>'+esc(pdaStatus(item.status)),`${Number(item.planned)} / ${Number(item.pending)} / ${Number(item.pendingObjects||0)}`,esc(pdaDate(item.asOf)),`<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-pda="detail" data-id="${esc(item.id)}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>查看原作业</button>`])):'<p>暂无已确认作业。未确认的本人预览不会在这里转成删除授权。</p>'}${s.next?`<button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="more" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>更早的已确认作业</button>`:''}</section>`:''}
 ${job?`<section class="panel" id="pda-job-detail"><h3>当前原作业</h3><p class="pda-reference">账号 ${esc(job.accountId)}<br>作业 ${esc(job.id)}<br>范围 ${job.domains.map(x=>esc(pdaDomain(x))).join('、')}<br>政策 ${esc(job.policyVersion||'尚未确认')}<br>观察时间 ${esc(pdaDate(job.asOf))}</p><p>${esc(pdaStatus(job.status))}；总待处理 ${Number(job.pending)}；非媒体 ${Number(job.pendingNonMedia||0)}；媒体候选 ${Number(job.pendingMedia||0)}；文件待核 ${Number(job.pendingObjects||0)}</p>${job.pending?`<form data-pda-apply="${esc(job.id)}" data-version="${job.version}"><p class="hint">继续原授权最多100个目标，执行时再次核对目标版本与引用；不扩展目标，不替用户代填确认。</p><button class="btn" type="submit" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>继续原已确认批次 · 最多100条</button></form>`:''}<h3>当前文件任务</h3>${(s.detail.objects||[]).length?table(['媒体与任务','状态','已尝试 / 下次时间','结果与操作'],s.detail.objects.map(item=>[esc(item.mediaId)+'<br>'+esc(item.id),esc(pdaStatus(item.status)),Number(item.attempts)+' / '+esc(pdaDate(item.nextAttempt)),esc(pdaReason(item.lastError))+(item.attempts<5&&['retry','manual_review'].includes(item.status)?`<br><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="retry-object" data-id="${esc(item.id)}" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>重排原任务，不清零次数</button>`:'')])):'<p>此作业未记录文件任务。</p>'}${s.detail.objectsNext?`<button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="objects-more" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>更多文件任务</button>`:''}${(s.detail.receipts||[]).map(pdaReceipt).join('')}${s.detail.receiptsNext?`<button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-pda="receipts-more" ${s.busy?'disabled':''}>更早的作业回执</button>`:''}</section>`:''}`;
}
async function pdaFetch(s,seq,more=false){const [cfg,jobs]=await Promise.all([api('admin/privacy/domains/config'),api('admin/privacy/domains/jobs'+(more&&s.next?'?before='+encodeURIComponent(s.next):''))]);if(!pdaCurrent(s,seq))return;s.config=cfg.config;s.jobs=more?s.jobs.concat(jobs.items.filter(x=>!s.jobs.some(old=>old.id===x.id))):jobs.items;s.next=jobs.next;if(s.selected){const data=await api('admin/privacy/domains/jobs/'+encodeURIComponent(s.selected));if(pdaCurrent(s,seq))s.detail=data;}}
async function pdaRun(work){const s=pdaState();if(!pdaCurrent(s)||s.busy)return;const seq=++s.seq;s.busy=true;s.error='';s.notice='';pdaRender(s);try{await work(s,seq);}catch(error){if(pdaCurrent(s,seq)&&!error.handled)s.error=error.message;}finally{if(pdaCurrent(s,seq)){s.busy=false;pdaRender(s);}}}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest('#privacy-domains-admin [data-pda]');if(!button)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!pdaNodeCurrent(button))return;pdaRun(async(s,seq)=>{const action=button.dataset.pda;if(action==='detail'){const data=await api('admin/privacy/domains/jobs/'+encodeURIComponent(button.dataset.id));if(pdaCurrent(s,seq)){s.selected=data.job.id;s.detail=data;}return;}if(['objects-more','receipts-more'].includes(action)){const field=action==='objects-more'?'objects':'receipts',cursor=s.detail?.[field+'Next'];if(!cursor)return;const data=await api('admin/privacy/domains/jobs/'+encodeURIComponent(s.selected)+'?'+(field==='objects'?'objectsBefore':'receiptsBefore')+'='+encodeURIComponent(cursor));if(!pdaCurrent(s,seq)||data.job.id!==s.selected)return;s.detail={...s.detail,job:data.job,[field]:s.detail[field].concat(data[field].filter(x=>!s.detail[field].some(old=>old.id===x.id))),[field+'Next']:data[field+'Next']};return;}if(action==='process'){const data=await api('admin/privacy/domains/objects/process',{limit:10});if(!pdaCurrent(s,seq))return;s.notice='本次已处理 '+Number(data.processed)+' 个原文件任务，具体结果见回执。';}if(action==='retry-object'){await api('admin/privacy/domains/objects/'+encodeURIComponent(button.dataset.id)+'/retry',{});if(!pdaCurrent(s,seq))return;s.notice='原文件任务已重排，尝试次数未重置。';}await pdaFetch(s,seq,action==='more');});},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{const form=event.target;if(form.id!=='pda-config-form'&&!form.hasAttribute('data-pda-apply'))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!pdaNodeCurrent(form))return;pdaRun(async(s,seq)=>{if(form.id==='pda-config-form'){const values=new FormData(form);if(!values.has('confirmed'))return;s.configDraft=String(values.get('config'));const config=JSON.parse(s.configDraft);await api('admin/privacy/domains/config',config,'PATCH');if(!pdaCurrent(s,seq))return;s.configDraft=null;s.notice='四类资料的独立规则已保存；未部署自动清理。';}else{const job=s.detail?.job;if(!job||job.id!==form.dataset.pdaApply||job.version!==Number(form.dataset.version))return;if(!s.applyRequest||s.applyRequest.jobId!==job.id||s.applyRequest.version!==job.version)s.applyRequest={jobId:job.id,version:job.version,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()};await api('admin/privacy/domains/jobs/'+encodeURIComponent(job.id)+'/apply',{requestId:s.applyRequest.requestId,version:job.version});if(!pdaCurrent(s,seq))return;s.applyRequest=null;s.notice='原已确认批次已处理，请分别核对数据库和文件结果。';}await pdaFetch(s,seq);});},true);

const hvAdmin={epoch:-1,items:[],editing:null,busy:false,error:''};
const hvAdminLanguages=[['zh','简体中文'],['zh-Hant','繁体中文'],['en','英文'],['bo','藏文']];
function hvAdminReset(){if(hvAdmin.epoch!==state.authEpoch)Object.assign(hvAdmin,{epoch:state.authEpoch,items:[],editing:null,busy:false,error:''});}
function hvAdminPause(release=false){for(const video of document.querySelectorAll('#hv-admin-editor video')){video.pause();if(release===true){video.removeAttribute('src');video.load();}}}
function hvAdminField(name,label,value='',type='text'){return `<label>${label}<input name="${name}" type="${type}" value="${esc(value??'')}" maxlength="${name==='mediaUrl'||name==='coverUrl'||name==='target'?'1500':'200'}"></label>`;}
function hvAdminEditor(){
 const item=hvAdmin.editing;if(!item)return '<h2>选择或新增视频</h2><p class="hint">这里复用首页轮播内容，不复制旧图文。当前没有正式介绍片时保持空列表或草稿。</p>';
 return `<h2>${item._new?'新增介绍视频':'编辑介绍视频'}</h2><form id="hv-admin-form" class="grid-form">${hvAdminField('id','内容编号',item.id)}${hvAdminField('position','排序值',item._position||0,'number')}${[['zh','简体标题'],['hant','繁体标题'],['en','英文标题'],['bo','藏文标题']].map(([key,label])=>hvAdminField(key,label,item[key]||'')).join('')}<label class="wide">视频地址<input name="mediaUrl" value="${esc(item.mediaUrl||'')}" maxlength="1500" placeholder="HTTPS地址或本站已上传素材"><input type="file" data-upload="mediaUrl" accept="video/mp4,video/webm,video/quicktime"><span class="field-note">读取真实视频文件后再保存。目标端编码、首帧与声音仍需实测；不是上传成功就代表全端验收。</span></label><label class="wide">专用封面<input name="coverUrl" value="${esc(item.coverUrl||'')}" maxlength="1500"><input type="file" data-upload="coverUrl" accept="image/png,image/jpeg"></label>${item.mediaUrl?mediaPreview('media',item.mediaUrl):''}<details class="wide"><summary>轮播多语说明与跳转按钮</summary><div class="grid-form">${[['','简体'],['Hant','繁体'],['En','英文'],['Bo','藏文']].map(([suffix,label])=>`${hvAdminField('bannerLabel'+suffix,label+'栏目标签',item['bannerLabel'+suffix]||'')}<label>${label}说明<textarea name="description${suffix}" maxlength="1000">${esc(item['description'+suffix]||'')}</textarea></label>${hvAdminField('buttonLabel'+suffix,label+'跳转按钮',item['buttonLabel'+suffix]||'')}`).join('')}</div></details>${hvAdminField('target','可选跳转 APP页面编号或HTTPS网址',item.target||'')}<fieldset class="wide" style="border:1px solid var(--line);padding:15px"><legend>独立字幕</legend><p class="hint">正式发布至少提供简繁英三条；藏文可另行审核后补齐。每条选择HTTPS WebVTT地址或导入/填写真实WEBVTT文本其中一种。导入仅把字幕文字存入内容配置，不声称已上传到生产CDN。</p>${hvAdminLanguages.map(([lang,label])=>{const track=(item.subtitleTracks||[]).find(t=>t.lang===lang)||{};return maxlength="20000" rows="5" placeholder="WEBVTT">${esc(track.text||'')}</textarea></label><label>从本机读取.vtt<input type="file" data-hv-caption="${lang}" accept=".vtt,text/vtt"></label></details>`;}).join('')}</fieldset><label class="wide">素材授权与成片依据<textarea name="rightsNote" rows="3" maxlength="2000" placeholder="记录版权/商用授权及审核依据；不要填写密钥、密码或用户个人资料">${esc(item.rightsNote||'')}</textarea></label><label class="wide inline-check"><input type="checkbox" name="mediaReviewed" ${item.mediaReviewed?'checked':''}>已实际审看成片、字幕及授权材料</label><label class="wide inline-check"><input type="checkbox" name="enabled" ${item.enabled!==false?'checked':''}>在首页展示；正式停用建议归档</label><p function renderHomeVideoAdmin(){
 hvAdminReset();if(state.nav!=='homeVideos'||!state.user)return;hvAdminPause(true);document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('首页介绍视频','<div class="actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="homeVideos">刷新</button><button class="btn" data-hv-admin="new">新增视频</button></div>')+'<p class="hint">复用真实首页轮播，先完成素材与语言审核再发布。字幕和封面不代表视频服务、CDN或目标设备已验收。</p><div class="editor-layout"><section>'+table(['标题','状态','排序','操作'],hvAdmin.items.map(item=>[esc(item.zh||item.id),badge(item._status),String(item._position),`<button class="btn secondary small" data-hv-admin="edit" data-id="${esc(item.id)}">编辑</button>`]))+'</section><section class="panel" id="hv-admin-editor">'+hvAdminEditor()+'</section></div>';const id=document.querySelector('#hv-admin-form input[name=id]');if(id)id.readOnly=!hvAdmin.editing?._new;
}
const hvAdminLoadBefore=load;
load=async function(nav){
 if(nav!=='homeVideos'){hvAdminPause(true);return hvAdminLoadBefore(nav);}
 if(!state.user||!document.getElementById('main'))return false;hvAdminReset();const epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=++state.seq;state.nav=nav;state.editing=null;
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));const main=document.getElementById('main');main.innerHTML=heading('首页介绍视频')+'<p class="hint">正在读取…</p>';
 try{const data=await api('admin/videos');if(!authCurrent(epoch)||state.seq!==seq||state.nav!==nav)return false;hvAdmin.items=data.items||[];hvAdmin.editing=null;hvAdmin.error='';renderHomeVideoAdmin();return true;}
 catch(error){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&state.seq===seq&&main.isConnected&&!error.handled){main.innerHTML=heading('首页介绍视频')+'<p class="error">'+esc(error.message)+'</p><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="homeVideos">重试</button>';}return false;}
};
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
 if(event.target.closest('[data-action="logout"]'))hvAdminPause(true);
 const button=event.target.closest('[data-hv-admin]');if(!button)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();hvAdminReset();if(state.nav!=='homeVideos'||hvAdmin.busy)return;
 if(button.dataset.hvAdmin==='new')hvAdmin.editing={id:'intro_'+Date.now().toString(36),mediaType:'video',_new:true,_position:0,_updatedAt:0,subtitleTracks:[],enabled:true};
 if(button.dataset.hvAdmin==='edit'){const item=hvAdmin.items.find(row=>row.id===button.dataset.id);hvAdmin.editing=item?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(item)):null;}
 hvAdmin.error='';renderHomeVideoAdmin();
},true);
document.addEventListener('change',async event=>{
 const input=event.target.closest('[data-hv-caption]');if(!input?.files?.[0])return;const file=input.files[0],epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=state.seq,form=input.closest('form');
 try{if(file.size>80000)throw Error('每份字幕文件最多80KB，读取后文本最多20000字符。');const text=await file.text();if(!authCurrent(epoch)||seq!==state.seq||!form?.isConnected)return;if(text.length>20000||!/^\uFEFF?WEBVTT(?:[ \t][^\r\n]*)?[\r\n]/.test(text))throw Error('请选择真实WEBVTT字幕文件。');form.elements['trackText_'+input.dataset.hvCaption].value=text;form.elements['trackUrl_'+input.dataset.hvCaption].value='';}
 catch(error){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&seq===state.seq&&form?.isConnected)form.querySelector('[data-hv-admin-error]').textContent=error.message;}
},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',async event=>{
 const form=event.target;if(form.getAttribute('id')!=='hv-admin-form')return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();hvAdminReset();if(hvAdmin.busy||!state.user)return;
 const epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=state.seq,active=()=>authCurrent(epoch)&&seq===state.seq&&state.nav==='homeVideos',values=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),submit=event.submitter;
 const item={id:String(values.id||''),mediaType:'video',mediaUrl:String(values.mediaUrl||''),coverUrl:String(values.coverUrl||''),target:String(values.target||''),rightsNote:String(values.rightsNote||''),mediaReviewed:values.mediaReviewed==='on',enabled:values.enabled==='on',subtitleTracks:[]};
 for(const key of ['zh','en','hant','bo','bannerLabel','bannerLabelEn','bannerLabelHant','bannerLabelBo','description','descriptionEn','descriptionHant','descriptionBo','buttonLabel','buttonLabelEn','buttonLabelHant','buttonLabelBo'])item[key]=String(values[key]||'');
 for(const [lang] of hvAdminLanguages){const url=String(values['trackUrl_'+lang]||'').trim(),text=String(values['trackText_'+lang]||'').trim();if(url||text)item.subtitleTracks.push({lang,...(url?{url}:{}),...(text?{text}:{})});}
 hvAdmin.busy=true;if(submit)submit.disabled=true;
 try{const data=await api('admin/videos',{item,status:submit?.value||'draft',position:Number(values.position),expectedUpdatedAt:hvAdmin.editing?._updatedAt||0});if(!active())return;hvAdmin.editing=data.item;const old=hvAdmin.items.findIndex(row=>row.id===data.item.id);if(old<0)hvAdmin.items.push(data.item);else hvAdmin.items[old]=data.item;hvAdmin.error='';renderHomeVideoAdmin();toast('视频配置已保存，实际素材仍按目标端验收。');}
 catch(error){if(active()&&!error.handled){hvAdmin.error=error.message;const output=form.querySelector('[data-hv-admin-error]');if(output?.isConnected)output.textContent=error.message;}}
 finally{if(authCurrent(epoch)){hvAdmin.busy=false;if(submit?.isConnected)submit.disabled=false;}}
},true);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)hvAdminPause();});window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>hvAdminPause(true));window.addEventListener('storage',event=>{if(event.key==='ew-identity-change-v1'){hvAdminPause(true);Object.assign(hvAdmin,{epoch:-1,items:[],editing:null,busy:false,error:''});}});

}

load=async function(nav){
 if(nav!=='aiGateway')return agAdminLoadBefore(nav);
 if(!state.user||!document.getElementById('main'))return false;agAdminReset();const epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=++state.seq;state.nav=nav;
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));const main=document.getElementById('main');main.innerHTML=heading('AI网关与测试额度')+'<p class="hint">正在读取…</p>';
 try{const [d,u,quota]=await Promise.all([api('admin/ai-gateway'),api('admin/users'),api('admin/membership-ai-quota')]);if(!authCurrent(epoch)||seq!==state.seq||state.nav!==nav)return false;agAdmin.config=d.config;agAdmin.users=u.items||[];agAdmin.quotaPolicy=quota.policy;renderAIGatewayAdmin();return true;}
 catch(error){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&seq===state.seq&&main.isConnected&&!error.handled)main.innerHTML=heading('AI网关与测试额度')+'<p class="error">'+esc(error.message)+'</p>';return false;}

document.addEventListener('submit',async event=>{
 const form=event.target,formId=form.getAttribute('id');if(!['ag-admin-config','ag-admin-grant'].includes(formId))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();agAdminReset();if(agAdmin.busy)return;

  if(formId==='ag-admin-config'){const data=JSON.parse(fields.get('configuration'));data.revision=agAdmin.config.revision;await api('admin/ai-gateway/config',data,'PATCH');}
  else{const accountId=String(fields.get('accountId')||''),credits=Number(fields.get('credits')),signature=JSON.stringify({accountId,credits});if(agAdmin.grantSignature!==signature){agAdmin.grantSignature=signature;agAdmin.grantKey=crypto.randomUUID();}await api('admin/ai-gateway/grants',{requestId:agAdmin.grantKey,accountId,credits,environment:'local-test'});if(authCurrent(epoch)){agAdmin.grantKey=null;agAdmin.grantSignature='';}}

 }catch(error){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&output.isConnected)output.textContent=error.message;}
 finally{if(authCurrent(epoch))agAdmin.busy=false;if(button.isConnected)button.disabled=false;}
},true);

// Explicit file preview and receipts, bound to the current administrator session.
let ciRuntime=null;
function ciState(){if(!ciRuntime||ciRuntime.epoch!==state.authEpoch||ciRuntime.actor!==state.user?.id)ciRuntime={epoch:state.authEpoch,actor:state.user?.id,busy:false,message:'',file:null,requests:{},jobs:[],jobsNext:null,detail:null,issues:[],diff:[],activation:null};return ciRuntime;}
function ciCurrent(s){return ciRuntime===s&&authCurrent(s.epoch)&&state.user?.id===s.actor&&state.user?.role==='admin';}
const ciKey=(s,key)=>s.requests[key]||(s.requests[key]=crypto.randomUUID());
const ciJSON=value=>'<pre class="ci-json">'+esc(JSON.stringify(value,null,2))+'</pre>';
const ciButton=(action,title,extra='')=>'<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-ci="'+action+'" '+extra+'>'+esc(title)+'</button>';
async function ciWork(s,task){if(!ciCurrent(s)||s.busy)return;s.busy=true;s.message='';ciRender();try{await task();}catch(e){if(ciCurrent(s)&&e.code!=='AUTH_CHANGED')s.message=e.message;}finally{if(ciCurrent(s)){s.busy=false;ciRender();}}}
async function ciCall(s,path,body,method){try{return await api('admin/cards/'+path,body,method);}catch(e){if(ciCurrent(s)&&e.status===403){ciRuntime=null;csAdmin.draft=null;csAdmin.data=null;document.getElementById('ci-admin')?.remove();reconcileAuth();}throw e;}}
async function ciExport(s){
 const response=await fetch('/api/admin/cards/imports/'+encodeURIComponent(s.detail.job.id)+'/export',{credentials:'same-origin'});if(!ciCurrent(s))return;
 if(!response.ok){if(response.status===401){requireLogin();return;}if(response.status===403){ciRuntime=null;csAdmin.draft=null;csAdmin.data=null;reconcileAuth();return;}throw Error('导出失败，请回查原作业后重试。');}
 const reader=response.body.getReader(),decoder=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}),encoder=new TextEncoder(),parts=[],manifest=[];let pending='',header=null,complete=null,sourceSeen=false,chunk=null,lines=[],issues=0,diffs=0;
 const digest=async text=>Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',encoder.encode(text))),v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
 const canonical=value=>value===null||typeof value!=='object'?JSON.stringify(value):Array.isArray(value)?'['+value.map(canonical).join(',')+']':'{'+Object.keys(value).sort().map(k=>JSON.stringify(k)+':'+canonical(value[k])).join(',')+'}';
 async function finishChunk(){if(!chunk)return;if(lines.length!==chunk.count||await digest(lines.join(''))!==chunk.sha256)throw Error('导出校验未通过，请重新回查原作业。');manifest.push({kind:chunk.kind,ordinal:chunk.ordinal,count:chunk.count,sha256:chunk.sha256});chunk=null;lines=[];}
 async function consume(line){if(!line)throw Error('导出格式不完整。');const row=JSON.parse(line);if(complete)throw Error('导出终态后出现额外内容。');if(row.type==='error'){ciRuntime=null;csAdmin.draft=null;csAdmin.data=null;document.getElementById('ci-admin')?.remove();reconcileAuth();throw Error('导出期间权限或资料已变化，请重新读取。');}if(!header){if(row.type!=='header'||row.schema!==1||row.job?.id!==s.detail.job.id||row.sourceSha256!==s.detail.job.sourceSha256||row.canonicalSha256!==s.detail.job.canonicalSha256)throw Error('导出缺少有效开头。');header=row;return;}
  if(row.type==='source'){if(sourceSeen||chunk||typeof row.rawText!=='string'||await digest(row.rawText)!==header.sourceSha256||encoder.encode(row.rawText).length!==header.rawByteCount)throw Error('原文件摘要校验未通过。');sourceSeen=true;return;}
  if(row.type==='chunk'){await finishChunk();if(!sourceSeen||!['issues','diff'].includes(row.kind)||!Number.isSafeInteger(row.count)||row.count<1||row.count>50||row.ordinal!==manifest.filter(x=>x.kind===row.kind).length)throw Error('导出分段不完整。');chunk=row;return;}
  if(row.type==='issue'||row.type==='diff'){const kind=row.type==='issue'?'issues':'diff',expected=kind==='issues'?issues:diffs;if(!chunk||chunk.kind!==kind||row.index!==expected||lines.length>=chunk.count)throw Error('导出记录顺序不完整。');lines.push(line+'\n');if(kind==='issues')issues++;else diffs++;return;}
  if(row.type==='complete'){await finishChunk();if(!sourceSeen||row.complete!==true||row.issues!==issues||row.diff!==diffs||header.summary.issues!==issues||header.summary.diff!==diffs||row.chunkManifestSha256!==await digest(canonical(manifest)))throw Error('导出未完整结束，请回查后重试。');complete=row;return;}throw Error('导出包含未知记录。');
 }
 try{for(;;){if(!ciCurrent(s))return;const {done,value}=await reader.read();if(done)break;parts.push(value);pending+=decoder.decode(value,{stream:true});let pos;while((pos=pending.indexOf('\n'))>=0){const line=pending.slice(0,pos);pending=pending.slice(pos+1);await consume(line);if(!ciCurrent(s))return;}if(pending.length>2097152)throw Error('导出单行超出约定限制。');}pending+=decoder.decode();if(pending||!complete)throw Error('导出中断，未保存不完整文件。');if(!ciCurrent(s))return;const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(parts,{type:'application/x-ndjson;charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='card-import-'+s.detail.job.id+'.ndjson';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),0);s.message='完整来源与诊断已校验，可保存私有导出文件。';}finally{await reader.cancel().catch(()=>{});}
}
function ciRender(){const s=ciState(),main=document.getElementById('main');if(!ciCurrent(s)||state.nav!=='cardService'||!main||!csAdmin.data)return;let host=document.getElementById('ci-admin');if(!host){host=document.createElement('section');host.id='ci-admin';host.className='panel ci-panel';main.querySelector('[data-cs-admin-epoch]')?.prepend(host);}const d=s.detail,j=d?.job;
 host.innerHTML='<h2>卡组文件导入与版本切换</h2><p>选择本人有权处理的 UTF-8 JSON 文件，先查看全部诊断与差异，再确认保存独立草稿。文件不自动发布，不补造正式卡文、翻译或版权批准。</p><label>卡组 JSON（最多256 KiB）<input type="file" accept=".json,application/json" id="ci-file"></label><p data-ci-file>'+esc(s.file?.name||'尚未选择文件')+'</p><div class="actions">'+ciButton('preview','预览所选文件')+ciButton('jobs','回查本人导入作业')+'</div><p role="alert" data-ci-message>'+esc(s.message)+'</p>'+table(['作业 / 状态','源文件摘要','操作'],s.jobs.map(x=>[esc(x.id)+' / '+esc(x.status),'<code>'+esc(x.sourceSha256)+'</code>',ciButton('open','回查','data-id="'+esc(x.id)+'"')]))+(s.jobsNext?ciButton('jobs-more','更多作业'):'')+
 (j?'<article class="ci-job" data-ci-job="'+esc(j.id)+'"><h3>冻结预览 · '+esc(j.status)+'</h3><h4>实际冻结目标</h4>'+ciJSON(d.target||null)+'<h4>提交来源声明（不代表审核通过）</h4>'+ciJSON(d.provenanceSummary||null)+'<p>修订 '+esc(j.version)+'；来源 SHA-256：<code>'+esc(j.sourceSha256)+'</code></p>'+ciJSON(d.summary)+'<p>问题 '+s.issues.length+' / '+esc(d.summary?.issues??'?')+'；差异 '+s.diff.length+' / '+esc(d.summary?.diff??'?')+'。分页尚未读完时可继续读取或导出完整清单。</p>'+table(['卡 / 语言 / 字段','诊断','阻止'],s.issues.map(x=>[esc(x.cardId||'卡组')+' / '+esc(x.language||'—')+'<br><code>'+esc(x.path)+'</code>',esc(x.severity)+' · '+esc(x.code)+'<p>'+esc(x.message)+'</p>',(x.blocksApply?'保存草稿 ':'')+(x.blocksPublish?'发布':'')]))+(d.issuesNext?ciButton('issues-more','更多问题'):'')+table(['卡 / 字段 / 变化','原值','新值'],s.diff.map(x=>[esc(x.cardId||'卡组')+'<br>'+esc(x.path)+' / '+esc(x.kind),ciJSON(x.before),ciJSON(x.after)]))+(d.diffNext?ciButton('diff-more','更多差异'):'')+'<div class="actions">'+ciButton('export','导出完整本人来源与诊断（私有）')+ciButton('reload','刷新原作业')+'</div>'+(j.resultVersionId?'<p>已保存草稿版本：'+esc(j.resultVersionId)+'。继续使用下方既有人工审核流程。</p>':'<label class="inline-check"><input id="ci-confirm" type="checkbox">我已核对来源、全部诊断与差异，确认仅创建待审核草稿</label>'+ciButton('apply','确认保存独立草稿'))+(d.receipt?'<h4>实际回执</h4>'+ciJSON(d.receipt):'')+'</article>':'')+
 '<form id="ci-activation"><h3>激活或回滚卡组指针</h3><p>仅切换到同环境、已发布且当前仍符合规则及素材条件的版本。保留原历史和抽取次数，不自动开启生产。</p><label>环境<select name="environment"><option value="sandbox">sandbox</option><option value="production">production</option></select></label><label>目标版本ID（留空为停止使用）<input name="targetVersionId" maxlength="100"></label><label>操作<select name="kind"><option value="activate">激活 / 停用</option><option value="rollback">回滚到合格旧版</option></select></label><label>操作原因<textarea name="reason" maxlength="2000" required></textarea></label><button class="btn secondary">核对切换条件</button></form>'+(s.activation?'<div data-ci-activation>'+ciJSON(s.activation.preview)+'<label class="inline-check"><input id="ci-activation-confirm" type="checkbox">确认按上方冻结修订与原因切换</label>'+ciButton('activate','确认切换')+'</div>':'');
 host.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea').forEach(e=>{if(s.busy)e.disabled=true;});
}
function ciDetail(s,d,append){if(!ciCurrent(s))return;if(!append){s.issues=d.issues||[];s.diff=d.diff||[];s.detail=d;}else{if(append==='issues')s.issues.push(...d.issues||[]);if(append==='diff')s.diff.push(...d.diff||[]);s.detail={...s.detail,...d,issuesNext:append==='issues'?d.issuesNext:s.detail.issuesNext,diffNext:append==='diff'?d.diffNext:s.detail.diffNext,issues:s.issues,diff:s.diff};}}
const ciOldRender=renderCardServiceAdmin;renderCardServiceAdmin=function(){ciOldRender();ciRender();};
const ciOldInvalidate=invalidateAuth;invalidateAuth=function(...args){ciRuntime=null;csAdmin.draft=null;csAdmin.data=null;document.getElementById('ci-admin')?.remove();return ciOldInvalidate(...args);};
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target.id!=='ci-file'||!csAdminEventActive(e.target))return;const s=ciState();if(s.busy)return;s.file=e.target.files?.[0]||null;ciRender();},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',e=>{if(e.target.id!=='ci-activation')return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!csAdminEventActive(e.target))return;const s=ciState(),f=new FormData(e.target),body={environment:String(f.get('environment')),targetVersionId:String(f.get('targetVersionId')).trim(),revision:csAdmin.data.config.revision},kind=String(f.get('kind')),reason=String(f.get('reason'));ciWork(s,async()=>{const preview=await ciCall(s,'activation/preview',body);if(ciCurrent(s))s.activation={body:{...body,kind,reason},preview};});},true);
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-ci]');if(!b)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!csAdminEventActive(b))return;const s=ciState(),action=b.dataset.ci,confirmed=document.getElementById('ci-confirm')?.checked,activationConfirmed=document.getElementById('ci-activation-confirm')?.checked;ciWork(s,async()=>{
 if(action==='preview'){if(!s.file||s.file.size<1||s.file.size>262144)throw Error('请选择大于0且不超过256 KiB的JSON文件。');const bytes=await s.file.arrayBuffer(),sha=Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes)),x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');if(!ciCurrent(s))return;const response=await fetch('/api/admin/cards/imports/preview',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-EW-Request-ID':ciKey(s,'preview:'+sha)},body:bytes});const d=await response.json();if(!ciCurrent(s))return;if(response.status===401){requireLogin();return;}if(response.status===403){ciRuntime=null;csAdmin.data=null;csAdmin.draft=null;reconcileAuth();return;}if(!response.ok)throw Object.assign(Error(d.error||'预览失败'),{status:response.status});ciDetail(s,d);}
 else if(action==='jobs'||action==='jobs-more'){const d=await ciCall(s,'imports'+(action==='jobs-more'?'?before='+encodeURIComponent(s.jobsNext):''));if(ciCurrent(s)){s.jobs=action==='jobs'?d.items:s.jobs.concat(d.items);s.jobsNext=d.next;}}
 else if(action==='open'||action==='reload'){const id=action==='open'?b.dataset.id:s.detail.job.id;ciDetail(s,await ciCall(s,'imports/'+encodeURIComponent(id)));}
 else if(action==='issues-more'||action==='diff-more'){const kind=action==='issues-more'?'issues':'diff',cursor=kind==='issues'?s.detail.issuesNext:s.detail.diffNext;ciDetail(s,await ciCall(s,'imports/'+encodeURIComponent(s.detail.job.id)+'?'+kind+'Before='+encodeURIComponent(cursor)),kind);}
 else if(action==='export')await ciExport(s);
 else if(action==='apply'){if(!confirmed)throw Error('请先核对预览并勾选确认。');const j=s.detail.job,r=await ciCall(s,'imports/'+encodeURIComponent(j.id)+'/apply',{requestId:ciKey(s,'apply:'+j.id+':'+j.version),confirmed:true,version:j.version});if(!ciCurrent(s))return;ciDetail(s,await ciCall(s,'imports/'+encodeURIComponent(j.id)));s.message='已创建待审核草稿，未发布。';csAdmin.data=await api('admin/cards');}
 else if(action==='activate'){if(!s.activation||!activationConfirmed)throw Error('请先核对切换条件并勾选确认。');if(!s.activation.preview.eligible)throw Error('当前条件不允许切换。');const body=s.activation.body,r=await ciCall(s,'activation/apply',{...body,requestId:ciKey(s,'activation:'+JSON.stringify(body)),confirmed:true});if(!ciCurrent(s))return;s.message='指针已切换，原历史与生产开关保留。';s.activation={body,preview:r};csAdmin.data=await api('admin/cards');}
 });},true);
const ciBaseRender=ciRender;ciRender=function(){ciBaseRender();const s=ciState(),host=document.getElementById('ci-admin');if(!host||!ciCurrent(s))return;if(s.activationDraft)for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(s.activationDraft)){const field=host.querySelector('#ci-activation [name="'+key+'"]');if(field)field.value=value;}host.querySelectorAll('button,input,textarea,select').forEach(x=>{if(s.busy)x.disabled=true;});};

// Independent administrator module. Server enforces all business/finance/support grants.
labels.commission='分佣沙箱';
let cmaRuntime={epoch:-1,data:null,busy:false,error:'',editing:null,filters:{},filterValues:{}};
function cmaState(){if(cmaRuntime.epoch!==state.authEpoch)cmaRuntime={epoch:state.authEpoch,data:null,busy:false,error:'',editing:null,filters:{},filterValues:{}};return cmaRuntime;}
function cmaField(name,label,value='',type='text',required=true){return `<label>${label}<input name="${name}" type="${type}" value="${esc(value??'')}" maxlength="2000" ${required?'required':''}></label>`;}
function cmaForm(kind,content,button){return `<form data-cma-form="${kind}" data-cma-epoch="${state.authEpoch}" class="grid-form">${content}<div class="actions wide"><button class="btn">${button}</button></div></form>`;}
function cmaMoney(n,currency){if(n===null||n===undefined)return '待费用确认';try{const f=new Intl.NumberFormat('zh-CN',{style:'currency',currency});return f.format(n/10**f.resolvedOptions().maximumFractionDigits);}catch{return String(n)+' '+currency;}}
const cmaStatuses={waiting_facts:'待费用确认',pending_confirmation:'待确认',frozen:'冻结中',payable:'可结算',statement_pending:'结算单处理中',settled_bookkeeping:'已结算记账（未转账）',revoked:'已撤销',awaiting_approval:'待另一财务审批',approved:'已审批（未记账）',booked:'已记账（未转账）',cancelled:'已取消',pending_recovery:'待抵扣/追偿',applied:'冲正已记账',reserved:'待抵扣结算',offset_booked:'抵扣已记账'};
function cmaStatus(v){return cmaStatuses[v]||v;}
function cmaRuleEditor(){const r=cmaState().editing||{},basis=r.basis||{};return cmaForm('rule',`${cmaField('ruleId','规则编号（新建时留空）',r.ruleId,'text',false)}<input name="expectedVersion" type="hidden" value="${r.version||0}">${cmaField('productId','已有沙箱商品编号',r.productId)}<label>分成类型<select name="kind"><option value="author" ${r.kind!=='promotion'?'selected':''}>作者作品</option><option function cmaRender(){const r=cmaState(),d=r.data;if(state.nav!=='commission'||!d)return;const permission=d.permission,admin=permission==='admin',business=admin||permission==='business',finance=admin||permission==='finance',epoch=state.authEpoch;
