 const f=r.filterValues||{};content+='<details class="panel"><summary>按作品、受益人及成交周期筛选</summary>'+cmaForm('filters',`${cmaField('beneficiary','老师/推广受益人ID',f.beneficiary,'text',false)}${cmaField('productId','商品ID',f.productId,'text',false)}${cmaField('contentId','作品内容ID',f.contentId,'text',false)}${cmaField('currency','币种',f.currency,'text',false)}<label>分成类型<select name="kind"><option value="">全部</option><option value="author" ${f.kind==='author'?'selected':''}>作者</option><option value="promotion" ${f.kind==='promotion'?'selected':''}>推广</option></select></label>${cmaField('from','成交起始日（UTC）',f.from,'date',false)}${cmaField('to','成交结束日（UTC）',f.to,'date',false)}<p class="hint wide">台账和冲正按原佣金成交周期筛选；命中的结算单显示完整原单总额，不当作筛选作品的小计。</p>`,'查询')+'<button class="btn secondary" data-cma-action="clear-filters" data-cma-epoch="'+epoch+'">清除筛选</button></details>';
 if(admin)content+='<details class="panel"><summary>沙箱开关与权限</summary>'+cmaForm('settings',`<label><input name="sandboxEnabled" type="checkbox" ${d.settings.sandboxEnabled?'checked':''}>开启沙箱规则与记账</label><label>作者/推广并行策略<select name="stackingPolicy">${[['exclusive_promotion','推广优先（单类）'],['exclusive_author','作者优先（单类）'],['allow_both','允许两类共存']].map(([v,l])=>`<option value="${v}" ${d.settings.stackingPolicy===v?'selected':''}>${l}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>同优先级归因<select name="attributionStrategy"><option value="last_touch" ${d.settings.attributionStrategy==='last_touch'?'selected':''}>最后有效归因</option><option value="first_touch" ${d.settings.attributionStrategy==='first_touch'?'selected':''}>最早有效归因</option></select></label>`,'保存沙箱设置')+cmaForm('grant',`${cmaField('userId','现有后台账号ID（admin/operator/reviewer）')}<label>分佣权限<select name="permission"><option value="business">业务：规则/邀请码</option><option value="finance">财务：事实/确认/结算</option><option value="support">客服：处理状态只读</option><option value="none">撤回专项权限</option></select></label>`,'保存专项授权')+table(['账号','权限'],(d.grants||[]).map(g=>[esc(g.user_id),esc(g.permission)]))+'</details>';
 if(business)content+='<details class="panel"><summary>版本规则与邀请码</summary>'+table(['规则','版本','商品','类型','状态','操作'],(d.rules||[]).map(v=>[esc(v.ruleId),String(v.version),esc(v.productId),v.kind==='author'?'作者':'推广',esc(v.status),`<button class="btn secondary small" data-cma-action="edit-rule" data-id="${esc(v.ruleId)}" data-version="${v.version}" data-cma-epoch="${epoch}">以此追加</button>`]))+'<button class="btn secondary" data-cma-action="new-rule" data-cma-epoch="'+epoch+'">新规则</button>'+cmaRuleEditor()+cmaForm('invite',`${cmaField('code','邀请码（4–64位字母数字等）')}${cmaField('beneficiary','已有受益人账号ID')}${cmaField('priority','归因优先级（0–1000）',0,'number')}${cmaField('days','有效天数',30,'number')}`,'创建新邀请码')+table(['邀请码','受益人','优先级','状态','操作'],(d.invites||[]).map(v=>[esc(v.code),esc(v.beneficiary),String(v.priority),esc(v.status),v.status==='active'?`<button class="btn danger small" data-cma-action="revoke-invite" data-id="${esc(v.code)}" data-cma-epoch="${epoch}">撤销后续归因</button>`:'']))+'</details>';
 content+='<section class="panel"><h2>订单项台账</h2>'+(finance?'<button class="btn secondary" data-cma-action="advance" data-cma-epoch="'+epoch+'">检查履约与冻结到期</button>':'')+table(['订单/项','受益人/规则','币种/原额/余额','状态','操作'],d.items.map(v=>[esc(v.orderId)+'<br>'+esc(v.itemId),esc(v.beneficiary)+'<br>'+esc(v.ruleId)+' v'+v.ruleVersion,esc(v.currency)+'<br>'+esc(cmaMoney(v.amountMinor,v.currency))+' / '+esc(cmaMoney(v.netMinor,v.currency)),esc(cmaStatus(v.status))+(v.reason?'<br>'+esc(v.reason):''),finance&&v.status==='pending_confirmation'?`<button class="btn secondary small" data-cma-action="confirm" data-id="${esc(v.id)}" data-cma-epoch="${epoch}">确认并冻结</button>`:'']))+'</section>';
 if(finance)content+='<details class="panel"><summary>费用事实与幂等补账</summary>'+cmaForm('facts',`${cmaField('itemId','已有订单项ID')}${cmaField('channelFeeMinor','渠道费最小币种单位（留空未知；确认0才填0）','','number',false)}<label class="wide">事实来源<textarea name="source" maxlength="2000" required></textarea></label><label class="wide"><input name="isTestData" type="checkbox" required>确认仅登记沙箱费用事实，非真实支付费率</label>`,'补录并重新核对')+cmaForm('sync',cmaField('orderId','既有统一交易订单ID'),'幂等同步付款/退款')+'</details><details class="panel"><summary>创建结算单（先确认冻结并检查到期）</summary>'+cmaForm('statement',`${cmaField('beneficiary','受益人账号ID')}${cmaField('currency','币种','USD')}${cmaField('from','周期起始日期','','date')}${cmaField('to','周期结束日期','','date')}<p class="hint wide">同币种可结算项自动纳入；待追偿调整自动抵扣，净额未达最低额不生成。创建人与审批人必须分开。</p>`,'生成待审批结算单')+'</details>';
 content+='<section class="panel"><h2>结算单与退款调整</h2>'+table(['结算单/受益人','金额','状态','操作'],d.statements.map(s=>[esc(s.id)+'<br>'+esc(s.beneficiary||''),s.total_minor===undefined?'—':esc(cmaMoney(s.total_minor,s.currency)),esc(cmaStatus(s.status)),finance&&['awaiting_approval','approved'].includes(s.status)?`${s.status==='awaiting_approval'?`<button class="btn secondary small" data-cma-action="approve" data-id="${esc(s.id)}" data-version="${s.version}" data-cma-epoch="${epoch}">另一财务审批</button>`:`<button class="btn secondary small" data-cma-action="book" data-id="${esc(s.id)}" data-version="${s.version}" data-cma-epoch="${epoch}">仅结算记账</button>`}<button class="btn danger small" data-cma-action="cancel" data-id="${esc(s.id)}" data-version="${s.version}" data-cma-epoch="${epoch}">取消</button>`:'']))+table(['调整','金额','处理状态'],d.adjustments.map(v=>[esc(v.id),v.amount_minor===undefined?'—':esc(cmaMoney(v.amount_minor,v.currency)),esc(cmaStatus(v.status))]))+'<details><summary>结算行追溯</summary><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere">'+esc(JSON.stringify(d.statements.map(s=>({id:s.id,lines:s.lines||[]})),null,2))+'</pre></details></section>';
 if(finance)content+='<details class="panel"><summary>测试付款凭证（与结算记账分开）</summary><p class="hint">只保存测试收款引用。真实付款关闭，proofVerified始终false，状态始终“测试已登记”，不能标已打款。不要填真实银行卡/证件或凭据。</p>'+cmaForm('payment',`${cmaField('statementId','已记账结算单ID')}${cmaField('name','测试收款人名称')}${cmaField('country','国家两位代码','US')}${cmaField('currency','结算币种','USD')}${cmaField('channel','测试渠道名称')}${cmaField('accountReference','测试收款引用（不填真实账号）')}${cmaField('proofReference','测试凭证引用（非真实转账证明）')}<label class="wide"><input name="isTestData" type="checkbox" required>确认全部为沙箱测试资料，未发生真实转账</label>`,'单列登记测试凭证')+table(['结算单','测试凭证','状态'],(d.payments||[]).map(v=>[esc(v.statementId),esc(v.proofReference),'测试登记 · 未核验 · 未转账']))+'</details>';
 document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=content;document.querySelectorAll('[data-cma-form] button,[data-cma-action]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=r.busy);
}
function cmaQuery(){const values=cmaState().filters;const query=new URLSearchParams(values).toString();return query?'?'+query:'';}
const cmaLoadBefore=load;
load=async function(nav){if(nav!=='commission')return cmaLoadBefore(nav);if(!state.user||!document.getElementById('main'))return false;const r=cmaState(),epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=++state.seq;state.nav=nav;state.editing=null;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('分佣沙箱')+'<p class="hint">正在读取…</p>';try{const d=await api('admin/commissions'+cmaQuery());if(!authCurrent(epoch)||state.seq!==seq||cmaRuntime!==r||state.nav!==nav)return false;r.data=d;r.error='';cmaRender();return true;}catch(e){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&state.seq===seq&&state.nav===nav&&!e.handled)document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('分佣沙箱')+'<p class="error">'+esc(e.message)+'</p><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="commission">重试</button>';return false;}};
async function cmaWrite(path,body,method='POST'){const r=cmaState(),epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=state.seq;if(r.busy||!state.user||state.nav!=='commission')return;const active=()=>authCurrent(epoch)&&state.seq===seq&&cmaRuntime===r&&state.nav==='commission';r.busy=true;r.error='';document.querySelectorAll('[data-cma-form] button,[data-cma-action]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);try{const saved=await api('admin/commissions/'+path,body,method);if(!active())return;if(saved.rule)r.editing=saved.rule;r.busy=false;const fresh=await api('admin/commissions'+cmaQuery());if(!active())return;r.data=fresh;r.error='';cmaRender();toast('沙箱记录已保存，未发生真实转账。');}catch(e){if(active()&&!e.handled){r.error=e.message;r.busy=false;const out=document.querySelector('#main .error[role=alert]');if(out)out.textContent=e.message;}}finally{if(authCurrent(epoch)&&cmaRuntime===r){r.busy=false;if(active())document.querySelectorAll('[data-cma-form] button,[data-cma-action]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);}}}
document.addEventListener('click',async event=>{const b=event.target.closest('[data-cma-action]');if(!b)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const r=cmaState();if(!state.user||r.busy||Number(b.dataset.cmaEpoch)!==state.authEpoch||state.nav!=='commission')return;const action=b.dataset.cmaAction;if(action==='clear-filters'){r.filters={};r.filterValues={};load('commission');return;}if(action==='edit-rule'){r.editing=r.data.rules.find(v=>v.ruleId===b.dataset.id&&v.version===Number(b.dataset.version));cmaRender();return;}if(action==='new-rule'){r.editing=null;cmaRender();return;}if(action==='export'){const epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=state.seq;try{const d=await api('admin/commissions/export'+cmaQuery());if(!authCurrent(epoch)||state.seq!==seq||state.nav!=='commission')return;const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(['\uFEFF'+d.csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='sandbox-commission-ledger.csv';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}catch(e){if(authCurrent(epoch)&&state.seq===seq){r.error=e.message;cmaRender();}}return;}if(action==='advance')return cmaWrite('advance',{});if(action==='confirm')return cmaWrite('confirm',{ledgerId:b.dataset.id});if(action==='revoke-invite')return cmaWrite('invites',{code:b.dataset.id,status:'revoked'});if(['approve','book','cancel'].includes(action))return cmaWrite('statements/'+encodeURIComponent(b.dataset.id)+'/'+action,{expectedVersion:Number(b.dataset.version)});},true);
document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{const form=event.target;if(!form.hasAttribute('data-cma-form'))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(Number(form.dataset.cmaEpoch)!==state.authEpoch||state.nav!=='commission'||cmaState().busy)return;const kind=form.dataset.cmaForm,v=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),num=k=>Number(v[k]),check=k=>v[k]==='on';
 if(kind==='filters'){const r=cmaState();r.filterValues=v;r.filters=Object.fromEntries(['beneficiary','productId','contentId','kind','currency'].filter(k=>String(v[k]||'').trim()).map(k=>[k,k==='currency'?String(v[k]).trim().toUpperCase():String(v[k]).trim()]));if(v.from)r.filters.from=String(Date.parse(v.from+'T00:00:00.000Z'));if(v.to)r.filters.to=String(Date.parse(v.to+'T23:59:59.999Z'));load('commission');return;}
 if(kind==='settings')return cmaWrite('settings',{sandboxEnabled:check('sandboxEnabled'),stackingPolicy:v.stackingPolicy,attributionStrategy:v.attributionStrategy,productionEnabled:false,actualPaymentsEnabled:false},'PATCH');
 if(kind==='grant')return cmaWrite('grants',{userId:v.userId,permission:v.permission});
 if(kind==='rule')return cmaWrite('rules',{...(String(v.ruleId||'').trim()?{ruleId:v.ruleId}:{}),expectedVersion:num('expectedVersion'),productId:v.productId,kind:v.kind,beneficiary:v.beneficiary,currency:v.currency,mode:v.mode,rateBps:num('rateBps'),fixedMinor:num('fixedMinor'),rounding:v.rounding,capMinor:v.capMinor===''?null:num('capMinor'),freezeDays:num('freezeDays'),minimumSettlementMinor:num('minimumSettlementMinor'),status:v.status,note:v.note,basis:Object.fromEntries(['deductTax','deductShipping','deductDiscount','deductChannelFee'].map(k=>[k,check(k)]))});
 if(kind==='invite')return cmaWrite('invites',{code:v.code,beneficiary:v.beneficiary,priority:num('priority'),validUntil:Date.now()+num('days')*86400000});
 if(kind==='facts')return cmaWrite('facts',{itemId:v.itemId,channelFeeMinor:v.channelFeeMinor===''?null:num('channelFeeMinor'),discountMinor:0,source:v.source,isTestData:check('isTestData')});
 if(kind==='sync')return cmaWrite('orders/'+encodeURIComponent(v.orderId)+'/sync',{});
 if(kind==='statement')return cmaWrite('statements',{beneficiary:v.beneficiary,currency:v.currency,from:Date.parse(v.from+'T00:00:00.000Z'),to:Math.min(Date.now(),Date.parse(v.to+'T23:59:59.999Z'))});
 if(kind==='payment')return cmaWrite('statements/'+encodeURIComponent(v.statementId)+'/payment',{requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),isTestData:check('isTestData'),recipient:Object.fromEntries(['name','country','currency','channel','accountReference'].map(k=>[k,v[k]])),proofReference:v.proofReference});
},true);

// Optional admin module. Call AfterSalesAdmin.install() from the existing admin build after account/commerce modules.
(function(root){
 'use strict';
 let installed=false,runtime=null;
 function get(){if(!runtime||runtime.epoch!==state.authEpoch)runtime={epoch:state.authEpoch,caseId:'',data:null,detail:null,busy:false,loading:false,error:'',keys:{},retry:null};return runtime;}
 function active(r,seq){return runtime===r&&authCurrent(r.epoch)&&state.nav==='afterSales'&&(seq===undefined||seq===state.seq);}
 const label=v=>({submitted:'待受理',accepted:'已受理',rejected:'已驳回',returning:'已登记退货物流',partially_received:'部分实收',received:'已完成收货验收'})[v]||v;
 const price=(n,c)=>typeof coAdminPrice==='function'?coAdminPrice(n,c):esc(String(n)+' '+c);
 function draw(){
  const r=get();if(state.nav!=='afterSales'||!state.user)return;const d=r.data,detail=r.detail,disabled=r.busy?'disabled':'';
  let html=heading('售后受理与退货验收','<button class="btn secondary" data-af-admin="refresh" '+disabled+'>重新查询</button>')+
   '<section class="panel"><h2>独立沙箱流程</h2><p class="hint">本模块不收款、不执行退款，不将受理/物流/验收当作资金到账。真实生产售后、承运商核验和退换货条款尚未启用。只接受统一沙箱已付款原单，原资金事实继续在统一交易模块记录。</p>'+
   (r.error?'<p class="error" role="alert">'+esc(r.error)+'</p>':'')+(r.retry?'<button class="btn secondary" data-af-admin="retry" '+disabled+'>同编号重试上次操作</button>':'')+'</section>';
  if(!d){document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=html+'<p>'+(r.loading?'正在读取…':'尚未读取到有权查看的售后资料。')+'</p>';return;}
  html+='<section class="panel"><h2>测试配置</h2><form data-af-admin-form="settings"><fieldset '+disabled+'><label class="inline-check"><input type="checkbox" name="sandboxEnabled" '+(d.settings.sandboxEnabled?'checked':'')+'>开启售后沙箱（统一交易沙箱也须开启）</label><label>明确测试规则版本<input name="ruleVersion" maxlength="120" value="'+esc(d.settings.ruleVersion||'')+'"></label><p class="hint">正式开关始终关闭；版本只是测试流程版本，不是负责人已审核的正式售后政策。</p><button class="btn">保存测试配置</button></fieldset></form></section>';
  html+='<section class="panel"><h2>申请列表</h2>'+table(['申请/原订单','账号','类型/状态','数量','操作'],d.items.map(c=>[esc(c.id)+'<br><small>'+esc(c.orderId)+'</small>',esc(c.ownerId),esc(c.type)+' / '+esc(label(c.status)),c.items.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.quantity,0),'<button class="btn secondary small" data-af-admin="open" data-id="'+esc(c.id)+'" '+disabled+'>查看</button>']))+'</section>';
  if(detail){
   const c=detail,m=c.money,canDecide=c.status==='submitted',canReceive=c.type==='return'&&['accepted','returning','partially_received'].includes(c.status)&&c.shipments.length>0&&d.settings.sandboxEnabled;
   html+='<section class="panel" data-af-admin-detail="'+esc(c.id)+'"><h2>'+esc(label(c.status))+'</h2><p>'+esc(c.reason)+'</p><p class="hint">原订单 '+esc(c.orderId)+' · 测试规则 '+esc(c.ruleVersion)+' · 版本 '+c.version+'</p>'+
    table(['订单项','类型','申请','实收','已入库','原项累计退款数量'],c.items.map(x=>[esc(x.snapshot.title)+'<br><small>'+esc(x.itemId)+'</small>',esc(x.type),x.quantity,x.receivedQuantity,x.restockedQuantity,x.orderItemRefundedQuantity]))+
    '<div class="hint"><p>资金独立：'+esc(m.orderPaymentStatus||'—')+'；原订单累计沙箱退款 '+price(m.orderRefundedMinor||0,m.currency||'USD')+'</p><p>该累计事实未逐笔绑定本申请，不表示本申请已退款或真实到账。此页不调用退款接口。</p></div>';
   if(canDecide)html+='<form data-af-admin-form="decision" data-case="'+esc(c.id)+'"><fieldset '+disabled+'><legend>受理或驳回</legend><label>决定<select name="decision" required><option value="">请选择</option><option value="accept">受理申请（不自动退款）</option><option value="reject">驳回申请</option></select></label><label>向申请人显示的理由<textarea name="reason" maxlength="2000" required></textarea></label><button class="btn">记录决定</button></fieldset></form>';
   html+='<h3>退货物流登记</h3>'+(c.shipments.length?table(['承运商','物流单号','登记日期','核验'],c.shipments.map(x=>[esc(x.carrier),esc(x.trackingNumber),esc(new Date(x.created).toISOString()),'用户登记，未接承运商核验'])):'<p class="hint">尚无物流登记；数字和会员申请不接受物流或仓库验收。</p>');
   if(canReceive){
    const lines=c.items.map(x=>{const left=x.quantity-x.receivedQuantity;return '<fieldset class="af-admin-receipt-item" '+(!left||r.busy?'disabled':'')+'><legend>'+esc(x.snapshot.title)+'</legend><p class="hint">本申请尚可登记实收 '+left+' 件。可入库数量必须由本次验收明确填写，默认0。</p><label>本次实际收到数量<input type="number" name="received:'+esc(x.id)+'" min="0" max="'+left+'" step="1" value="0"></label><label>本次可重新入库数量<input type="number" name="restock:'+esc(x.id)+'" min="0" max="'+left+'" step="1" value="0"></label><label>本次物品状态/判断依据<input name="condition:'+esc(x.id)+'" maxlength="200"></label></fieldset>';}).join('');
    html+='<form data-af-admin-form="inspect" data-case="'+esc(c.id)+'"><fieldset '+disabled+'><legend>收货验收与明确入库</legend><label>唯一收货批次/验收单号<input name="receiptReference" maxlength="100" required></label><p class="hint">同一原订单的该单号只能使用一次（跨申请也不重复）；网络不确定时用“同编号重试”，不能换编号重复登记同批物品。登记不代表真实承运核验。</p>'+lines+'<label>验收说明<textarea name="note" maxlength="2000" required></textarea></label><button class="btn">记录实收及明确入库数量</button></fieldset></form>';
   }
   html+='<h3>验收记录</h3>'+table(['验收单号','说明','数量/入库/状态','库存事件'],c.inspections.flatMap(x=>x.items.map(i=>[esc(x.receiptReference),esc(x.note),i.receivedQuantity+' / '+i.restockQuantity+' / '+esc(i.condition),esc(i.inventoryEventId)])))+
    '<details><summary>完整操作记录</summary>'+table(['日期','操作','说明'],c.history.map(x=>[esc(new Date(x.created).toISOString()),esc(x.kind),esc(x.data.reason||x.data.note||'')]))+'</details></section>';
  }
  document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=html;
 }
 async function loadHere(){
  const r=get();if(!state.user)return false;if(r.busy){draw();return false;}const seq=++state.seq;r.loading=true;r.error='';
  try{const jobs=[api('admin/after-sales?environment=sandbox')];if(r.caseId)jobs.push(api('admin/after-sales/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(r.caseId)));const result=await Promise.all(jobs);if(!active(r,seq))return false;r.data=result[0];r.detail=r.caseId?result[1]:null;return true;}
  catch(e){if(active(r,seq)){r.error=e.message;if(e.status===401||e.status===403){r.data=null;r.detail=null;r.retry=null;}}return false;}
  finally{if(active(r,seq)){r.loading=false;draw();}}
 }
 function requestKey(r,path,payload){const k=JSON.stringify([path,payload]);return r.keys[k]||(r.keys[k]=crypto.randomUUID());}
 async function mutate(path,payload,method='POST'){
  const r=get();if(r.busy)return;const seq=++state.seq;r.loading=false;r.busy=true;r.error='';r.mutationSeq=seq;let handled=false;
  const body=method==='POST'?{...payload,requestId:payload.requestId||requestKey(r,path,payload)}:payload;r.retry={path,body,method};draw();
  try{await api(path,body,method);if(!active(r,seq))return;handled=true;r.busy=false;r.retry=null;await loadHere();}
  catch(e){if(active(r,seq)){handled=true;r.busy=false;r.error=e.message;if(e.status===401||e.status===403){r.data=null;r.detail=null;r.retry=null;}draw();}}
  finally{if(runtime===r&&authCurrent(r.epoch)&&r.mutationSeq===seq){r.busy=false;if(!handled&&state.nav==='afterSales')void loadHere();}}
 }
 function install(){
  if(installed)return;if(typeof load!=='function'||typeof api!=='function'||typeof labels!=='object')throw Error('Load existing admin framework first');installed=true;labels.afterSales='售后受理与退货验收';
   if(typeof allowedNavs==='function'){const priorAllowed=allowedNavs;allowedNavs=function(user=state.user){const items=priorAllowed(user).filter(k=>k!=='afterSales');if(user?.role==='admin')items.push('afterSales');return items;};}
  const previousLoad=load;load=async function(nav){if(nav!=='afterSales')return previousLoad(nav);state.nav=nav;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));return loadHere();};
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
   const b=event.target.closest?.('[data-af-admin]');if(!b)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const r=get();if(r.busy)return;
   if(b.dataset.afAdmin==='refresh')void loadHere();
   if(b.dataset.afAdmin==='open'){r.caseId=b.dataset.id;r.detail=null;r.retry=null;void loadHere();}
   if(b.dataset.afAdmin==='retry'&&r.retry)void mutate(r.retry.path,r.retry.body,r.retry.method);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{
   const form=event.target.closest?.('[data-af-admin-form]');if(!form)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const r=get();if(r.busy||state.nav!=='afterSales'||!form.reportValidity())return;const f=new FormData(form),kind=form.dataset.afAdminForm;
   if(kind==='settings'){void mutate('admin/after-sales/settings',{sandboxEnabled:f.get('sandboxEnabled')==='on',productionEnabled:false,ruleVersion:String(f.get('ruleVersion')||'').trim()||null},'PATCH');return;}
   const c=r.detail;if(!c||c.id!==form.dataset.case)return;
   if(kind==='decision')void mutate('admin/after-sales/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(c.id)+'/decision',{version:c.version,decision:String(f.get('decision')),reason:String(f.get('reason'))});
   if(kind==='inspect'){
    const items=[];
    for(const x of c.items){
     const receivedQuantity=Number(f.get('received:'+x.id)||0),restockQuantity=Number(f.get('restock:'+x.id)||0);
     if(!receivedQuantity&&!restockQuantity)continue;
     const condition=String(f.get('condition:'+x.id)||'').trim();
     if(!Number.isSafeInteger(receivedQuantity)||receivedQuantity<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(restockQuantity)||restockQuantity<0||restockQuantity>receivedQuantity||!condition){r.error='请逐项填写实际实收、明确入库数量及状态依据；不可自动全入库。';draw();return;}
     items.push({caseItemId:x.id,receivedQuantity,restockQuantity,condition});
    }
    if(!items.length){r.error='请至少填写一项本次实际收到的数量。';draw();return;}
    void mutate('admin/after-sales/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(c.id)+'/inspect',{version:c.version,receiptReference:String(f.get('receiptReference')),items,note:String(f.get('note'))});
   }
  },true);
 }
 root.AfterSalesAdmin=Object.freeze({install,openCase(id){const r=get();r.caseId=String(id);r.detail=null;r.retry=null;return load('afterSales');}});
})(typeof globalThis==='object'?globalThis:this);


AfterSalesAdmin.install();

 function get(){if(!runtime||runtime.epoch!==state.authEpoch||runtime.actor!==state.user?.id)runtime={epoch:state.authEpoch,actor:state.user?.id,locale:'zh',seq:0,busy:false,loading:false,error:'',data:null,plan:null,drafts:{},keys:{},retry:null};return runtime;}
 function t(k){const s=get(),index={en:0,zh:1,'zh-Hant':2,bo:3}[s.locale]??1;return words[k]?.[index]||k;}
 const tr=(en,zh,hant,bo)=>[en,zh,hant,bo][{en:0,zh:1,'zh-Hant':2,bo:3}[get().locale]??1];
 function active(s,seq){return runtime===s&&authCurrent(s.epoch)&&state.user?.id===s.actor&&state.user?.role==='admin'&&['settings',nav].includes(state.nav)&&(seq===undefined||s.seq===seq);}
 function scoped(s){return 'data-sr-epoch="'+s.epoch+'" data-sr-actor="'+esc(s.actor)+'" lang="'+esc(s.locale)+'"';}
 function eventCurrent(node){const f=node.closest?.('[data-sr-epoch]'),s=get();return !!f&&active(s)&&Number(f.dataset.srEpoch)===s.epoch&&f.dataset.srActor===s.actor;}
 function capture(form){get().drafts[form.id]=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form).entries());}
 function val(form,k,fallback=''){const d=get().drafts[form];return esc(d&&Object.hasOwn(d,k)?d[k]:fallback);}

 function status(c){return c.unmigrated?t('legacy'):c.refReady?t('ready'):c.hasCredential?t('missing'):t('empty');}

 function settingsPage(){const s=get();if(!active(s)||state.nav!=='settings')return;const d=state.settings,a=d?.ai;if(!a)return;const disabled=s.busy||s.loading?'disabled':'',deviceLegacy=Object.values(d.integrations||{}).some(c=>c.unmigrated);

  html+='<section class="panel"><h2>'+tr('Device references','设备凭据引用','設備憑據引用','སྒྲིག་ཆས་ཐོ་སྦྱོར།')+'</h2>'+(deviceLegacy?'<p class="hint" data-sr-group-block>'+deviceGroupNotice()+'</p>':'')+'<form id="sr-device-form"><fieldset '+(s.busy||s.loading||deviceLegacy?'disabled':'')+'><label>'+tr('Provider ID','供应商编号','供應商編號','ཞབས་ཞུ་ID')+'<input name="provider" required pattern="[A-Za-z0-9_-]+" value="'+val('sr-device-form','provider')+'"></label><label>EW_DEVICE_… · '+tr('reference name only; blank disables','仅引用名；空白停用','僅引用名；空白停用','ཐོ་སྦྱོར་མིང་ཙམ།')+'<input name="secretRef" autocomplete="off" value="'+val('sr-device-form','secretRef')+'"></label><label>'+tr('Field map JSON','字段映射JSON','欄位對應JSON','ཞིང་གི་ཐོ་JSON')+'<textarea name="fieldMap">'+val('sr-device-form','fieldMap','{}')+'</textarea></label><button class="btn">'+t('save')+'</button></fieldset></form>'+table([tr('ID','编号','編號','ID'),tr('Status','状态','狀態','གནས་སྟངས།'),tr('Reference','引用','引用','ཐོ་སྦྱོར།'),''],Object.entries(d.integrations||{}).map(([k,c])=>[esc(k),status(c),esc(c.secretRef||'—'),'<button class="btn secondary" data-sr-edit="'+esc(k)+'" '+(deviceLegacy?'disabled':'')+'> '+tr('Edit','编辑','編輯','བཅོས།')+'</button>']))+'</section></div>';document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=html;
 }
 function plansPage(){const s=get();if(!active(s))return;const d=s.data,p=s.plan,disabled=s.busy||s.loading?'disabled':'';let html=head()+'<section class="panel">'+(s.loading?'<p role="status">'+tr('Loading…','正在读取…','正在讀取…','ཀློག་བཞིན།')+'</p>':'');if(d){html+='<p>'+tr('Configuration version','配置版本','配置版本','སྒྲིག་གཞིའི་ཐོ།')+' '+d.revision+'</p><form id="sr-plan-form"><fieldset '+disabled+'>'+d.items.map((x,i)=>'<article><label><input type="checkbox" name="item" value="'+i+'" '+(!x.unmigrated?'disabled':s.drafts['sr-plan-form']?.['choose:'+i]?'checked':'')+'> '+esc(x.kind==='ai'?'AI':x.providerId)+' · '+status(x)+'</label><label>'+esc(x.kind==='ai'?'EW_AI_…':'EW_DEVICE_…')+'<input name="reference:'+i+'" autocomplete="off" value="'+val('sr-plan-form','reference:'+i,x.reference)+'" '+(!x.unmigrated?'disabled':'')+'></label></article>').join('')+'<button class="btn">'+t('preview')+'</button></fieldset></form>';}html+='</section>';
  if(p){html+='<section class="panel" data-sr-plan="'+esc(p.id)+'"><h2>'+t(p.status==='applied'?'applied':p.status==='prepared'?'prepared':'blocked')+'</h2><p>'+esc(p.id)+'</p>'+table([tr('Target','目标','目標','དམིགས།'),tr('Reference','引用','引用','ཐོ་སྦྱོར།'),tr('Status','状态','狀態','གནས་སྟངས།')],p.targets.map(x=>[esc(x.kind==='ai'?'AI':x.providerId),esc(x.reference),x.refReady?t('ready'):t('missing')]))+(p.status==='prepared'?'<form id="sr-apply-form" data-plan="'+esc(p.id)+'"><fieldset '+disabled+'><label><input type="checkbox" name="confirmed" required> '+t('confirm')+'</label><button class="btn">'+t('apply')+'</button></fieldset></form>':'')+'</section>';}
  document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=html+'</div>';
 }
 function draw(){if(state.nav==='settings')settingsPage();else if(state.nav===nav)plansPage();}
 async function refresh(){const s=get();if(!active(s)||s.busy||s.loading)return;++state.seq;const seq=++s.seq,page=state.nav;s.loading=true;s.error='';draw();try{const d=await api(page==='settings'?'admin/settings':'admin/secret-references');if(!active(s,seq)||state.nav!==page)return;if(page==='settings')state.settings=d;else s.data=d;}catch(e){if(active(s,seq))s.error=e.message;}finally{if(active(s,seq)&&state.nav===page){s.loading=false;draw();}}}
 async function mutate(path,payload){const s=get();if(!active(s)||s.busy||s.loading)return;++state.seq;const seq=++s.seq,page=state.nav,sig=JSON.stringify([path,payload]),body={...payload,requestId:payload.requestId||(s.keys[sig]||(s.keys[sig]=crypto.randomUUID()))};s.busy=true;s.error='';s.retry={path,body};draw();try{const d=await api(path,body,'POST');if(!active(s,seq)||state.nav!==page)return;s.retry=null;s.busy=false;s.drafts={};if(path==='admin/secret-references/plans')s.plan=d.item;else if(path.endsWith('/apply')){s.data=d.status;if(s.plan)s.plan={...s.plan,status:'applied'};}else if(page==='settings'){const latest=await api('admin/settings');if(!active(s,seq)||state.nav!==page)return;state.settings=latest;}if(active(s,seq)&&state.nav===page)draw();}catch(e){if(active(s,seq)&&state.nav===page){s.error=e.message;if([401,403].includes(e.status)){s.data=null;s.plan=null;s.retry=null;s.drafts={};}}}finally{if(runtime===s&&authCurrent(s.epoch)&&s.seq===seq){s.busy=false;if(active(s))draw();}}}
 function install(){if(installed)return;if(typeof renderSettings!=='function'||typeof api!=='function'||typeof load!=='function')throw Error('Load existing admin first');installed=true;labels[nav]='服务端凭据引用';const priorAllowed=allowedNavs;allowedNavs=function(user=state.user){const list=priorAllowed(user).filter(x=>x!==nav);if(user?.role==='admin')list.push(nav);return list;};renderSettings=settingsPage;const priorLoad=load;load=async function(page){if(page!==nav)return priorLoad(page);++state.seq;state.nav=nav;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));if(state.user?.role!=='admin'){document.getElementById('main').innerHTML='<p>Administrator access required</p>';return;}return refresh();};

// Exact decimal input in the original currency's major unit. No floating-point parsing.
(function(root){
 'use strict';
 const codes='CNY USD EUR GBP HKD TWD JPY KRW CAD AUD NZD SGD CHF THB MYR IDR VND PHP INR AED SAR BRL MXN ZAR BHD KWD OMR JOD TND'.split(' ');
 function exponent(currency){if(!codes.includes(currency))throw Error('Unsupported original currency');return ['JPY','KRW','IDR','VND'].includes(currency)?0:['KWD','BHD','OMR','JOD','TND'].includes(currency)?3:2;}
 function parse(value,currency,nullable=false){
  if(typeof value!=='string')throw Error('Amount must be explicit decimal text');
  const raw=value.trim(),d=exponent(currency);if(!raw&&nullable)return null;
  if(!new RegExp('^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(?:\\.([0-9]{1,'+Math.max(d,1)+'}))?$').test(raw)||(!d&&raw.includes('.')))throw Error('Invalid currency amount');
  const [whole,fraction='']=raw.split('.');if(fraction.length>d)throw Error('Too many decimal places');
  const minor=BigInt(whole)*10n**BigInt(d)+BigInt((fraction+'0'.repeat(d)).slice(0,d)||'0');
  if(minor>1000000000n)throw Error('Amount exceeds supported range');return Number(minor);
 }
 function decimal(minor,currency){const d=exponent(currency);if(!Number.isSafeInteger(minor)||minor<0)throw Error('Invalid minor amount');const raw=String(minor).padStart(d+1,'0');return d?raw.slice(0,-d)+'.'+raw.slice(-d):raw;}
 function format(minor,currency){return minor===null||minor===undefined?'—':currency+' '+decimal(minor,currency);}
 root.AfterSalesFinanceMoney=Object.freeze({exponent,parse,decimal,format,currencies:Object.freeze(codes)});
})(typeof globalThis==='object'?globalThis:this);

// Explicit admin approval and append-only attribution. Never calls refund/fulfill/stock/entitlement APIs.
(function(root){

 async function loadHere(){const r=get();if(!active(r)||r.busy)return;const seq=++r.seq,id=r.caseId;r.loading=true;r.error='';draw();try{const [list,detail,events]=await Promise.all([api('admin/after-sales-finance'),id?api('admin/after-sales-finance/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(id)):Promise.resolve(null),id?api('admin/after-sales-finance/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/events'):Promise.resolve({items:[]})]);if(!active(r,seq)||r.caseId!==id)return;if(r.detail?.financialVersion!==detail?.financialVersion)r.drafts={};r.items=list.items||[];r.detail=detail;r.events=events.items||[];}catch(e){if(active(r,seq)){r.error=e.message;if([401,403].includes(e.status)){r.items=[];r.detail=null;r.events=[];r.retry=null;r.drafts={};}}}finally{if(active(r,seq)){r.loading=false;draw();}}}
 async function mutate(path,payload){const r=get();if(!active(r)||r.busy||r.loading)return;const seq=++r.seq,operation=++r.operation;r.busy=true;r.error='';const sig=JSON.stringify([path,payload]),requestId=payload.requestId||(r.keys[sig]||(r.keys[sig]=crypto.randomUUID())),body={...payload,requestId};r.retry={path,body};draw();try{await api(path,body,'POST');if(!active(r,seq)||r.operation!==operation)return;r.busy=false;r.retry=null;r.drafts={};await loadHere();}catch(e){if(active(r,seq)&&r.operation===operation){r.error=e.message;if([401,403].includes(e.status)){r.items=[];r.detail=null;r.retry=null;r.drafts={};}}}finally{if(runtime===r&&authCurrent(r.epoch)&&r.operation===operation){r.busy=false;if(active(r))draw();}}}
 function install(){
  if(installed)return;if(typeof load!=='function'||typeof api!=='function'||!root.AfterSalesFinanceMoney)throw Error('Load admin/money framework first');installed=true;labels[nav]='售后退款关联';
  const priorAllowed=allowedNavs;allowedNavs=function(user=state.user){const list=priorAllowed(user).filter(x=>x!==nav);if(user?.role==='admin')list.push(nav);return list;};
  const priorLoad=load;load=async function(target){if(target!==nav)return priorLoad(target);state.nav=nav;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));if(state.user?.role!=='admin'){document.getElementById('main').innerHTML='<p>仅管理员可查看退款关联。</p>';return;}return loadHere();};
  document.addEventListener('input',e=>{const form=e.target.closest?.('[data-asf-form]');if(form&&eventCurrent(form)&&!get().busy&&!get().loading)readDraft(form,get());},true);
  document.addEventListener('change',e=>{const form=e.target.closest?.('[data-asf-form]');if(form&&eventCurrent(form)&&!get().busy&&!get().loading)readDraft(form,get());},true);
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest?.('[data-asf-admin]');if(!b)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!eventCurrent(b))return;const r=get();if(r.busy||r.loading)return;if(b.dataset.asfAdmin==='refresh')void loadHere();if(b.dataset.asfAdmin==='open'){++r.seq;r.caseId=b.dataset.id;r.detail=null;r.events=[];r.retry=null;r.drafts={};void loadHere();}if(b.dataset.asfAdmin==='retry'&&r.retry)void mutate(r.retry.path,r.retry.body);},true);
  document.addEventListener('submit',e=>{const form=e.target.closest?.('[data-asf-form]');if(!form)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!eventCurrent(form))return;const r=get(),d=r.detail;if(r.busy||r.loading||!d||d.caseId!==form.dataset.case||Number(form.dataset.version)!==d.financialVersion||!form.reportValidity())return;readDraft(form,r);const f=new FormData(form),kind=form.dataset.asfForm,body={environment:'sandbox',currency:d.currency,expectedVersion:d.financialVersion,reason:String(f.get('reason')||'')};
   try{if(kind==='approve'){body.ruleLabel=String(f.get('ruleLabel')||'');body.items=d.items.map(x=>({caseItemId:x.id,approvedMinor:money().parse(String(f.get('amount:'+x.id)||''),d.currency,true)}));}
    else if(kind==='link'){body.approvalId=d.latestApproval?.id;body.refundEventId=String(f.get('refundEventId')||'');body.items=d.items.filter(x=>String(f.get('amount:'+x.id)||'').trim()).map(x=>({caseItemId:x.id,amountMinor:money().parse(String(f.get('amount:'+x.id)),d.currency)}));if(!body.items.length||body.items.some(x=>x.amountMinor<=0))throw Error('请选择至少一个明确的正金额项目。');}
    else if(kind==='reverse'){body.items=[{linkId:form.dataset.link,amountMinor:money().parse(String(f.get('amount')||''),d.currency)}];if(body.items[0].amountMinor<=0)throw Error('撤销金额必须大于零。');}else return;
   }catch{r.error='请按原币种主单位填写有效金额，不接受指数、千分位、负数或超过币种精度的小数。空白批准为未知；关联与撤销必须为正金额。';draw();return;}
   void mutate('admin/after-sales-finance/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(d.caseId)+'/'+kind,body);
  },true);
 }

labels.reconciliation='沙箱内部对账与本地异常';
let rcRuntime=null;
function rcState(){if(!rcRuntime||rcRuntime.epoch!==state.authEpoch||rcRuntime.userId!==state.user?.id)rcRuntime={epoch:state.authEpoch,userId:state.user?.id||'',data:null,error:'',filters:{},busy:false,sequence:0};return rcRuntime;}
function rcCurrent(r,seq){return rcRuntime===r&&authCurrent(r.epoch)&&state.user?.id===r.userId&&(seq===undefined||seq===r.sequence);}
const rcPriorAllowed=allowedNavs;
allowedNavs=function(user=state.user){const list=rcPriorAllowed(user).filter(k=>k!=='reconciliation'),allowed=user?.role==='admin'||['operator','reviewer'].includes(user?.role)&&state.commissionPermission==='finance';if(allowed&&Object.hasOwn(labels,'reconciliation'))list.push('reconciliation');return list;};
function rcQuery(){const query=new URLSearchParams(rcState().filters).toString();return query?'?'+query:'';}
function rcMoney(value,currency){if(value===null||value===undefined)return '未知 / 未覆盖';if(!Number.isSafeInteger(value))return '金额不可安全计算';try{const digits=new Intl.NumberFormat('zh-CN',{style:'currency',currency}).resolvedOptions().maximumFractionDigits,n=BigInt(value),m=n<0n?-n:n,scale=10n**BigInt(digits);return currency+' '+(n<0n?'-':'')+(m/scale).toLocaleString('zh-CN')+(digits?'.'+String(m%scale).padStart(digits,'0'):'');}catch{return String(value)+' '+currency+'（最小单位）';}}
const rcSeverity={error:'不一致',warning:'需复核', 'not-covered':'未覆盖 / 无法核对'};
const rcCheckLabels={orders_and_cash:'统一订单与付款/退款',item_links_and_entitlements:'订单项与独立权益',membership_links:'会员成交快照与订阅链接',commission_math_and_refunds:'佣金原额与累计冲正',statement_original_lines:'原结算行与追偿关联'};
function rcRender(){const r=rcState(),d=r.data;if(!d||state.nav!=='reconciliation'||!rcCurrent(r))return;const meta=Object.values(d.sources),t=Object.entries(d.totalsByCurrency),f=r.filters;

 '<p class="hint">当前只读一致性快照，手动刷新，不发送外部告警。未实现真实渠道每日对账、回调监控或银行到账核验；不会暗中补账、发权益、续期、改佣金或执行付款。</p><p class="error" role="alert">'+esc(r.error)+'</p>'+
 '<section class="panel"><form data-rc-form data-rc-epoch="'+r.epoch+'" data-rc-user="'+esc(r.userId)+'" class="grid-form"><label>订单ID（可空）<input name="orderId" maxlength="120" value="'+esc(f.orderId||'')+'"></label><label>币种（可空，三位大写）<input name="currency" maxlength="3" value="'+esc(f.currency||'')+'"></label><div class="actions wide"><button class="btn">查看此范围</button><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-rc="clear" data-rc-epoch="'+r.epoch+'" data-rc-user="'+esc(r.userId)+'">清除筛选</button></div></form><p class="hint">筛选作用于已加载的当前记录；不会消除每表上限导致的截断。结算单保留完整历史总额，不当作某件作品小计。</p></section>'+
 '<section class="panel"><h2>当前观察</h2><p>'+esc(new Date(d.observedAt).toISOString())+' · '+(d.partial?'部分来源缺失或截断，不能声称全账一致':'覆盖的内部检查：'+(d.counts.errors?'发现不一致':'未检出不一致'))+'</p><p>不一致 '+d.counts.errors+' · 需复核 '+d.counts.warnings+' · 未覆盖 '+d.counts.notCovered+'</p>'+table(['检查范围','状态'],d.checks.map(c=>[esc(rcCheckLabels[c.code]||c.code),c.status==='covered'?'当前已覆盖':'缺表/截断，未覆盖']))+'</section>'+
 '<section class="panel"><h2>按币种分别汇总</h2><p class="hint">金额均来自实际沙箱原账；已知佣金小计与未知条数分别显示。没有跨币种总额，也不把未知渠道费、到账或真实打款填成0。</p>'+table(['币种','已应用付款 / 退款 / 净额','已登记已知佣金原额 / 净额','佣金未知金额条数','已记账原结算单','渠道费 / 到账 / 真实打款'],t.map(([currency,v])=>[esc(currency),esc(rcMoney(v.paidMinor,currency))+' / '+esc(rcMoney(v.refundedMinor,currency))+' / '+esc(rcMoney(v.netPaidMinor,currency)),esc(rcMoney(v.ledgerOriginalKnownMinor,currency))+' / '+esc(rcMoney(v.ledgerNetKnownMinor,currency)),v.ledgerUnknownAmountCount==null?'未覆盖':String(v.ledgerUnknownAmountCount),esc(rcMoney(v.bookedStatementMinor,currency)),'未知 / 未覆盖']))+'</section>'+
 '<section class="panel"><h2>本地检查结果</h2><p class="hint">这里只定位问题，不自动修复。不一致不等于已经发生真实资金损失；待追偿不等于已追回，已记账不等于已打款。</p>'+table(['类别 / 定位','订单 / 项','币种','观察值 / 期望值 / 差额','解释'],d.findings.map(x=>[esc(rcSeverity[x.severity]||x.severity)+'<br>'+esc(x.code)+'<br>'+esc(x.sourceId||''),esc(x.orderId||'—')+'<br>'+esc(x.itemId||'—'),esc(x.currency||'—'),esc(x.observed??'未知')+' / '+esc(x.expected??'未知')+' / '+esc(x.deltaMinor??'未知'),esc(x.detail||'请结合原订单、事件及成交快照复核；本页未修改原账。')]))+'</section>'+
 '<details class="panel"><summary>来源覆盖与明确缺口</summary>'+table(['来源','实际行数 / 已加载','完整性'],meta.map(m=>[esc(m.table),String(m.totalRows)+' / '+String(m.loadedRows),!m.exists?'缺表，未覆盖':m.truncated?'已截断，不能核对全部关联':'本次读快照已完整加载']))+'<p>真实渠道日账、手续费与到账、支付失败事件、回调积压、真实退款失败、拒付、外部告警投递及历史as-of重建均未覆盖。</p><p class="hint">数据读取在同一只读快照中完成。未知来源、缺历史快照和缺费用会单列，不能视作差异为0或生产验收通过。</p></details>';
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-rc-form] button,[data-rc]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=r.busy);
}
const rcLoadBefore=load;
load=async function(nav){if(nav!=='reconciliation')return rcLoadBefore(nav);if(!state.user||!document.getElementById('main'))return false;const r=rcState(),epoch=state.authEpoch,seq=++state.seq,request=++r.sequence;state.nav=nav;r.data=null;r.error='';r.busy=true;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('沙箱内部对账与本地异常')+'<p class="hint">正在读取只读快照，不执行补账…</p>';try{const d=await api('admin/reconciliation'+rcQuery());if(!rcCurrent(r,request)||state.seq!==seq||state.nav!==nav)return false;if(d.readOnly!==true||d.productionEnabled!==false||d.externalAlertsEnabled!==false)throw Error('报告边界响应不符合只读沙箱契约。');r.data=d;r.busy=false;rcRender();return true;}catch(e){if(rcCurrent(r,request)&&state.seq===seq&&state.nav===nav&&!e.handled){r.data=null;r.error=e.message;document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('沙箱内部对账与本地异常')+'<p class="error">'+esc(e.message)+'</p><button class="btn secondary" data-nav="reconciliation">重新读取当前权限和报告</button>';}return false;}finally{if(rcCurrent(r,request))r.busy=false;}};
document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{const form=event.target;if(!form.hasAttribute('data-rc-form'))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const r=rcState();if(r.busy||Number(form.dataset.rcEpoch)!==r.epoch||form.dataset.rcUser!==r.userId||state.nav!=='reconciliation')return;const v=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));r.filters=Object.fromEntries(['orderId','currency'].filter(k=>String(v[k]||'').trim()).map(k=>[k,k==='currency'?String(v[k]).trim().toUpperCase():String(v[k]).trim()]));load('reconciliation');},true);
document.addEventListener('click',async event=>{const b=event.target.closest('[data-rc]');if(!b)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();const r=rcState();if(r.busy||Number(b.dataset.rcEpoch)!==r.epoch||b.dataset.rcUser!==r.userId||state.nav!=='reconciliation')return;if(b.dataset.rc==='clear'){r.filters={};load('reconciliation');return;}if(b.dataset.rc!=='export')return;const seq=++r.sequence,epoch=state.authEpoch;try{const d=await api('admin/reconciliation/export'+rcQuery());if(!rcCurrent(r,seq)||state.nav!=='reconciliation'||!authCurrent(epoch))return;if(d.readOnly!==true||typeof d.csv!=='string')throw Error('未取得有效只读导出。');const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(['\uFEFF'+d.csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='sandbox-read-only-findings.csv';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}catch(e){if(rcCurrent(r,seq)&&state.nav==='reconciliation'&&!e.handled){r.error=e.message;rcRender();}}},true);

// Explicit administrator policy grant. Navigation is not server authorization.
labels.retention='到期资料清理';
let rtaRuntime=null;
function rtaState(){if(!rtaRuntime||rtaRuntime.epoch!==state.authEpoch||rtaRuntime.user!==state.user?.id)rtaRuntime={epoch:state.authEpoch,user:state.user?.id||'',data:null,busy:false,nonce:0,error:'',preview:null,receipt:null,owner:''};return rtaRuntime;}
function rtaCurrent(r,n){return rtaRuntime===r&&authCurrent(r.epoch)&&state.user?.id===r.user&&state.nav==='retention'&&(n===undefined||r.nonce===n);}
const rtaAllowedBefore=allowedNavs;allowedNavs=function(user=state.user){const list=rtaAllowedBefore(user).filter(x=>x!=='retention');if(user?.role==='admin')list.push('retention');return list;};
function rtaAttrs(r){return `data-rta-epoch="${r.epoch}" data-rta-user="${esc(r.user)}"`;}
function rtaRender(){const r=rtaState();if(!rtaCurrent(r)||!r.data)return;const d=r.data,p=r.preview,attrs=rtaAttrs(r),fields=['timer','activity','measurements','cards','memory'];document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('到期资料清理')+'<p class="hint">默认关闭、先预览再明确执行。服务器按当前管理员角色重新鉴权；未配置期限或历史年龄未知均跳过。不会删除金融/履约/额度账，不证明第三方或备份已清，未部署自动定时任务。</p><p class="error" role="alert">'+esc(r.error)+'</p>'+`<section class="panel"><form data-rta-form="config" ${attrs} class="grid-form"><label><input type="checkbox" name="enabled" ${d.config.enabled?'checked':''}>授权执行当前政策到期清理</label><label>政策版本<input name="policyVersion" value="${esc(d.policyVersion)}" readonly></label>${fields.map(k=>`<label>${({timer:'计时记录',activity:'活动记录',measurements:'设备测量',cards:'抽卡记录',memory:'长期记忆'})[k]}期限（天；空白=未确认）<input name="${k}" type="number" min="0" max="36500" step="1" value="${d.config.retentionDays?.[k]??''}"></label>`).join('')}<p class="hint wide">个人记录/会话期限在原隐私政策设置维护；不套用其他范围期限。当前记录 ${d.retention.recordsDays??'未知'} 天；会话 ${d.retention.conversationsDays??'未知'} 天。0天表示当前时间之前即到期，仍按预览与版本重核，不恢复已删资料。</p><label class="wide"><input name="confirmed" type="checkbox" required>明确授权当前政策与这些独立期限</label><button class="btn">保存执行授权</button></form></section><section class="panel"><form data-rta-form="preview" ${attrs} class="grid-form"><label>明确账号ID<input name="owner" value="${esc(r.owner)}" maxlength="120" required></label><label class="wide"><input type="checkbox" name="confirmed" required>仅预览此账号七类资料，不删除</label><button class="btn secondary">预览此账号</button></form></section>`+(p?`<section class="panel"><h2>当前账号 ${esc(p.accountId)} 的预览</h2><p>观察时间 ${esc(new Date(p.asOf).toISOString())} · 计划 ${p.summary.planned} · 待核 ${p.pending}${p.summary.truncated?' · 有截断，需后续新预览':''}</p>`+table(['范围','期限','到期','未知年龄','说明'],Object.entries(p.summary.scopes).map(([k,x])=>[esc(k),x.retentionDays===null?'未确认/跳过':String(x.retentionDays),String(x.eligible),String(x.ageUnknown??'未覆盖'),esc(x.skipReason||'按实际跟踪时间；执行前重核')]))+`<form data-rta-form="apply" ${attrs}><label><input name="confirmed" type="checkbox" required>本人以当前管理员身份明确授权，仅执行此账号本政策预览下一批，不替代供应商/备份清除</label><button class="btn" ${!d.config.enabled||!p.pending?'disabled':''}>实际执行下一批（最多100条）</button></form></section>`:'');if(r.receipt){const x=r.receipt.details;document.getElementById('main').innerHTML+='<section class="panel"><h2>本批本地清理回执</h2>'+table(['范围','实际删除条数'],Object.entries(x.counts||{}).map(([k,v])=>[esc(k),String(v)]))+'<p>变更或缺失跳过 '+String(x.changedOrMissingSkipped||0)+' · 剩余 '+String(x.pending||0)+'；供应商与备份仍待确认，本机副本未清。回执不含正文。</p></section>';}document.querySelectorAll('[data-rta-form] button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=b.disabled||r.busy);}
async function rtaWrite(path,body,method='POST'){const r=rtaState();if(!rtaCurrent(r)||r.busy)return false;const n=++r.nonce;r.busy=true;r.error='';rtaRender();try{const d=await api('admin/privacy/retention/'+path,body,method);if(!rtaCurrent(r,n))return false;if(path==='config')r.data=d;else if(path==='preview')r.preview=d.item;else{r.receipt=d.item;if(r.preview&&d.item.details.pending>0){r.preview.pending=d.item.details.pending;r.preview.version=d.item.details.nextVersion;}else r.preview=null;toast('本地批次已处理；外部及备份仍待确认。');}return true;}catch(e){if(rtaCurrent(r,n)&&!e.handled)r.error=e.message;return false;}finally{if(rtaCurrent(r,n)){r.busy=false;rtaRender();}}}
const rtaLoadBefore=load;load=async function(nav){if(nav!=='retention')return rtaLoadBefore(nav);if(state.user?.role!=='admin')return false;state.nav=nav;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));const r=rtaState(),n=++r.nonce;r.busy=true;try{const d=await api('admin/privacy/retention/config');if(!rtaCurrent(r,n))return false;r.data=d;r.error='';r.busy=false;rtaRender();return true;}catch(e){if(rtaCurrent(r,n)&&!e.handled){r.error=e.message;document.getElementById('main').innerHTML=heading('到期资料清理')+'<p class="error">'+esc(e.message)+'</p><button data-nav="retention" class="btn secondary">重试</button>';}return false;}finally{if(rtaCurrent(r,n))r.busy=false;}};
document.addEventListener('submit',e=>{const f=e.target;if(!f.hasAttribute('data-rta-form'))return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();const r=rtaState();if(!rtaCurrent(r)||r.busy||f.dataset.rtaUser!==r.user||Number(f.dataset.rtaEpoch)!==r.epoch)return;const v=new FormData(f);if(!v.has('confirmed'))return;const kind=f.dataset.rtaForm;if(kind==='config'){const retentionDays=Object.fromEntries(['timer','activity','measurements','cards','memory'].map(k=>[k,String(v.get(k)).trim()===''?null:Number(v.get(k))]));return rtaWrite('config',{enabled:v.has('enabled'),confirmed:true,policyVersion:v.get('policyVersion'),revision:r.data.config.revision,retentionDays},'PATCH');}if(kind==='preview'){r.owner=String(v.get('owner')).trim();return rtaWrite('preview',{owner:r.owner,scopes:['records','conversations','timer','activity','measurements','cards','memory'],requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});}if(kind==='apply'&&r.preview)return rtaWrite('jobs/'+encodeURIComponent(r.preview.id)+'/apply',{owner:r.preview.accountId,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),version:r.preview.version,confirmed:true,policyVersion:r.preview.policyVersion});},true);

labels.baziSamples='八字样本核对';
const bzsAdminBase='admin/bazi-samples';
let bzsAdminRuntime=null;
function bzsAdminState(){
 const owner=state.user?.id,epoch=state.authEpoch;
 if(!bzsAdminRuntime||bzsAdminRuntime.owner!==owner||bzsAdminRuntime.epoch!==epoch)bzsAdminRuntime={owner,epoch,versions:[],versionNext:null,version:null,cases:[],caseNext:null,selected:new Set(),run:null,runCases:[],runNext:null,detail:null,fields:[],fieldNext:null,raw:null,period:null,busy:false,message:'',keys:new Map(),processIntent:null,reviewDrafts:new Map(),reviewOpen:null};
 return bzsAdminRuntime;
}
function bzsAdminCurrent(s){return s===bzsAdminRuntime&&s.viewSeq===state.seq&&s.owner===state.user?.id&&s.epoch===state.authEpoch&&state.user?.role==='admin'&&state.nav==='baziSamples';}
function bzsAdminKey(s,intent){if(!s.keys.has(intent))s.keys.set(intent,crypto.randomUUID());return s.keys.get(intent);}
function bzsAdminQuery(path,cursor){return bzsAdminBase+path+(cursor?'?before='+encodeURIComponent(cursor):'');}
function bzsAdminCasePath(s){return '/runs/'+encodeURIComponent(s.run.id)+'/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(s.detail.item.caseId);}
function bzsAdminJson(value){return '<pre class="bzs-json" tabindex="0">'+esc(JSON.stringify(value,null,2))+'</pre>';}
function bzsAdminCounts(c={}){return '<dl class="bzs-counts">'+Object.entries(c).map(([k,v])=>'<div><dt>'+esc(k)+'</dt><dd>'+esc(v)+'</dd></div>').join('')+'</dl>';}
function bzsAdminButton(action,label,extra=''){return '<button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-bzs-admin="'+action+'" '+extra+'>'+label+'</button>';}
function bzsAdminDownload(value,name){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(value,null,2)+'\n'],{type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}

 return '<form id="bzs-review-form" data-index="'+index+'"><h3>分类建议：'+esc(f.path)+'</h3><p class="hint">只追加建议，不更改原结果，也不代表老师确认。失败后保留本次草稿；换号时清除。</p><label>分类<select name="category">'+['undetermined','input','rule','algorithm','display'].map(x=>'<option '+(v.category===x?'selected':'')+'>'+x+'</option>').join('')+'</select></label><label>证据路径<textarea name="paths" required>'+esc(v.paths)+'</textarea></label><label>参考说明<input name="reference" maxlength="1000" value="'+esc(v.reference)+'"></label><label>说明<textarea name="note" maxlength="2000" required>'+esc(v.note)+'</textarea></label><label>关联复测运行编号（可选）<input name="retestRunId" value="'+esc(v.retestRunId)+'"></label><button class="btn">提交分类建议</button></form>';
}
function bzsAdminRender(s){
 if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;
 const main=document.getElementById('main'),v=s.version?.item,r=s.run,d=s.detail;
 main.innerHTML='<section class="bzs-admin">'+heading('八字样本核对',bzsAdminButton('refresh','刷新样本版本'))+
 '<p class="bzs-boundary">本地算法回放与逐字段对比。老师确认样本：0；专业算法验收率：未评定。导入文件中的审核声明只作为未核实来源保存。服务端 DOM 状态始终为未运行，原生页面观察另记验证证据。</p>'+
 '<p class="error" role="alert" id="bzs-admin-message">'+esc(s.message)+'</p><p role="status">'+(s.busy?'正在处理，请等待当前请求结果。':'')+'</p>'+
 '<article class="panel"><h2>导入原始 JSON 文件</h2><p class="hint">UTF-8，最多 2 MiB / 100 个样本。服务端核对原始字节和结构；同版本内容冲突会拒绝。原始文件不保存，导出为规范 JSON。</p><label>选择文件<input id="bzs-import-file" type="file" accept=".json,application/json"></label>'+bzsAdminButton('import','导入文件')+'</article>'+
 '<article class="panel"><h2>样本版本</h2>'+table(['名称 / 版本','来源字节 SHA-256','样本数','操作'],s.versions.map(x=>[esc(x.title)+'<br>'+esc(x.setKey)+' / '+esc(x.version),'<code>'+esc(x.sourceBytesSha256)+'</code><br>'+esc(x.sourceByteLength)+' 字节',esc(x.caseCount),bzsAdminButton('version','打开','data-id="'+esc(x.id)+'"')]))+(s.versionNext?bzsAdminButton('versions-more','更多版本'):'')+'</article>'+
 (v?'<article class="panel"><h2>'+esc(v.title)+' · '+esc(v.version)+'</h2><p>规范内容 SHA-256：<code>'+esc(v.canonicalSha256)+'</code></p><p>首次来源字节 SHA-256：<code>'+esc(v.sourceBytesSha256)+'</code></p><details><summary>来源与完整样本顺序</summary>'+bzsAdminJson(s.version.header)+'</details><div class="actions">'+bzsAdminButton('select-all','选择全部 '+s.version.header.caseIds.length+' 个')+bzsAdminButton('select-none','清空选择')+'</div><p>已选 '+s.selected.size+' 个；全部选择包含未翻页的样本。</p>'+table(['选取','样本','标签','内容 SHA-256'],s.cases.map(x=>['<input aria-label="选择 '+esc(x.caseId)+'" type="checkbox" data-bzs-case="'+esc(x.caseId)+'" '+(s.selected.has(x.caseId)?'checked':'')+'>',esc(x.label)+'<br><code>'+esc(x.caseId)+'</code>',esc(x.tags.join(' / ')),'<code>'+esc(x.canonicalSha256)+'</code>']))+(s.caseNext?bzsAdminButton('cases-more','更多样本'):'')+'<p class="hint">新建运行会冻结样本顺序和当前实现；已有运行不会随源码变化重新计算。</p>'+bzsAdminButton('create-run','为所选样本新建运行')+'</article>':'')+
 '<article class="panel"><h2>继续已有运行</h2><label>运行编号<input id="bzs-run-id" value="'+esc(r?.id||'')+'" autocomplete="off"></label>'+bzsAdminButton('load-run','读取运行')+'</article>'+
 (r?'<article class="panel"><h2>运行 '+esc(r.id)+'</h2><p>状态：'+esc(r.state)+'；已处理（含失败） '+r.processed+' / '+r.total+'；已计算 '+r.succeeded+'；失败 '+r.failed+'；阻断 '+r.blocked+'</p><p>冻结清单 SHA-256：<code>'+esc(r.manifestHash)+'</code></p>'+bzsAdminCounts(r.counts)+'<details><summary>实现、运行环境与冻结清单</summary>'+bzsAdminJson(r.manifest)+'</details><div class="actions">'+bzsAdminButton('process',s.processIntent?'重试当前处理请求':'处理下一个样本',r.state==='completed'||r.state==='erased'?'disabled':'')+bzsAdminButton('run-refresh','刷新运行')+bzsAdminButton('run-export','导出运行摘要')+'</div><p class="hint">每次只处理一个。失败结果保留；需要复测时新建运行。</p>'+table(['样本','状态','错误','操作'],s.runCases.map(x=>[esc(x.caseId),esc(x.state),esc(x.errorCode||''),bzsAdminButton('detail','查看','data-id="'+esc(x.caseId)+'"')]))+(s.runNext?bzsAdminButton('run-cases-more','更多结果'):'')+'</article>':'')+
 (d?'<article class="panel"><h2>样本 '+esc(d.item.caseId)+'</h2><p>状态：'+esc(d.item.state)+' · '+esc(d.item.errorCode||'')+'；服务端 DOM：'+esc(d.item.domStatus)+'</p>'+bzsAdminCounts(d.item.counts)+'<div class="actions"><a class="btn secondary" href="/experience?baziSampleRun='+encodeURIComponent(r.id)+'&amp;baziSampleCase='+encodeURIComponent(d.item.caseId)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">打开 APP 临时核对</a>'+bzsAdminButton('case-export','导出规范结果（默认去名）')+bzsAdminButton('raw','读取原始计算索引')+'</div><label class="inline-check"><input id="bzs-export-names" type="checkbox">完整导出时包含出生资料称呼（私有内容）</label><p class="hint">默认精确去除称呼字段，指纹仍对应原冻结报告；自由文本不视作已匿名。</p><details><summary>原始样本输入与预期</summary>'+bzsAdminJson(d.sampleCase)+'</details><details><summary>标准化、对照与批注阶段</summary>'+bzsAdminJson(d.stages)+'</details>'+table(['字段','预期状态 / 值','实际状态 / 值','对比 / 原因','分类'],s.fields.map((x,i)=>['<code>'+esc(x.path)+'</code>',esc(x.expectedState)+bzsAdminJson(x.expected),esc(x.actualState)+bzsAdminJson(x.actual),esc(x.comparison)+'<br>'+esc(x.reasonCode),esc(x.category)+(x.id?'<br>'+bzsAdminButton('review','提交分类建议','data-index="'+i+'"'):'<br>未提供预期，不判通过')]))+(s.fieldNext?bzsAdminButton('fields-more','更多字段'):'')+
 (s.raw?'<h3>完整计算数据索引</h3>'+bzsAdminJson(s.raw.header)+bzsAdminJson(s.raw.index)+'<div class="actions">'+(s.raw.index?.periods||[]).map(p=>bzsAdminButton('period','读取大运段 '+p.index,'data-index="'+p.index+'"')).join('')+'</div>':'')+(s.period?bzsAdminJson(s.period):'')+'<details><summary>分类建议历史（仅建议，未代表专业审核）</summary>'+table(['编号 / 时间','分类 / 状态','说明','证据 / 复测'],(d.reviews||[]).map(x=>[esc(x.id)+'<br>'+esc(new Date(x.created).toLocaleString('zh-CN')),esc(x.category)+' / '+esc(x.classificationState),esc(x.note),bzsAdminJson(x.evidence)+'<br>'+esc(x.retestRunId||'')]))+(d.reviewsNext?bzsAdminButton('reviews-more','更多分类建议'):'')+'</details><div id="bzs-review-editor">'+bzsAdminReviewEditor(s)+'</div></article>':'')+'</section>';
 main.querySelectorAll('[data-bzs-admin], [data-bzs-case], #bzs-import-file, #bzs-run-id, #bzs-review-form input, #bzs-review-form select, #bzs-review-form textarea, #bzs-review-form button').forEach(x=>{if(s.busy)x.disabled=true;});
}
async function bzsAdminReadRun(s,id){const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+'/runs/'+encodeURIComponent(id));if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;s.run=d.run;const c=await api(bzsAdminBase+'/runs/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/cases');if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;s.runCases=c.items;s.runNext=c.next;}
async function bzsAdminWork(s,task){if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s)||s.busy)return;s.busy=true;s.message='';bzsAdminRender(s);try{await task();}catch(e){if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)&&e.status===403){bzsAdminRuntime=null;reconcileAuth();}else if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)&&e.code!=='AUTH_CHANGED')s.message=e.message;}finally{if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.busy=false;bzsAdminRender(s);}}}
async function bzsAdminImport(s,file){
 if(!file)throw Error('请先选择 JSON 文件。');if(file.size>2097152||!file.size)throw Error('文件必须大于 0 字节且不超过 2 MiB。');
 const bytes=await file.arrayBuffer(),hash=Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes)),x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;
 const requestId=bzsAdminKey(s,'import:'+hash),response=await fetch('/api/'+bzsAdminBase+'/import',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-EW-Request-ID':requestId},body:bytes});
 const data=await response.json();if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;if(response.status===401){requireLogin();return;}if(!response.ok)throw Object.assign(Error(data.error||'导入失败'),{status:response.status});
 s.message=data.duplicate?'已回查原有版本，来源字节信息保留首次导入记录。':'导入成功，尚未计算或完成专业审核。';const list=await api(bzsAdminBase);if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;s.versions=list.items;s.versionNext=list.next;
}
const bzsAdminOriginalAllowed=allowedNavs;
allowedNavs=function(user=state.user){return bzsAdminOriginalAllowed(user).filter(k=>k!=='baziSamples'||user?.role==='admin');};
const bzsAdminOriginalInvalidate=invalidateAuth;
invalidateAuth=function(...args){bzsAdminRuntime=null;document.querySelector('.bzs-admin')?.remove();return bzsAdminOriginalInvalidate(...args);};
const bzsAdminOriginalLoad=load;
load=async function(nav){if(nav!=='baziSamples')return bzsAdminOriginalLoad(nav);if(state.user?.role!=='admin')return false;++state.seq;state.nav=nav;document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-current',b.dataset.nav===nav?'page':'false'));bzsAdminRuntime={...bzsAdminState(),viewSeq:state.seq,busy:false};const s=bzsAdminRuntime;bzsAdminRender(s);await bzsAdminWork(s,async()=>{const d=await api(bzsAdminBase);if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.versions=d.items;s.versionNext=d.next;}});};
document.addEventListener('change',event=>{const node=event.target.closest('[data-bzs-case]');if(!node)return;const s=bzsAdminState();if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s)||s.busy)return;node.checked?s.selected.add(node.dataset.bzsCase):s.selected.delete(node.dataset.bzsCase);bzsAdminRender(s);});
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
 const button=event.target.closest('[data-bzs-admin]');if(!button)return;event.preventDefault();const s=bzsAdminState();if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s)||s.busy)return;
 const action=button.dataset.bzsAdmin,file=document.getElementById('bzs-import-file')?.files[0],runId=document.getElementById('bzs-run-id')?.value.trim(),includeName=document.getElementById('bzs-export-names')?.checked===true;
 if(action==='review'){const f=s.fields[Number(button.dataset.index)];if(f?.id){s.reviewOpen=f.id;bzsAdminRender(s);document.getElementById('bzs-review-editor')?.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});}return;}
 if(action==='select-all'||action==='select-none'){s.selected=new Set(action==='select-all'?s.version.header.caseIds:[]);bzsAdminRender(s);return;}
 bzsAdminWork(s,async()=>{
  if(action==='import')return bzsAdminImport(s,file);
  if(action==='refresh'||action==='versions-more'){const d=await api(bzsAdminQuery('',action==='versions-more'?s.versionNext:null));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.versions=action==='refresh'?d.items:s.versions.concat(d.items);s.versionNext=d.next;}return;}
  if(action==='version'||action==='cases-more'){const more=action==='cases-more',id=more?s.version.item.id:button.dataset.id,d=await api(bzsAdminQuery('/'+encodeURIComponent(id),more?s.caseNext:null));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.version=d;s.cases=more?s.cases.concat(d.cases):d.cases;s.caseNext=d.next;if(!more)s.selected=new Set();}return;}
  if(action==='create-run'){const ids=s.version.header.caseIds.filter(id=>s.selected.has(id));if(!ids.length)throw Error('请先选择样本。');const intent={setVersionId:s.version.item.id,caseIds:ids},intentKey='run:'+JSON.stringify(intent),d=await api(bzsAdminBase+'/runs',{requestId:bzsAdminKey(s,intentKey),...intent});if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.keys.delete(intentKey);s.detail=null;s.fields=[];s.processIntent=null;await bzsAdminReadRun(s,d.run.id);}return;}
  if(action==='load-run'||action==='run-refresh'){if(!runId)throw Error('请输入运行编号。');if(s.run?.id!==runId){s.processIntent=null;s.detail=null;s.fields=[];s.raw=null;s.period=null;}await bzsAdminReadRun(s,runId);return;}
  const path='/runs/'+encodeURIComponent(s.run.id);
  if(action==='process'){if(!s.processIntent)s.processIntent={requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),version:s.run.version,limit:1};let d;try{d=await api(bzsAdminBase+path+'/process',s.processIntent);}catch(error){if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)&&error.status===409&&['run_version_changed','run_busy'].includes(error.message)){s.processIntent=null;await bzsAdminReadRun(s,s.run.id);}throw error;}if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.processIntent=null;s.run=d.run;await bzsAdminReadRun(s,d.run.id);}return;}
  if(action==='run-cases-more'){const d=await api(bzsAdminQuery(path+'/cases',s.runNext));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.runCases.push(...d.items);s.runNext=d.next;}return;}
  if(action==='run-export'){const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+path+'/export');if(bzsAdminCurrent(s))bzsAdminDownload(d,'bazi-run-'+s.run.id+'.json');return;}
  if(action==='detail'){const p=path+'/cases/'+encodeURIComponent(button.dataset.id),d=await api(bzsAdminBase+p);if(!bzsAdminCurrent(s))return;s.detail=d;s.raw=null;s.period=null;s.fields=[];s.fieldNext=null;if(d.item.id){const f=await api(bzsAdminBase+p+'/fields');if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.fields=f.items;s.fieldNext=f.next;}}return;}
  const casePath=bzsAdminCasePath(s);
  if(action==='reviews-more'){const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+casePath+'?reviewsBefore='+encodeURIComponent(s.detail.reviewsNext));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.detail.reviews.push(...d.reviews);s.detail.reviewsNext=d.reviewsNext;}return;}
  if(action==='fields-more'){const d=await api(bzsAdminQuery(casePath+'/fields',s.fieldNext));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.fields.push(...d.items);s.fieldNext=d.next;}return;}
  if(action==='raw'){const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+casePath+'/raw');if(bzsAdminCurrent(s))s.raw=d;return;}
  if(action==='period'){const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+casePath+'/raw/periods/'+button.dataset.index);if(bzsAdminCurrent(s))s.period=d;return;}
  if(action==='case-export'){const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+casePath+'/export'+(includeName?'?includeName=1':''));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s))bzsAdminDownload(d,'bazi-case-'+s.detail.item.caseId+'.json');}
 });
});
document.addEventListener('input',event=>{const form=event.target.closest('#bzs-review-form');if(!form)return;const s=bzsAdminState(),f=s.fields[Number(form.dataset.index)];if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)&&f?.id&&!s.busy)s.reviewDrafts.set(f.id,Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)));});
document.addEventListener('submit',event=>{const form=event.target;if(form.id!=='bzs-review-form')return;event.preventDefault();const s=bzsAdminState(),values=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)),finding=s.fields[Number(form.dataset.index)];if(!finding?.id||!bzsAdminCurrent(s)||s.busy)return;s.reviewDrafts.set(finding.id,values);const data={category:values.category,evidence:{paths:values.paths.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean),fingerprints:s.detail.item.fingerprints,reference:values.reference||null},note:values.note,retestRunId:values.retestRunId||null};bzsAdminWork(s,async()=>{const d=await api(bzsAdminBase+'/findings/'+encodeURIComponent(finding.id)+'/reviews',{requestId:bzsAdminKey(s,'review:'+finding.id+':'+JSON.stringify(data)),...data});if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.reviewDrafts.delete(finding.id);s.reviewOpen=null;s.message='分类建议已追加：'+d.item.id+'（proposed，未代表专业审核）';try{const updated=await api(bzsAdminBase+bzsAdminCasePath(s));if(bzsAdminCurrent(s)){s.detail.reviews=updated.reviews;s.detail.reviewsNext=updated.reviewsNext;}}catch(error){if(error.status===403)throw error;if(bzsAdminCurrent(s))s.message+='。历史回查暂未完成：'+error.message;}}});});

(async()=>{if(!['http:','https:'].includes(location.protocol)){el.innerHTML='<main class="auth"><h1>请通过本地服务打开后台</h1><p>先运行资料包中的“启动本地计时后台.py”，再访问后台。管理数据保存在当前项目内。</p><p><a href="http://127.0.0.1:8765/admin">打开本地管理后台</a></p></main>';return;}await authBootstrap();})();
</script></body></html>
